- Decision: the token address check (P9-C4) also applies to `--agent-advertise`, which no schema pattern covers, and to an input port above 65535, which the F-C5 pattern `[0-9]{1,5}` admits; it discharges register row 41's issuing-side clause, the rest of that row being P8's.
- Decision: `core.host.enroll-local` also calls `Sessions.Disconnect` after its commit: it revokes the local host's serials, and spec section 2 says revocation closes the live session.
- Decision: host names are unique (`name-free` guard and the transactional re-check in `create`); spec section 3 gives only the name pattern, and `core.host.list` and the typed confirmations identify hosts by name.
- Decision: revoking a pending or already revoked host is allowed (spec section 3 states no rule); deleting keeps the host's audit rows and the dispatcher's index rows.
- Decision: a host without a `core_host_update` row follows `stable` with no schedule, the reading P11's scheduler must share.
- Decision: the window and the schema-hash rule gate Read, Resource (window only: resources carry no hash), Plan and Apply; Change, Changes, Confirm, Revert and ingestion are not gated. Spec section 5 says "every other action answers host.version_unsupported" without naming the change routes; leaving them open keeps a pending change confirmable and revertible.
- Decision: for an older released schema, only fields the agent's schema lacks are refused at the controller; any other mismatch dispatches and the agent's validation reports it field by field. "First field" means first in JSON-pointer order. An agent action version absent from the agent's `schemaHashes` is "unknown" like an unknown hash. The unavailable answer uses `module.unavailable`, the existing code for "not usable on this host"; the spec names only the reason text.
- Decision: the ingestion deadline runs on the configured `core.Clock` (`AfterFunc` cancelling the context), so `TestIngestDeadline` advances the fake clock instead of waiting 30 s (Global Constraints).
- `core.Audit.Ingest` has a fixed signature (`internal/core/contract.go:362`, asserted at `internal/controller/audit/audit.go:43`), so the epoch-aware insert is a new method `IngestEpoch` and `Ingest` stores the empty epoch (dry run: changing `Ingest` failed with `*Store does not implement core.Audit (wrong type for method Ingest)`).
- `TestCoreInputFieldsAreDocumented` (`internal/controller/coreactions/registry_test.go`) is extended in Task 14, not weakened: the reflector writes the description of the nullable `UpdatePolicy.schedule` (`internal/core/fleet.go:176`) on the `$ref` branch of its `oneOf`, and the CLI takes such a nested field from `--input`, never a flag (`internal/cli/input.go:88-91`). Because `schedule` has no `omitempty` it is required in the input, so `darkpanel host update-policy` needs `--input` (or `"schedule": null`); changing that is an F-C5 change outside P9.
- Task order: revoke and delete (12) precede create (13) because the registry refuses a compensable action whose compensating action is not registered (`internal/core/registry.go:92`).
- Task 10 edits the fakes of 7's `TestCoreModuleSkipsEnablement` and 9's `agentAt` to report the schema hash of their `core` action, as a real agent does; without that they fail with `agent schema unknown` once negotiation exists (dry run 2026-10-20).
- Ownership: the part touches only its owned paths plus the snapshot directories it regenerates (`schemas/core.host.*/`); `schemas/core.module.*` did not change, because `core.ModuleState` reflects to the same schema as the deleted copy (schemagen after Task 6 rewrote only `schemas/core.host.list/v1.json`). `internal/controller/audit/epoch_test.go` imports `dispatch` only to assert `*dispatch.Hosts` implements `audit.Epochs`.
- Codex is not used this session; no task carries an `**Executor:**` line.

### P10 agent daemon

- Every task was dry-run on 2026-10-20 in `/root/.cache/plan5-dry/wt-p10` on branch `dryrun/plan5-p10` from `dryrun/plan5-base` (`39cb3d1`): commits `a904d01`, `e7aa613`, `0d68550`, `dff0921`, `840fdfc`, `9643632`, `cd4764e`, `730f386`, one per task in order. At every commit `go build ./...`, `go vet ./...`, `golangci-lint run --allow-serial-runners ./...` (`0 issues.`) and `go test -race -count=1` of the task's packages passed; on the last commit `go test -count=1 ./...` (the whole repository, `internal/controller/server` end-to-end and `internal/modules` snapshot tests included) and `go mod tidy -diff` passed. The code blocks below are those commits' files and patches, unedited. `go test -count=15 -race` of `internal/agent/daemon`, `internal/agent/socket` and `internal/agent/runtime` passed on the last commit.
- Names consumed, read 2026-10-20 on `39cb3d1`: `transport.AgentConfig`, `EnrollConfig`, `Dial` (`internal/transport/client.go:15,38,85`); `NewBackoff`, `Backoff.Next`, `Reset` (`backoff.go:23,29,42`); `ControllerSession`, `AgentSession`, `Session.Open`, `Done`, `Ping` (`session.go:37,47,74,125,129`); `Heartbeat` and its zero-value defaults (`heartbeat.go:25,48`); `Handshake` (`server.go:39`); `ParseToken`, `Token.String` (`token.go:54,35`); `update.RecoverStart`, `Updater.Recover`, `update.New` (`internal/update/recover.go:19,37`, `updater.go:115`); `api.Config`, `OnHello`, `NewHandler` (`internal/agent/api/handler.go:54,59,78`); `api.SocketConfig.Identity`, `NewSocketHandler` (`socket.go:21,31`); `install.AgentLockName` (`internal/install/layout.go:50`); `install.CheckResult`, `install.Agent` (`install.go:46,56`); uninstall's lock check (`uninstall.go:369-387`, message `an agent still runs` at 382); `fleet.LoadOrCreateAuthority` (`internal/controller/fleet/ca.go:71`), `Authority.Renew`, `Handshake`, `RevokeHost` (`certs.go:100,143,162`), `Authority.Enroll` (`tokens.go:85`); agent `core_meta` (`internal/core/store/migrations/agent/0001_core.sql:4-7`) and `core_locks` (`0002_lifecycle.sql:46-49`).
- The M1 runtime excluded the core in `modules()` (`internal/agent/runtime/runtime.go:162-172` on `39cb3d1`) and answered `Preflight("core")` with `not_found` (`client.go:34`). Its engine already ran a core agent-side action: in the dry run `TestCoreModuleIsAlwaysLoaded`'s `Read(core.probe.get)` passed before Task 8, and only the Hello and Preflight assertions failed. So "always loaded" (spec section 6) is implemented as listing the core in Hello as available and answering its Preflight.
- Decision: no `internal/core/store/migrations/agent/0006_journal_epoch.sql`. The spec's contracts list names it, but SQL cannot create a ULID, and creating the row in `runtime.Open` gives the same lifetime: created with agent.db's first runtime start (the installer migrates agent.db but never opens the runtime, and no reader needs the epoch earlier), kept across restarts and re-enrollments, and replaced when agent.db is recreated. "Replaced when its journal table is recreated" becomes a rule for any future migration that rebuilds `core_journal` (P10-C1), recorded in the code comment; no such migration exists.
- Decision: before enrollment the runtime serves as host id `unenrolled` (`UnenrolledHostID`), because `runtime.Config.HostID` is required and plans, changes and Hello carry it. When a generation appears, or names another host, the daemon reopens the runtime under a write lock that waits for running socket and session requests, so an enrollment while the daemon runs needs no unit restart (spec section 3: "the service then dials").
- Decision: the daemon runs `Recover(RecoverStart)` after `runtime.Open` and before `socket.Listen`. Spec section 8 and the brief say "after the lock and before serving"; running it after the runtime's migrations means the sweep's `core_locks` statements see the current schema on the first start of a new binary.
- Decision: the agent runs `transport.Heartbeat` with the zero `HeartbeatConfig` (row 42's "zero or positive"). yamux keep-alive is off (`session.go` `muxConfig`), so without it an agent whose controller vanished without closing the TCP connection would wait on a dead session until TCP gave up; with it, three missed pings (45 s) close the session and the dial loop redials.
- Decision: after a successful renewal the daemon closes its session and redials at once (backoff just reset, so 0 to 1 s). Spec section 2 deletes the previous generation "after the first successful handshake with the new one"; reconnecting makes that handshake happen while the old credentials still exist on disk, and lets the controller revoke the old serial then rather than at its expiry.
- Decision: acceptance is a yamux ping answered on the new session; refusal is a TLS alert from the controller (`net.OpError` with `Op` `"remote error"`, `$GOROOT/src/crypto/tls/conn.go:733,740`, go1.27.2). In TLS 1.3 the client's handshake completes before the server verifies the client certificate, so `transport.Dial` succeeds for a revoked agent and the alert arrives on the first read; dry run 2026-10-20: with refusal detection disabled, `TestCheck` failed with `Check of a revoked host = 2, <nil>; want refused`.
- Decision: `agent check` without credentials answers `CheckRefused` (only a token helps, the same branch `install`'s enrollment step takes, `internal/install/install.go:445-471`); `Check` deletes no generation.
- Decision: `ReadTokenFile` takes the owner uid as a parameter so tests pass `os.Getuid()`; it checks the opened file (no TOCTOU), refuses FIFOs without blocking (`O_NONBLOCK`), and follows a symlink to check its target, as `install.sh`'s `stat -L` does.
- Row 42 is discharged in two halves: Task 19 builds `EnrollConfig` from the `ParseToken` result unchanged and adds `TestEnrollEndToEnd` against the real `fleet.Authority` behind `transport.Handshake`; Task 27 calls `transport.Heartbeat` with the zero config. Row 67's three acceptance tests are `TestSocketRefusesOtherPeers` and `TestSocketAdmitsRootByDefault` (Task 18), `TestCheck` (Task 20) and `TestRunHoldsTheAgentLock` (Task 27). Rows 42 and 67 are therefore cited on Task 27, the task that completes them.
- Row 74 is half outside this plan: the daemon sets `api.Config.Log` on both handlers it builds (Task 27), but defaulting a nil `Log` in `internal/agent/api` or documenting it as required is an edit to `internal/agent/api/`, which no plan-5 task may touch (Global Constraints). Task 27 cites row 74 for P10's duty; the handler half needs P12 or a register note.
- Socket tests ran as root on this host (`id -u` = 0), so `TestSocketAdmitsRootByDefault` took its root branch; the compiled test binary was also run as uid 65534 (`setpriv --reuid=65534`) on 2026-10-20, on `34ccd73` and again on the amended `dff0921`, and every socket test passed, the default refusing that uid. Task 18's Step 4 runs that check only as root with `setpriv` installed.
- Unit tests touch no real systemd, `/etc`, `/run` or `/var/lib`: layouts come from `install.Rooted(t.TempDir())` with the socket under `os.MkdirTemp("", "dp")`, systemd is `stubSystemd` (every unit `not-found`), controllers listen on `127.0.0.1:0`, and the daemon's clock is a `coretest.FakeClock`; `waitClock` advances it by `transport.BackoffMax` between polls, so no test waits on a backoff, `CredentialPoll` or `RenewRetry`. Certificates use a fake clock starting at `time.Now()` truncated to the second, because TLS verification reads the real time. The renewal tests start the authority's clock 300 days back (the enrolled certificate is past two thirds of its life) and then advance it to now, so the renewed certificate is fresh and the daemon renews exactly once.
- Dry-run findings fixed in the tests before the commits: `TestCheck` first read the controller's refusal counter before the controller's goroutine recorded it (it failed once in an 8-run `-race` repeat of the package), so it waits for the counter; `TestEnrollChecksTheAnswer`'s first "another ca" case was also caught by the chain check, so the fingerprint check went untested (a mutation removing it passed), and the case now swaps in a consistent root and certificate of another authority; `TestRunDeletesTheOldGenerationAfterAHandshake` advances the authority's clock one second before `Renew`, because `Authority.Handshake` revokes only serials whose `created_at` is earlier (`certs.go:148`).
- Mutations that the tests caught on 2026-10-20: the generation steps reordered to switch `current` before writing (`TestSaveGenerationInterrupted`), the enrollment fingerprint and host checks removed (`TestEnrollChecksTheAnswer`), pruning removed (`TestRunDeletesTheOldGenerationAfterAHandshake`), and the start recovery moved after `socket.Listen` (`TestRunRecoversBeforeServing`).
- Tasks 19 to 28 share package `daemon` and run one after another.
- Cross-part needs that X1-X6 do not cover: (1) P8's enrollment and renewal endpoints answer a refusal as problem details carrying `code` (`enroll.token_invalid`, `enroll.rate_limited`), which `install`'s enrollment step relies on (`internal/install/install.go:473-476`), and a success as `200` with the JSON of `EnrollResponse` or `RenewResponse`; (2) P8 builds a `transport.ControllerSession` for every accepted `ALPNAgent` connection, since acceptance is a ping answered on it; (3) a `darkpanel agent check` connection is a session to P8's registry, so it replaces a running daemon's session, which redials; (4) P9's dispatcher must keep skipping the core in `Hosts.hello` (Hello now lists it); (5) P12 passes `AllowPeer` admitting the invoking user under `--dev`.

- The engine returns a plan's existing change before it checks expiry, the confirmation or the state hash (`internal/core/lifecycle/apply.go:79-88`, read 2026-10-20), and never purges a plan that has a change (`internal/core/lifecycle/recover.go:118-119`), so re-applying a stored plan after a lost answer or a restart never acts twice; the dispatcher keeps an applied plan in `core_plan_index` and answers `plan.not_found` for an unapplied one after `PlanIndexRetention` (`internal/controller/dispatch/dispatch.go:18-22`, `apply.go:67-84`).
- The engine's apply re-runs the handler's Plan and returns its error (`internal/core/lifecycle/apply.go:201-212`), so a second rollout planned while none was active is refused at apply with `rollout.in_progress` by the plan check (`TestRolloutActionRefusals`), and two applies that both pass the recheck are serialised by the writer, where Create's check sees the first (`TestCreateRefusesASecondActiveRollout`, four concurrent creates, one succeeds).
- Irreversible actions must return a confirmation phrase (`internal/core/lifecycle/engine.go:183-187`).
- Migration numbers may have gaps (`internal/core/store/migrate.go:18-21`), so `0007_rollout.sql` builds and migrates before or after P9's `0006_journal_epoch.sql` merges; a persistent development database that applied 0007 before 0006 existed would refuse 0006 (`migrate.go:199-216`), which no test or release database does.
- SQLite (modernc.org/sqlite v1.60.1) accepts a partial unique index on the constant expression `(1)`: with it removed, `TestRolloutMigration` failed with `a second running rollout was accepted` (dry-run mutation, 2026-10-20).
- `audit.Store.Append` writes inside the caller's transaction (`internal/controller/audit/audit.go:117-120`) and `core_audit.phase` accepts `apply` (`0004_audit.sql`); the scheduler takes the audit log as `core.Journal`, so P9's epoch change to the audit store does not reach it. The tests use `coretest.MemJournal`, which ignores the transaction, so atomicity is shown by a failing entry rolling the rollout and the window back.
- `coretest.FakeClock.Advance` runs due timers on the calling goroutine (`internal/core/coretest/clock.go:49-77`), so driver and scheduler passes run synchronously inside the tests' `Advance` calls and no test waits real time.
- The rollback action's transient unit is already covered by P5's `TestStartRollback` (`internal/update/rollback_test.go:331-359`); `TestRollbackAction` checks it once more through the engine.
- 2026-10-19 is a Monday (`date -u -d 2026-10-19 +%A`), which the schedule tests rely on.
- Dry run 2026-10-20: `TestActionsRegisterWithoutAnUpdater` failed with `error = update: this process has no updater; update actions run on the agent, want code internal`, because P5's `wantCode` requires an `*errcode.Error`; an uncoded error is what the API reports as `internal` (`errcode.From`, `internal/core/errcode/errcode.go:180`), so the test uses `wantInternal`.
- Dry run 2026-10-20: two helper names collided across tasks of package `rollout` (`setHostState` in Task 11's test and the driver's `record` test helper against the store type `record`); the production helper is `setRolloutHost` and the test helper `updateRecord`.
- Dry run 2026-10-20 (plan-review amendment): with every `internal` treated as "not yet", `TestDriverRefusalsFailTheHost/a_plan_answered_internal` and `/an_apply_answered_internal` failed with `rollout = running [OWN=updating A=waiting], want failed [OWN=failed A=skipped]`, and with status refusals failing the host `TestDriverFollowWaitsThroughAStatusRefusal/not_found` failed with `rollout = failed [OWN=failed A=skipped], want running [OWN=updating A=waiting]`; both pass with the amended `notYet` and `follow`.
- Dry run 2026-10-20: `TestDriverYieldsToACancelDuringACall` failed with `rollout = failed [OWN=failed A=skipped], want cancelled` with the staleness check in `(*Driver).write` disabled, and `TestDriverSteppedUpdateAdvancesOnlyOnTheTarget` failed with the target-version check removed; the scheduler tests failed with the own-host exception removed.
- Decision: the ten-minute limit runs from when the host's operation id is stored, as spec step 3 states it for polling. Counted from the start of the host's turn, a controller restart longer than `PlanTTL` (which the spec requires to re-plan) would always time the host out. Before the operation is stored, an offline host or a transport error waits without a limit; such a rollout is cancelled at once by `core.update.rollout-cancel`, because no host was handed its update.
- Decision: a host is handed its update when its plan id is stored. A cancel without `force` then drains, and while draining the driver re-applies that stored plan to learn its outcome, so an apply the driver had decided before the cancel may still start; a cleared plan while draining skips the host.
- Decision (amended after plan review): when planning, applying and resolving a change, the driver treats an error without an `*errcode.Error` (`errors.As` fails: transport or local failure), `host.offline` and `lock.held` as "not yet" and every coded answer as a failure of the host, `internal` included, because `errcode.From` reports an uncoded error and an agent's coded `internal` (for example update actions registered without an updater) alike and the latter would otherwise hold the rollout running with no time limit; while following a stored operation every status error is "not yet", because the operation may still run and the spec fails the rollout only once it is terminal, and `HostTimeout` then drains it with `update.interrupted`. `plan.state_changed` is re-planned like `plan.expired` and `plan.not_found`, because the engine answers it only when no change was made.
- Decision: draining skips the waiting hosts at once; a forced cancel marks a handed-off updating host `failed` with `update.interrupted` and a host without a plan `skipped`; timeouts also use `update.interrupted`. Spec section 11 adds no rollout failure code and this part may not edit `internal/core/errcode`.
- Decision: a rollout's error is its failed host's error with the message prefixed `host <id>: `; a cancelled rollout carries no error unless it was already draining after a timeout.
- Decision: hosts must be `enrolled` to join a rollout (a pending or revoked host has no session). The spec's `--embedded-agent` development mode keeps its local host `pending` and its version `dev`, so it cannot run rollouts; the agent refuses to update a `dev` build anyway (S-C11).
- Decision: a controller version that does not parse counts as older than every target (X5 makes `dev` equal only to itself), both for the action's own-host refusal and for the scheduler's hold-back rule; the scheduler skips a host whose `agent_version` is null or does not parse.
- Decision: the update rollback's confirmation phrase is the version it returns to; the rollout cancel's is the rollout id. The update apply's Verify turns a failed record's known error code into the change's error code, so the driver reports `update.signature_invalid` and the like.
- Decision: the scheduler claims at most one window per pass and visits the controller's own host first; an index error ends the pass (logged) and the next minute retries.
- Decision (columns beyond the spec's list): `core_rollouts.outcome`, `change_id`, the error as `error_code` and `error_message` on both tables, `core_rollout_hosts.followed_at`, primary key `(rollout_id, host_id)`, `UNIQUE (rollout_id, position)`, a partial unique index allowing one `updating` host per rollout, and no foreign key from `host_id` to `core_hosts`, so P9's `core.host.delete` is never blocked by rollout history.
- Decision: the driver's dispatcher dependency is the three-method `rollout.Dispatcher`, and the scheduler's online test is `dispatch.ClientSource` (F-C9), which P8's session registry implements (X3); `rollout` imports `dispatch` (allowed by X2) and `internal/update`.
- No deviation from the ownership table: P11 touches only `internal/update/actions.go`, `internal/update/actions_test.go`, `internal/controller/rollout/` and `0007_rollout.sql`. Register rows 12 and 15 stay with the spec (harness acceptance); row 23 is untouched (the update and rollout actions declare no lock keys, as spec section 8 requires for `core.update.apply`).

```go
package schemas_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/registry"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/schemas"
)

func TestHashKey(t *testing.T) {
	if got := schemas.HashKey(core.ActionRef{Name: "hostinfo.summary", Version: 2}); got != "hostinfo.summary@2" {
		t.Errorf("HashKey = %q, want hostinfo.summary@2", got)
	}
}

// TestActionHashesMatchTheEmbeddedSnapshots shows that a hash computed from an
// action definition names the committed snapshot the controller looks up.
func TestActionHashesMatchTheEmbeddedSnapshots(t *testing.T) {
	reg, err := registry.Build()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, side := range []core.Side{core.SideAgent, core.SideController} {
		hashes, err := schemas.ActionHashes(reg, side)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		n := 0
		for _, def := range reg.Actions() {
			if def.Spec.Side != side {
				continue
			}
			n++
			file, err := fs.ReadFile(schemas.FS, fmt.Sprintf("%s/v%d.json", def.Spec.Name, def.Spec.Version))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			want, err := schemas.SnapshotHashes(file)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if got := hashes[schemas.HashKey(def.Ref())]; got != want {
				t.Errorf("%s: hash %+v, want the embedded snapshot's %+v", def.Ref(), got, want)
			}
		}
		// The agent side has no action until the update actions are
		// registered, so only the controller side must be non-empty.
		if len(hashes) != n || side == core.SideController && n == 0 {
			t.Errorf("side %s: %d hashes for %d actions", side, len(hashes), n)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `schemas/hashes.go`:

```go
package schemas

import (
	"fmt"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
)

// HashKey is the key of an action version in AgentHello.SchemaHashes:
// "<action name>@<version>".
func HashKey(ref core.ActionRef) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s@%d", ref.Name, ref.Version)
}

// ActionHash returns the schema hashes of one action version. It hashes the
// snapshot schema.ActionSnapshot renders rather than the embedded file, so an
// action without a committed snapshot, such as a test module's, still has a
// hash; for a registered action the two are the same bytes, which
// TestSnapshotsCurrent enforces.
func ActionHash(def core.ActionDef) (core.SchemaHash, error) {
	snap, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(def)
	if err != nil {
		return core.SchemaHash{}, fmt.Errorf("schemas: snapshot %s: %w", def.Ref(), err)
	}
	return SnapshotHashes(snap)
}

// ActionHashes returns the hashes of every action version of side in reg,
// keyed by HashKey.
func ActionHashes(reg *core.Registry, side core.Side) (map[string]core.SchemaHash, error) {
	out := map[string]core.SchemaHash{}
	for _, def := range reg.Actions() {
		if def.Spec.Side != side {
			continue
		}
		h, err := ActionHash(def)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[HashKey(def.Ref())] = h
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

Create `internal/update/actions_test.go`:

```go
package update

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"log/slog"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/lifecycle"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
)

// actionPrincipal is the rollout driver, the principal that applies update
// actions on a host.
var actionPrincipal = core.Principal{Actor: core.ActorRollout, Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}

// actionModule is a core module holding only the given actions.
type actionModule struct{ defs []core.ActionDef }

func (m actionModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: core.ModuleIDCore, Version: 1, Actions: m.defs, DefaultEnabled: true}
}

// newActionEngine returns the agent engine of host h1 over e's agent
// database, serving the update actions of u.
func newActionEngine(t *testing.T, e *updaterEnv, u *Updater) *lifecycle.Engine {
	t.Helper()
	reg, err := core.NewRegistry(actionModule{Actions(u)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	eng, err := lifecycle.New(lifecycle.Config{
		Side: core.SideAgent, Registry: reg, Validator: schema.NewValidator(), Journal: &coretest.MemJournal{},
		Redact: schema.Redact,
		Base: core.Env{HostID: "h1", DB: e.db, Sealer: coretest.Sealer{}, Clock: e.clock,
			Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(eng.Close)
	return eng
}

// actionDef returns the action of defs named name.
func actionDef(t *testing.T, defs []core.ActionDef, name string) core.ActionDef {
	t.Helper()
	i := slices.IndexFunc(defs, func(d core.ActionDef) bool { return d.Spec.Name == name })
	if i < 0 {
		t.Fatalf("no action %s", name)
	}
	return defs[i]
}

// wantInternal fails unless err is one the API reports as internal: an
// error without a code.
func wantInternal(t *testing.T, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	if err == nil || errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.Internal {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want an internal error", err)
	}
}

// diffText joins a plan's diff lines.
func diffText(diff []core.DiffLine) string {
	var b strings.Builder
	for _, l := range diff {
		b.WriteString(l.Op + " " + l.Text + "\n")
	}
	return b.String()
}

func TestActionsRegisterWithoutAnUpdater(t *testing.T) {
	defs := Actions(nil)
	reg, err := core.NewRegistry(actionModule{defs})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]struct {
		class         core.Class
		compensatedBy string
	}{
		ActionApply:    {core.ClassCompensable, ActionRollback},
		ActionRollback: {core.ClassIrreversible, ""},
		ActionStatus:   {core.ClassRead, ""},
	}
	if len(reg.Actions()) != len(want) {
		t.Fatalf("registered %d actions, want %d", len(reg.Actions()), len(want))
	}
	for _, def := range reg.Actions() {
		w, ok := want[def.Spec.Name]
		switch {
		case !ok:
			t.Errorf("unexpected action %s", def.Ref())
		case def.Spec.Version != 1 || def.Spec.Side != core.SideAgent || def.Spec.Class != w.class ||
			def.Spec.CompensatedBy != w.compensatedBy || len(def.Spec.LockKeys) != 0 || def.Spec.BreakGlass:
			t.Errorf("%s spec = %+v", def.Ref(), def.Spec)
		}
		if _, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(def); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s snapshot: %v", def.Ref(), err)
		}
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	apply := actionDef(t, defs, ActionApply)
	_, err = apply.Handler.Plan(ctx, core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`))
	wantInternal(t, err)
	_, err = apply.Handler.Apply(ctx, core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`))
	wantInternal(t, err)
	err = apply.Handler.Verify(ctx, core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`),
		json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	wantInternal(t, err)
	rollback := actionDef(t, defs, ActionRollback)
	_, err = rollback.Handler.Plan(ctx, core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{"force":false}`))
	wantInternal(t, err)
	_, err = actionDef(t, defs, ActionStatus).Handler.Read(ctx, core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	wantInternal(t, err)
}

func TestApplyAction(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := newUpdaterEnv(t, "1.2.0")
	eng := newActionEngine(t, e, e.updater(t, "1.2.0"))
	plan, err := eng.Plan(ctx, actionPrincipal, core.ActionRef{Name: ActionApply, Version: 1},
		json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.4.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	diff := diffText(plan.Diff)
	for _, want := range []string{"- darkpanel 1.2.0", "+ darkpanel 1.4.0 (stable)", "installs 1.3.1, then 1.4.0"} {
		if !strings.Contains(diff, want) {
			t.Errorf("diff = %q, want it to contain %q", diff, want)
		}
	}
	v, err := eng.Apply(ctx, actionPrincipal, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if v.State != core.StateCompleted {
		t.Fatalf("change = %+v, want completed", v)
	}
	var tr core.UpdateTransaction
	if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &tr); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if tr.State != core.UpdatePending || tr.FromVersion != "1.2.0" || tr.ToVersion != "1.3.1" ||
		tr.TargetVersion != "1.4.0" || tr.OperationID != tr.ID {
		t.Errorf("transaction = %+v", tr)
	}
	if r := mustRecord(t, e.p, tr.ID); r.ChangeID != v.ID {
		t.Errorf("record change id = %q, want the change %q", r.ChangeID, v.ID)
	}
	if units := e.sd.unitsOf("StartTransientService"); !slices.Equal(units, []string{RunnerUnit(tr.ID)}) {
		t.Errorf("transient units = %v", units)
	}
	if got := lockRows(t, e.db); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"* " + lifecycle.ReservationOwner(tr.ID)}) {
		t.Errorf("core_locks = %v", got)
	}
	again, err := eng.Apply(ctx, actionPrincipal, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	if err != nil || again.ID != v.ID {
		t.Errorf("repeated apply = %+v, %v; want the same change", again, err)
	}
	if n := len(e.sd.unitsOf("StartTransientService")); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("a repeated apply started %d runner units, want 1", n)
	}
}

func TestApplyActionRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := newUpdaterEnv(t, "1.2.0")
	eng := newActionEngine(t, e, e.updater(t, "1.2.0"))
	ref := core.ActionRef{Name: ActionApply, Version: 1}
	tests := []struct {
		input string
		code  errcode.Code
	}{
		{`{"version":"1.1.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`, errcode.UpdateDowngradeRefused},
		{`{"version":"1.9.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`, errcode.UpdateNotFound},
		{`{"version":"1.6.0-beta.1","channel":"stable","force":false}`, errcode.UpdateNotFound},
		{`{"version":"1.2.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`, errcode.InvalidInput},
		{`{"version":"v1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`, errcode.InvalidInput},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		_, err := eng.Plan(ctx, actionPrincipal, ref, json.RawMessage(tt.input))
		wantCode(t, err, tt.code)
	}
	if n := len(e.sd.unitsOf("StartTransientService")); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("refused plans started %d units", n)
	}
	wantNoLocks(t, e.db)
}

func TestApplyActionVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := newUpdaterEnv(t, "1.2.0")
	pending := testRecord("01A", "01A", 0)
	pending.ChangeID = "C1"
	failed := testRecord("01B", "01B", time.Second)
	failed.ChangeID, failed.State = "C2", core.UpdateFailed
	failed.Error = &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid), Message: "no trusted signature"}
	for _, r := range []Record{pending, failed} {
		if err := WriteRecord(e.p, r); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	verify := actionDef(t, Actions(e.updater(t, "1.2.0")), ActionApply).Handler.Verify
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`)
	tests := []struct {
		name, changeID, out string
		code                errcode.Code // "" for success, errcode.Internal for an uncoded error
	}{
		{"pending record", "C1", `{"id":"01A"}`, ""},
		{"failed record", "C2", `{"id":"01B"}`, errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid},
		{"missing record", "C1", `{"id":"01MISSING"}`, errcode.NotFound},
		{"recovery finds the record by change", "C1", `{}`, ""},
		{"recovery finds the failed record", "C2", `{}`, errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid},
		{"recovery without a record", "C3", `{}`, errcode.Internal},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			err := verify(ctx, core.Env{ChangeID: tt.changeID}, in, json.RawMessage(tt.out))
			if tt.code == "" {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("Verify = %v, want nil", err)
				}
				return
			}
			if tt.code == errcode.Internal {
				wantInternal(t, err)
				return
			}
			wantCode(t, err, tt.code)
		})
	}
}

func TestRollbackAction(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := newUpdaterEnv(t, "1.2.0")
	committedUpdate(t, e, "01UP", "1.2.0", "1.3.0")
	eng := newActionEngine(t, e, e.updater(t, "1.3.0"))
	plan, err := eng.Plan(ctx, actionPrincipal, core.ActionRef{Name: ActionRollback, Version: 1},
		json.RawMessage(`{"force":false}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if plan.Confirmation != "1.2.0" {
		t.Errorf("confirmation = %q, want the version rolled back to", plan.Confirmation)
	}
	if diff := diffText(plan.Diff); !strings.Contains(diff, "- darkpanel 1.3.0") || !strings.Contains(diff, "+ darkpanel 1.2.0") {
		t.Errorf("diff = %q", diff)
	}
	_, err = eng.Apply(ctx, actionPrincipal, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanConfirmationNeeded)
	v, err := eng.Apply(ctx, actionPrincipal, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{Confirmation: "1.2.0"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if v.State != core.StateCompleted {
		t.Fatalf("change = %+v, want completed", v)
	}
	var tr core.UpdateTransaction
	if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &tr); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if tr.Kind != core.UpdateKindRollback || tr.State != core.UpdatePending || tr.ToVersion != "1.2.0" {
		t.Errorf("transaction = %+v", tr)
	}
	if r := mustRecord(t, e.p, tr.ID); r.ChangeID != v.ID {
		t.Errorf("record change id = %q, want the change %q", r.ChangeID, v.ID)
	}
	if units := e.sd.unitsOf("StartTransientService"); !slices.Equal(units, []string{RollbackRunnerUnit(tr.ID)}) {
		t.Errorf("transient units = %v", units)
	}
}

func TestRollbackActionRefusesWithoutAnUpdate(t *testing.T) {

	eng := newActionEngine(t, e, e.updater(t, "1.2.0"))

func TestStatusAction(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := newUpdaterEnv(t, "1.2.0")
	committedUpdate(t, e, "01UP", "1.2.0", "1.3.0")
	eng := newActionEngine(t, e, e.updater(t, "1.3.0"))
	ref := core.ActionRef{Name: ActionStatus, Version: 1}
	out, err := eng.Read(ctx, actionPrincipal, ref, json.RawMessage(`{"operationId":"01UP"}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var st core.UpdateStatus
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &st); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(st.Transactions) != 1 || st.Transactions[0].ID != "01UP" || st.Transactions[0].State != core.UpdateCommitted {
		t.Errorf("status = %+v", st)
	}
	_, err = eng.Read(ctx, actionPrincipal, ref, json.RawMessage(`{"operationId":"01UP","changeId":"C1"}`))
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/update/actions.go`:

```go
package update

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

// The core module's agent-side update actions (spec section 8). ADR 0003
// freezes their names for protocol 1.
const (
	ActionApply    = "core.update.apply"
	ActionRollback = "core.update.rollback"
	ActionStatus   = "core.update.status"
)

// Actions returns core.update.apply/v1, core.update.rollback/v1 and
// core.update.status/v1 over u, for coreactions.Deps.Extra. Every process
// registers the one core manifest, but only the agent runs updates: the
// controller process passes nil, and every handler then fails with an
// internal error.
func Actions(u *Updater) []core.ActionDef {
	a := updateActions{u: u}
	return []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineMutating[core.UpdateApplyInput, core.UpdateTransaction](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionApply, Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideAgent,
			CompensatedBy: ActionRollback,
			Summary: "Update this host to a signed release; the change completes before the services restart, " +
				"and core.update.status follows the update.",
		}, applyAction{a}),
		core.DefineMutating[core.UpdateRollbackInput, core.UpdateTransaction](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionRollback, Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideAgent,
			Summary: "Roll this host back to the binary its last update replaced; the confirmation phrase is " +
				"that binary's version.",
		}, rollbackAction{a}),
		core.DefineRead[core.UpdateStatusInput, core.UpdateStatus](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionStatus, Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent,
			Summary: "List this host's update and rollback transactions, newest first.",
		}, statusAction{a}),
	}
}

type updateActions struct{ u *Updater }

func (a updateActions) updater() (*Updater, error) {
	if a.u == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("update: this process has no updater; update actions run on the agent")
	}
	return a.u, nil
}

// actionStateHash is a plan's state hash over the parts that decide what
// an apply would do.
func actionStateHash(parts ...string) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(parts, "\x00")))
	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// verifyStarted checks that the record a change's Apply wrote exists and has
// not failed: the record id from the change's output, or, for the Verify
// that startup recovery runs without an output, the oldest record of the
// operation changeID started. A failed record's error code becomes the
// change's.
func (u *Updater) verifyStarted(ctx context.Context, changeID, id string) error {
	var tr core.UpdateTransaction
	switch {
	case id != "":
		var err error
		if tr, err = u.Transaction(ctx, id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	case changeID == "":
		return errors.New("update: verify without a change or a transaction")
	default:
		st, err := u.Status(ctx, core.UpdateStatusInput{ChangeID: changeID})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if len(st.Transactions) == 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("update: change %s wrote no update record", changeID)
		}
		tr = st.Transactions[len(st.Transactions)-1]
	}
	if tr.State != core.UpdateFailed {
		return nil
	}
	if tr.Error != nil && errcode.Known(errcode.Code(tr.Error.Code)) {
		return errcode.New(errcode.Code(tr.Error.Code), tr.Error.Message)
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("update: transaction %s failed", tr.ID)
}

type applyAction struct{ updateActions }

func (h applyAction) Plan(ctx context.Context, _ core.Env, in core.UpdateApplyInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	steps, err := u.CheckApply(ctx, in)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	versions := make([]string, len(steps))
	hashed := []string{u.cfg.Version}
	for i, s := range steps {
		versions[i] = s.Version
		hashed = append(hashed, s.Version+"@"+s.Channel)
	}
	diff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "darkpanel " + u.cfg.Version},
		{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel %s (%s)", in.Version, in.Channel)},
	}
	if len(steps) > 1 {
		diff = append(diff, core.DiffLine{Op: " ", Text: "installs " + strings.Join(versions, ", then ") +
			", one minor version at a time"})
	}
	diff = append(diff, core.DiffLine{Op: " ", Text: "the services restart after this change completes; " +
		"a failed health check rolls the host back automatically"})
	return core.PlanOutcome{Diff: diff, Guards: []core.Guard{}, StateHash: actionStateHash(hashed...)}, nil
}

func (h applyAction) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.UpdateApplyInput) (core.UpdateTransaction, error) {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdateTransaction{}, err
	}
	return u.StartUpdate(ctx, env.ChangeID, in)
}

func (h applyAction) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ core.UpdateApplyInput, out core.UpdateTransaction) error {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return u.verifyStarted(ctx, env.ChangeID, out.ID)
}

type rollbackAction struct{ updateActions }

func (h rollbackAction) Plan(ctx context.Context, _ core.Env, in core.UpdateRollbackInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	last, err := u.rollbackTarget(ctx, in.Force)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "-", Text: "darkpanel " + last.ToVersion},
			{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel %s, the binary update %s replaced", last.FromVersion, last.OperationID)},
			{Op: " ", Text: "the services restart; core.update.status shows the rollback's result"},
		},
		Guards:       []core.Guard{},
		StateHash:    actionStateHash(last.ID, last.State, last.PrevSHA256),
		Confirmation: last.FromVersion,
	}, nil
}

func (h rollbackAction) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.UpdateRollbackInput) (core.UpdateTransaction, error) {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdateTransaction{}, err
	}
	return u.StartRollback(ctx, env.ChangeID, in)
}

func (h rollbackAction) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ core.UpdateRollbackInput, out core.UpdateTransaction) error {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return u.verifyStarted(ctx, env.ChangeID, out.ID)
}

type statusAction struct{ updateActions }

func (h statusAction) Read(ctx context.Context, _ core.Env, in core.UpdateStatusInput) (core.UpdateStatus, error) {
	u, err := h.updater()
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdateStatus{}, err
	}
	return u.Status(ctx, in)
}
```

```go
	}
	if ifs.count() != 1 {

			addrs("100.64.0.2", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::2"), `["100.64.0.2","fd7a:115c:a1e0::2"]`},
		{"interface gone", false, nil, addrs("100.64.0.2", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::2"), `["100.64.0.2","fd7a:115c:a1e0::2"]`},
		{"addresses gone", true, nil, nil, `[]`},
	}

		if got := w.Addrs(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, s.want) {

		}
		if got := savedAddrs(t, db); got != s.saved {
			t.Fatalf("%s: saved %s, want %s", s.name, got, s.saved)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(log.String(), "listen: Tailscale addresses changed") {
		t.Fatalf("the change was not logged:\n%s", log.String())
	}

	got := w.Addrs()

	if w.Addrs()[0] != netip.MustParseAddr("100.64.0.3") {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the watcher**

Create `internal/controller/listen/watch.go`:

```go
package listen

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"net/netip"
	"slices"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// TailscaleWatcher follows the addresses of tailscale0 for a listener (ADR
// 0006); the web and the agent listener each run one. It is not safe for
// concurrent use: its owner serialises Refresh and Addrs.
type TailscaleWatcher struct {
	db      *store.DB
	ifaces  InterfaceAddrs
	log     *slog.Logger
	addrs   []netip.Addr
	unsaved bool // addrs differ from those saved in core_meta
}

// NewTailscaleWatcher starts from the addresses saved in core_meta,
// replaced by those of tailscale0 when that interface exists, so a restart
// serves the tailnet before tailscaled is up. Nil ifaces means
// SystemInterfaceAddrs; a nil log discards.
func NewTailscaleWatcher(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, ifaces InterfaceAddrs, log *slog.Logger) (*TailscaleWatcher, error) {
	if ifaces == nil {
		ifaces = SystemInterfaceAddrs
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	saved, err := LoadTailscaleAddrs(ctx, db)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	w := &TailscaleWatcher{db: db, ifaces: ifaces, log: log, addrs: saved}
	w.Refresh(ctx)
	return w, nil
}

// Refresh reads tailscale0. An absent interface means tailscaled is not up,
// so the last addresses stay; a present one replaces them, even with none.
// A failed save is retried at every Refresh.
func (w *TailscaleWatcher) Refresh(ctx context.Context) {
	list, present, err := w.ifaces(TailscaleInterface)
	if err != nil {
		w.log.Warn("listen: cannot read the Tailscale addresses", "err", err)
	} else if next := TailscaleAddrs(list); present && !slices.Equal(next, w.addrs) {
		w.log.Info("listen: Tailscale addresses changed", "from", w.addrs, "to", next)
		w.addrs = next
		w.unsaved = true
	}
	if !w.unsaved {
		return
	}
	if err := SaveTailscaleAddrs(ctx, w.db, w.addrs); err != nil {
		w.log.Warn("listen: cannot save the Tailscale addresses", "err", err)
		return
	}
	w.unsaved = false
}

// Addrs returns the current Tailscale addresses, sorted, or nil.
func (w *TailscaleWatcher) Addrs() []netip.Addr { return slices.Clone(w.addrs) }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/controller/coreactions/window.go`:

```go
package coreactions

import "github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"

// ProtocolVersion is the agent protocol this controller speaks (ADR 0003).
const ProtocolVersion = 1

// Supported reports whether an agent running agentVersion over protocol is
// inside the support window of a controller running controllerVersion (spec
// section 5): protocol 1, the controller's major version, and its minor
// version or the one before. An agent outside the window, older or newer,
// accepts only the core.update actions. A version that does not parse, such
// as a development build's "dev", is equal only to the same string.
func Supported(controllerVersion, agentVersion string, protocol int) bool {
	if protocol != ProtocolVersion {
		return false
	}
	if agentVersion == controllerVersion {
		return true
	}
	c, err := update.ParseVersion(controllerVersion)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	a, err := update.ParseVersion(agentVersion)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	return a.Major == c.Major && (a.Minor == c.Minor || a.Minor+1 == c.Minor)
}
```

```diff
 )
 
@@ -28,6 +29,24 @@ type Deps struct {

 	// rule and uninstall cover them.
 	OwnedPaths []core.OwnedPath
+	// Authority issues enrollment tokens and revokes agent certificates.
+	Authority *fleet.Authority
+	// AgentAdvertise is the address an enrollment token carries when the
+	// request names none: serve's --agent-advertise.
+	AgentAdvertise string
+	// Sessions closes the live agent session of a revoked or deleted host;
+	// without it no session is closed.
+	Sessions Sessions
+	// Version is the controller's own version, the reference of the agent
+	// support window; empty leaves every reporting agent supported.
+	Version string
+}
+
+// Sessions closes a host's live agent session when the host is revoked or
+// deleted.
+type Sessions interface {
+	// Disconnect closes hostID's live session, if it has one.
+	Disconnect(hostID string)
 }
 
 // Module returns the core module.
```

```go
// journalEpochOf reads the journal epoch agent.db stores.
func journalEpochOf(t *testing.T, f *fixture) string {
	t.Helper()
	var v string
	if err := f.db.Read().QueryRow("SELECT value FROM core_meta WHERE key = 'journal_epoch'").Scan(&v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("journal_epoch: %v", err)
	}
	return v
}

func TestJournalEpoch(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	hello := func() string {
		t.Helper()
		h, err := f.rt.Hello(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return h.JournalEpoch
	}
	first := journalEpochOf(t, f)
	if _, err := ulid.ParseStrict(first); err != nil || hello() != first {
		t.Fatalf("epoch %q (%v), Hello %q; want one ULID", first, err, hello())
	}
	// A restart keeps the epoch, also under another host id, as after a
	// re-enrollment.
	f.restart()
	if got := hello(); got != first {
		t.Errorf("after a restart the epoch is %q, want %q", got, first)
	}
	f.rt.Close()
	c := f.config()
	c.HostID = "h2"
	rt, err := Open(ctx, c)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(rt.Close)
	f.rt = rt
	if got := hello(); got != first {
		t.Errorf("under another host id the epoch is %q, want %q", got, first)
	}
	// A migration that recreates core_journal deletes the row, and the next
	// start creates a new epoch.
	if _, err := f.db.Writer().ExecContext(ctx, "DELETE FROM core_meta WHERE key = 'journal_epoch'"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.restart()
	renewed := hello()
	if _, err := ulid.ParseStrict(renewed); err != nil || renewed == first || journalEpochOf(t, f) != renewed {
		t.Errorf("after the row was deleted the epoch is %q, want a new ULID", renewed)
	}
	// Another agent.db has an epoch of its own.
	if other := newFixture(t); journalEpochOf(t, other) == renewed {
		t.Error("two agent databases share an epoch")
	}
}
```


with:

```go
	epoch   string                     // the journal epoch, fixed at Open
```

Then replace:

```go

```

with:

```go
	if err := r.migrate(ctx); err != nil {

		return nil, err

```

Then insert before `// check runs m's preflight checks in order; the first failure's error text is`:

```go
// journalEpochKey is the core_meta key of the journal epoch (spec section
// 5): a ULID created with agent.db and kept across restarts and
// re-enrollments, so the controller never ingests an acknowledged entry
// twice. A migration that recreates core_journal must delete this row in the
// same file: the next Open then creates a new epoch, and the controller
// restarts its cursor instead of skipping the new journal's first entries.
const journalEpochKey = "journal_epoch"

// journalEpoch returns agent.db's journal epoch, creating it when the
// database has none.
func journalEpoch(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, now time.Time) (string, error) {
	var epoch string
	err := db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT value FROM core_meta WHERE key = ?", journalEpochKey).Scan(&epoch)
		if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			return err
		}
		epoch = ulid.MustNew(ulid.Timestamp(now), rand.Reader).String()
		_, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO core_meta (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)", journalEpochKey, epoch)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("runtime: journal epoch: %w", err)
	}
	return epoch, nil
}
```

In `internal/agent/runtime/client.go`, replace:

```go
// Hello reports the agent, every module's availability as of its last check
// (migrations and preflight at Open, or a later Preflight) and the schema
// hashes of every agent-side action version.
func (r *Runtime) Hello(context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) {
	return core.AgentHello{
		ProtocolVersion: ProtocolVersion, BinaryVersion: r.cfg.BinaryVersion, OS: goruntime.GOOS,
		HostID: r.cfg.HostID, Modules: r.statuses(), SchemaHashes: maps.Clone(r.hashes),
	}, nil
}
```

with:

```go
// Hello reports the agent, every module's availability as of its last check
// (migrations and preflight at Open, or a later Preflight), the schema
// hashes of every agent-side action version and the journal epoch.
func (r *Runtime) Hello(context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) {
	return core.AgentHello{
		ProtocolVersion: ProtocolVersion, BinaryVersion: r.cfg.BinaryVersion, OS: goruntime.GOOS,
		HostID: r.cfg.HostID, Modules: r.statuses(), SchemaHashes: maps.Clone(r.hashes), JournalEpoch: r.epoch,
	}, nil
}
```

```diff
+// TestHostListReportsAgents covers the fields the agents' Hello and sessions
+// supply: a host is online only while the directory hands out its client,
+// and supported only when its last Hello is inside the window.
+func TestHostListReportsAgents(t *testing.T) {
+	e := newTestEnvWith(t, func(_ *testEnv, d *Deps) { d.Version = "1.3.0" })
+	e.addHost("h1", "alpha", true)
+	e.addHost("h2", "beta", false)
+	e.addHost("h3", "gamma", false)
+	e.addHost("h4", "delta", false)
+	seen := t0.Add(time.Minute)
+	report := `UPDATE core_hosts SET state = ?, last_seen_at = ?, agent_version = ?, protocol_version = ?, os = 'linux', arch = ?
+		WHERE id = ?`
+	e.exec(report, core.HostEnrolled, formatTime(seen), "1.3.0", 1, "amd64", "h1")
+	e.exec(report, core.HostEnrolled, formatTime(seen), "1.2.4", 1, "arm64", "h2")
+	e.exec(report, core.HostRevoked, formatTime(seen), "1.4.0", 1, "amd64", "h3")
+	e.hosts["h1"] = &coretest.FakeAgent{}
+
+	var got core.HostList
+	if err := e.read(root, "core.host.list", NoInput{}, &got); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	reported := func(id, name, state, version, arch string, local, online, supported bool) core.Host {
+		return core.Host{ID: id, Name: name, IsLocal: local, State: state, Online: online, LastSeenAt: ptr(seen),
+			AgentVersion: ptr(version), ProtocolVersion: ptr(1), OS: ptr("linux"), Arch: ptr(arch),
+			Supported: supported, Modules: []core.ModuleState{}}
+	}
+	want := []core.Host{
+		reported("h1", "alpha", core.HostEnrolled, "1.3.0", "amd64", true, true, true),
+		reported("h2", "beta", core.HostEnrolled, "1.2.4", "arm64", false, false, true),
+		{ID: "h4", Name: "delta", State: core.HostPending, Modules: []core.ModuleState{}},
+		reported("h3", "gamma", core.HostRevoked, "1.4.0", "amd64", false, false, false),
+	}
+	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Hosts, want) {
+		t.Errorf("hosts = %+v\nwant    %+v", got.Hosts, want)
+	}
+}
```

```diff
+// hostColumns are the core_hosts columns scanHost reads, in its order.
+const hostColumns = `id, name, is_local, state, last_seen_at, agent_version, protocol_version, os, arch`
+
+// scanHost reads one row of hostColumns: the stored half of a Host.
+func scanHost(row interface{ Scan(dest ...any) error }) (core.Host, error) {
+	var h core.Host
+	var lastSeen, version, osName, arch sql.NullString
+	var protocol sql.NullInt64
+	if err := row.Scan(&h.ID, &h.Name, &h.IsLocal, &h.State, &lastSeen, &version, &protocol, &osName, &arch); err != nil {
+		return core.Host{}, err
+	}
+	if lastSeen.Valid {
+		at, err := parseTime(lastSeen.String)
+		if err != nil {
+			return core.Host{}, err
+		}
+		h.LastSeenAt = &at
+	}
+	if protocol.Valid {
+		p := int(protocol.Int64)
+		h.ProtocolVersion = &p
+	}
+	h.AgentVersion, h.OS, h.Arch = nullable(version), nullable(osName), nullable(arch)
+	return h, nil
+}
+
+func nullable(s sql.NullString) *string {
+	if !s.Valid {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return &s.String
+}
+
+// complete fills in the half of h that core_hosts does not store: whether
+// its agent is online, whether its version is supported, and its modules.
+func (m module) complete(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, q querier, h *core.Host) error {
+	h.Online = online(ctx, env, h.ID)
+	h.Supported = h.AgentVersion != nil && h.ProtocolVersion != nil &&
+		(m.d.Version == "" || Supported(m.d.Version, *h.AgentVersion, *h.ProtocolVersion))
+	var err error
+	h.Modules, err = m.moduleStates(ctx, q, h.ID)
+	return err
+}
+
+// online reports whether hostID's agent has a live session. A host that
+// answers host.offline is offline; so is one that fails in any other way,
+// which is logged, because one broken agent must not fail a list of hosts.
+func online(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string) bool {
+	if env.Agents == nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	_, err := env.Agents.Agent(ctx, hostID)
+	if err != nil && errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.HostOffline && env.Log != nil {
+		env.Log.Warn("coreactions: host unreachable", "host", hostID, "error", err)
+	}
+	return err == nil
+}
```

```go
package dispatch

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/coreactions"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

// logRecords is a slog.Handler that keeps each record as
// "<level> <message> host=<host attribute>".
type logRecords struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	got []string
}

func (l *logRecords) Enabled(context.Context, slog.Level) bool { return true }

func (l *logRecords) Handle(_ context.Context, r slog.Record) error {
	host := ""
	r.Attrs(func(a slog.Attr) bool {
		if a.Key == "host" {
			host = a.Value.String()
		}
		return true
	})
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.got = append(l.got, fmt.Sprintf("%s %s host=%s", r.Level, r.Message, host))
	return nil
}

func (l *logRecords) WithAttrs([]slog.Attr) slog.Handler { return l }

func (l *logRecords) WithGroup(string) slog.Handler { return l }

func (l *logRecords) lines() []string {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(l.got)
}

// agentRow is the part of a core_hosts row Hello writes; "<null>" stands for
// NULL.
type agentRow struct {
	Version, OS, Arch, LastSeen string
	Protocol                    int64
}

func (h *harness) agentRow(hostID string) agentRow {
	h.t.Helper()
	var version, osName, arch, seen sql.NullString
	var protocol sql.NullInt64
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT agent_version, protocol_version, os, arch, last_seen_at FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`,
		hostID).Scan(&version, &protocol, &osName, &arch, &seen); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	text := func(s sql.NullString) string {
		if !s.Valid {
			return "<null>"
		}
		return s.String
	}
	return agentRow{Version: text(version), OS: text(osName), Arch: text(arch), LastSeen: text(seen), Protocol: protocol.Int64}
}

func TestHelloRecordsTheAgent(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	h.addHost("h2")
	hello := core.AgentHello{ProtocolVersion: 1, BinaryVersion: "1.2.3", OS: "linux", Arch: "arm64", HostID: "h2",
		JournalEpoch: "01EPOCHA"}
	h.clients.Add("h2", &coretest.FakeAgent{HelloFunc: func(context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) { return hello, nil }})
	if got := h.hosts.JournalEpoch("h2"); got != "" {
		t.Errorf("epoch before Hello = %q, want empty", got)
	}
	h.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	if _, err := h.hosts.Agent(ctx, "h2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := agentRow{Version: "1.2.3", OS: "linux", Arch: "arm64", LastSeen: "2026-10-17T12:01:00.000000000Z", Protocol: 1}
	if got := h.agentRow("h2"); got != want {
		t.Errorf("core_hosts = %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
	if got := h.hosts.JournalEpoch("h2"); got != "01EPOCHA" {
		t.Errorf("epoch = %q, want 01EPOCHA", got)
	}

	// A reconnected agent says Hello again; an empty field is stored as NULL.
	hello.BinaryVersion, hello.Arch, hello.JournalEpoch = "1.3.0", "", "01EPOCHB"
	h.clients.Add("h2", &coretest.FakeAgent{HelloFunc: func(context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) { return hello, nil }})
	h.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	if _, err := h.hosts.Agent(ctx, "h2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want = agentRow{Version: "1.3.0", OS: "linux", Arch: "<null>", LastSeen: "2026-10-17T12:02:00.000000000Z", Protocol: 1}
	if got := h.agentRow("h2"); got != want {
		t.Errorf("after a reconnect core_hosts = %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
	if got := h.hosts.JournalEpoch("h2"); got != "01EPOCHB" {
		t.Errorf("epoch after a reconnect = %q, want 01EPOCHB", got)
	}
	var state string
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT state FROM core_hosts WHERE id = 'h2'`).Scan(&state); err != nil || state != core.HostPending {
		t.Errorf("state = %q, %v; Hello must not change it", state, err)
	}
}

var refCoreEcho = core.ActionRef{Name: "core.echo.get", Version: 1}

// TestCoreModuleSkipsEnablement: the core module has no core_module_state
// row, and its agent actions dispatch anyway.
func TestCoreModuleSkipsEnablement(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	echoAction := core.DefineRead(core.ActionSpec{Name: refCoreEcho.Name, Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}, echo{})
	var err error
	if h.reg, err = core.NewRegistry(append(modules(), coreactions.Module(coreactions.Deps{Extra: []core.ActionDef{echoAction}}))...); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.hosts = NewHosts(HostsConfig{DB: h.db, Registry: h.reg, Clients: h.clients, Clock: h.clock, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)})
	h.clients.Add(testHost, &coretest.FakeAgent{
		HelloFunc: h.rt.Hello,
		ReadFunc: func(_ context.Context, _ core.Principal, a core.ActionRef, _ json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error) {
			return json.RawMessage(`{"value":"` + a.Name + `"}`), nil
		},
	})
	d, err := New(h.config(&coretest.MemAudit{}))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refCoreEcho, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"x"}`))
	if err != nil || string(out) != `{"value":"core.echo.get"}` {
		t.Errorf("Read = %s, %v; want the agent's answer", out, err)
	}
	if n := h.count("SELECT count(*) FROM core_module_state WHERE module_id = 'core'"); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("core module rows = %d, want none", n)
	}
}

func TestChangesSkipOfflineHosts(t *testing.T) {
	h, d := newDispatcher(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	logs := &logRecords{}
	d.cfg.Log = slog.New(logs)
	h.applyAgent(d, "a", core.ApplyOptions{})
	h.addHost("h9")
	vs, err := d.Changes(ctx, alice, core.ChangeFilter{})
	if err != nil || len(vs) != 1 || vs[0].HostID != testHost {
		t.Errorf("Changes = %+v, %v; want h1's one change", vs, err)
	}
	if got, want := logs.lines(), []string{"DEBUG dispatch: offline host skipped host=h9"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("logs = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
	_, err = d.Changes(ctx, alice, core.ChangeFilter{HostID: "h9"})
	_ = wantCode(t, err, errcode.HostOffline)
}
```

```go
package dispatch

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/coreactions"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

var refUpdateStatus = core.ActionRef{Name: "core.update.status", Version: 1}

// newWindowHarness is a dispatcher of a controller at version whose registry
// also has an agent-side core.update.status, as the assembled controller's
// does.
func newWindowHarness(t *testing.T, version string) (*harness, *Dispatcher) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newHarness(t)
	status := core.DefineRead(core.ActionSpec{Name: refUpdateStatus.Name, Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}, echo{})
	var err error
	if h.reg, err = core.NewRegistry(append(modules(), coreactions.Module(coreactions.Deps{Extra: []core.ActionDef{status}}))...); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.hosts = NewHosts(HostsConfig{DB: h.db, Registry: h.reg, Clients: h.clients, Clock: h.clock, Log: h.hosts.cfg.Log})
	cfg := h.config(&coretest.MemAudit{})
	cfg.Version = version
	d, err := New(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h, d
}

// agentAt gives h1 a new session whose agent is the runtime reporting
// version and protocol; it answers core.update.status itself, which the
// fixture runtime does not have.
func (h *harness) agentAt(version string, protocol int) {
	h.clients.Add(testHost, &coretest.FakeAgent{
		HelloFunc: func(ctx context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) {
			hello, err := h.rt.Hello(ctx)
			hello.BinaryVersion, hello.ProtocolVersion = version, protocol
			return hello, err
		},
		ReadFunc: func(ctx context.Context, p core.Principal, a core.ActionRef, input json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error) {
			if a == refUpdateStatus {
				return json.RawMessage(`{"transactions":[]}`), nil
			}
			return h.rt.Read(ctx, p, a, input)
		},
		ResourceFunc: h.rt.Resource,
		PlanFunc:     h.rt.Plan,
		ApplyFunc:    h.rt.Apply,
	})
}

func TestVersionWindow(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		controller, agent string
		protocol          int
		supported         bool
	}{
		{"1.3.0", "1.3.0", 1, true},
		{"1.3.0", "1.3.4", 1, true},
		{"1.3.0", "1.2.9", 1, true},
		{"1.3.0", "1.1.0", 1, false},
		{"1.3.0", "1.4.0", 1, false},
		{"1.3.0", "2.3.0", 1, false},
		{"1.3.0", "1.3.0", 2, false},
		{"1.3.0", "dev", 1, false},
		{"dev", "dev", 1, true},
		{"", "0.1.0", 1, true},
	} {
		h, d := newWindowHarness(t, tc.controller)
		h.agentAt(tc.agent, tc.protocol)
		name := tc.agent + " under " + tc.controller
		calls := map[string]func() error{
			"read": func() error {
				_, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refGet, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a"}`))
				return err
			},
			"resource": func() error { _, err := d.Resource(ctx, alice, testHost, "kv.status"); return err },
			"plan": func() error {
				_, err := d.Plan(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a","value":"1"}`))
				return err
			},
		}
		for call, run := range calls {
			err := run()
			switch {
			case tc.supported && err != nil:
				t.Errorf("%s: %s = %v, want it dispatched", name, call, err)
			case !tc.supported:
				e := wantCode(t, err, errcode.HostVersionUnsupported)
				if !strings.Contains(e.Detail, "host h1 runs agent "+tc.agent) {
					t.Errorf("%s: %s detail %q does not name the agent's version", name, call, e.Detail)
				}
			}
		}
		if out, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refUpdateStatus, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"x"}`)); err != nil ||
			string(out) != `{"transactions":[]}` {
			t.Errorf("%s: core.update.status = %s, %v; want it dispatched in every case", name, out, err)
		}
	}
}

// TestApplyChecksTheWindowAgain: an agent replaced by one outside the window
// after the plan cannot apply it.
func TestApplyChecksTheWindowAgain(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d := newWindowHarness(t, "1.3.0")
	h.agentAt("1.3.0", 1)
	pv := h.plan(d, testHost, refSet, `{"name":"a","value":"1"}`)
	h.agentAt("1.4.0", 1)
	_, err := d.Apply(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, pv.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	_ = wantCode(t, err, errcode.HostVersionUnsupported)
	h.agentAt("1.2.0", 1)
	if v, err := d.Apply(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, pv.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{}); err != nil || v.State != core.StatePending {
		t.Errorf("Apply from a previous-minor agent = %+v, %v; want the pending change", v, err)
	}
}
```

```diff
+// updatePrefix starts the names of the only actions an agent outside the

+const updatePrefix = core.ModuleIDCore + ".update."
+
+// inWindow answers host.version_unsupported for name when hostID's agent,
+// as its last Hello describes it, is outside the controller's support
+// window (spec section 5).
+func (d *Dispatcher) inWindow(hostID, name string) error {
+	if d.cfg.Version == "" || strings.HasPrefix(name, updatePrefix) {
+		return nil
+	}

+	}
+	if coreactions.Supported(d.cfg.Version, hello.BinaryVersion, hello.ProtocolVersion) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return errcode.New(errcode.HostVersionUnsupported, fmt.Sprintf(
+		"host %s runs agent %s (protocol %d), outside the support window of controller %s; only core.update actions run there",
+		hostID, hello.BinaryVersion, hello.ProtocolVersion, d.cfg.Version))
+}
```

```go
package dispatch

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"maps"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/schemas"
)

// olderSnapshot is ref's snapshot as a previous minor released it: the
// input of every kv item action had only its name.
func (h *harness) olderSnapshot(ref core.ActionRef) ([]byte, core.SchemaHash) {
	h.t.Helper()
	def, ok := h.reg.Action(ref)
	if !ok {
		h.t.Fatalf("%s is not registered", ref)
	}
	raw, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(def)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var snap map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &snap); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, d := range snap["input"].(map[string]any)["$defs"].(map[string]any) {
		delete(d.(map[string]any)["properties"].(map[string]any), "value")
	}
	older, err := json.Marshal(snap)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hash, err := schemas.SnapshotHashes(older)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return older, hash
}

// newNegotiationHarness is a dispatcher whose released snapshots hold the
// v1.2.0 kv.item.get and kv.item.set, with the hashes of those snapshots.
func newNegotiationHarness(t *testing.T) (*harness, *Dispatcher, map[string]core.SchemaHash) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newHarness(t)
	released := fstest.MapFS{}
	older := map[string]core.SchemaHash{}
	for _, ref := range []core.ActionRef{refGet, refSet} {
		snap, hash := h.olderSnapshot(ref)
		released["released/v1.2.0/"+ref.Name+"/v1.json"] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: snap}
		older[schemas.HashKey(ref)] = hash
	}
	cfg := h.config(&coretest.MemAudit{})
	cfg.Snapshots = released
	d, err := New(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h, d, older
}

// agentWith gives h1 a new session whose agent is the runtime reporting the
// schema hashes edit leaves of its own.
func (h *harness) agentWith(edit func(map[string]core.SchemaHash)) {
	h.clients.Add(testHost, &coretest.FakeAgent{
		HelloFunc: func(ctx context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) {
			hello, err := h.rt.Hello(ctx)
			hello.SchemaHashes = maps.Clone(hello.SchemaHashes)
			edit(hello.SchemaHashes)
			return hello, err
		},
		ReadFunc: h.rt.Read, ResourceFunc: h.rt.Resource, PlanFunc: h.rt.Plan, ApplyFunc: h.rt.Apply,
	})
}

func TestSchemaNegotiationWithAPreviousMinor(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d, older := newNegotiationHarness(t)
	withValue := json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a","value":"1"}`)
	if _, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refGet, withValue); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read from an agent of this binary: %v", err)
	}

	h.agentWith(func(m map[string]core.SchemaHash) { maps.Copy(m, older) })
	if out, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refGet, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a"}`)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Read within the older schema = %s, %v; want it dispatched", out, err)
	}
	if _, err := d.Plan(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a"}`)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Plan within the older schema: %v; want it dispatched", err)
	}
	for name, call := range map[string]func() error{
		"read": func() error { _, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refGet, withValue); return err },
		"plan": func() error { _, err := d.Plan(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, withValue); return err },
	} {
		e := wantCode(t, call(), errcode.InvalidInput)
		if len(e.Fields) != 1 || e.Fields[0].Path != "/value" || !strings.Contains(e.Detail, "no field /value") {
			t.Errorf("%s: %+v, want the field /value the agent's schema lacks", name, e)
		}
	}
}

func TestSchemaNegotiationRefusesAnUnknownSchema(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d, _ := newNegotiationHarness(t)
	for name, edit := range map[string]func(map[string]core.SchemaHash){
		"unknown hash": func(m map[string]core.SchemaHash) {
			m[schemas.HashKey(refGet)] = core.SchemaHash{Input: strings.Repeat("0", 64), Output: strings.Repeat("0", 64)}
		},
		"no hash": func(m map[string]core.SchemaHash) { delete(m, schemas.HashKey(refGet)) },
	} {
		h.agentWith(edit)
		_, err := d.Read(ctx, alice, testHost, refGet, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"a"}`))
		e := wantCode(t, err, errcode.ModuleUnavailable)
		if want := "action kv.item.get/v1 is unavailable on host h1: " + ReasonSchemaUnknown; e.Detail != want {
			t.Errorf("%s: detail %q, want %q", name, e.Detail, want)
		}
		if _, err := d.Resource(ctx, alice, testHost, "kv.status"); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: resources carry no schema hash and must still dispatch: %v", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestApplyChecksTheSchemaAgain(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d, _ := newNegotiationHarness(t)
	pv := h.plan(d, testHost, refSet, `{"name":"a","value":"1"}`)
	h.agentWith(func(m map[string]core.SchemaHash) { delete(m, schemas.HashKey(refSet)) })
	_, err := d.Apply(ctx, alice, testHost, refSet, pv.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	_ = wantCode(t, err, errcode.ModuleUnavailable)
}

func TestNewRefusesABrokenReleasedSnapshot(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	cfg := h.config(&coretest.MemAudit{})
	cfg.Snapshots = fstest.MapFS{"released/v1.2.0/kv.item.get/v1.json": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`{"input":`)}}
	if _, err := New(cfg); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "released/v1.2.0/kv.item.get/v1.json") {
		t.Errorf("New = %v, want an error naming the broken snapshot", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/controller/dispatch/negotiate.go`:

```go
package dispatch

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"slices"
	"strings"

	"github.com/santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6"
	"github.com/santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6/kind"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/schemas"
)

// ReasonSchemaUnknown is why an action version is unavailable on a host
// whose agent reports a schema hash this controller cannot map to a schema
// (spec section 5).
const ReasonSchemaUnknown = "agent schema unknown"

// negotiation is what the schema-hash rule compares an agent's Hello with.
type negotiation struct {
	current  map[string]core.SchemaHash                        // this binary's agent actions, by schemas.HashKey
	released map[string]map[core.SchemaHash]*jsonschema.Schema // released snapshots' compiled input schemas, by key and hashes
}

// newNegotiation hashes reg's agent actions and compiles the input schema of
// every released action snapshot in snapshots.
func newNegotiation(reg *core.Registry, snapshots fs.FS) (negotiation, error) {
	current, err := schemas.ActionHashes(reg, core.SideAgent)
	if err != nil {
		return negotiation{}, err
	}
	n := negotiation{current: current, released: map[string]map[core.SchemaHash]*jsonschema.Schema{}}
	err = fs.WalkDir(snapshots, "released", func(name string, e fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		// released/<tag>/<action>/v<n>.json
		parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
		if e.IsDir() || len(parts) != 4 || path.Ext(name) != ".json" {
			return nil
		}
		version, err := core.ParseVersion(strings.TrimSuffix(parts[3], ".json"))
		if err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		return n.add(snapshots, name, core.ActionRef{Name: parts[2], Version: version})
	})
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return negotiation{}, err
	}
	return n, nil
}

// add compiles the input schema of the released snapshot at name. A resource
// snapshot has no input and names no action, so it is skipped.
func (n negotiation) add(snapshots fs.FS, name string, ref core.ActionRef) error {
	raw, err := fs.ReadFile(snapshots, name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var snap struct {
		Input json.RawMessage `json:"input"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &snap); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: snapshot %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	if snap.Input == nil {
		return nil
	}
	hash, err := schemas.SnapshotHashes(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: snapshot %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	doc, err := jsonschema.UnmarshalJSON(bytes.NewReader(snap.Input))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: snapshot %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	closeObjects(doc)
	c := jsonschema.NewCompiler()
	c.AssertFormat()
	url := "urn:darkpanel:" + name
	if err := c.AddResource(url, doc); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: snapshot %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	sch, err := c.Compile(url)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: compile snapshot %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	key := schemas.HashKey(ref)
	if n.released[key] == nil {
		n.released[key] = map[core.SchemaHash]*jsonschema.Schema{}
	}
	n.released[key][hash] = sch
	return nil
}

// closeObjects refuses unknown properties in every object schema of doc that
// does not decide that itself, so validation against an older schema names
// every field the older agent lacks.
func closeObjects(doc any) {
	switch v := doc.(type) {
	case map[string]any:
		if _, ok := v["properties"]; ok {
			if _, set := v["additionalProperties"]; !set {
				v["additionalProperties"] = false
			}
		}
		for _, child := range v {
			closeObjects(child)
		}
	case []any:
		for _, child := range v {
			closeObjects(child)
		}
	}
}

// negotiate applies the schema-hash rule to def on hostID, as its last Hello
// describes the agent (spec section 5): an equal hash dispatches; a hash of a
// released snapshot of def dispatches unless input has a field that
// snapshot's schema lacks; any other hash, or none, leaves def unavailable
// there. A nil input, as an apply has, is not validated.
func (d *Dispatcher) negotiate(hostID string, def core.ActionDef, input json.RawMessage) error {
	hello, ok := d.cfg.Hosts.greetingOf(hostID)
	if !ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("dispatch: host %s has not said hello", hostID)
	}
	key := schemas.HashKey(def.Ref())
	got, reported := hello.SchemaHashes[key]
	if reported && got == d.neg.current[key] {
		return nil
	}
	if older, found := d.neg.released[key][got]; reported && found {
		if input == nil {
			return nil
		}
		return unknownField(hostID, def.Ref(), older, input)
	}
	return errcode.New(errcode.ModuleUnavailable,
		fmt.Sprintf("action %s is unavailable on host %s: %s", def.Ref(), hostID, ReasonSchemaUnknown))
}

// unknownField validates input against an older agent's input schema and
// answers request.invalid_input naming the first field, in JSON pointer
// order, that the schema lacks. Every other mismatch is left to the agent,
// whose own validation names each failing field.
func unknownField(hostID string, ref core.ActionRef, older *jsonschema.Schema, input json.RawMessage) error {
	inst, err := jsonschema.UnmarshalJSON(bytes.NewReader(input))
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}
	var verr *jsonschema.ValidationError
	if !errors.As(older.Validate(inst), &verr) {
		return nil
	}
	fields := unknownPaths(verr, nil)
	if len(fields) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	slices.Sort(fields)
	return &errcode.Error{
		Code:   errcode.InvalidInput,
		Detail: fmt.Sprintf("host %s runs an older %s whose input has no field %s", hostID, ref, fields[0]),
		Fields: []errcode.FieldError{{Path: fields[0], Message: "unknown to the agent's version of the action"}},
	}
}

func unknownPaths(e *jsonschema.ValidationError, out []string) []string {
	for _, c := range e.Causes {
		out = unknownPaths(c, out)
	}
	if k, ok := e.ErrorKind.(*kind.AdditionalProperties); ok {
		at := jsonPointer(e.InstanceLocation)
		for _, p := range k.Properties {
			out = append(out, at+"/"+escapePointer(p))
		}
	}
	return out
}

func jsonPointer(tokens []string) string {
	var sb strings.Builder
	for _, tok := range tokens {
		sb.WriteString("/" + escapePointer(tok))
	}
	return sb.String()
}

func escapePointer(tok string) string {
	return strings.ReplaceAll(strings.ReplaceAll(tok, "~", "~0"), "/", "~1")
}
```
