```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// testStart is when every rollout test's clock starts: a Monday.
var testStart = time.Date(2026, 10, 19, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

// openDB returns a migrated controller database in a temporary directory.
func openDB(t *testing.T) *store.DB {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	db, err := store.Open(ctx, filepath.Join(dir, "controller.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = db.Close() })
	set, err := store.CoreMigrations(store.SideController)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	opts := store.MigrateOptions{
		Side: store.SideController, BinaryVersion: "test", BackupRoot: filepath.Join(dir, "backups"), Keep: 5,
		Now: testStart,
	}
	if _, err := store.Migrate(ctx, db, opts, set, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return db
}

// exec runs one statement in a write transaction of its own.
func exec(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, query string, args ...any) {
	t.Helper()
	err := db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.Exec(query, args...)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", query, err)
	}
}

// addHost adds an enrolled host whose agent runs version; local marks the
// controller's own host.
func addHost(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, id string, local bool, version string) {
	t.Helper()
	exec(t, db, `INSERT INTO core_hosts (id, name, is_local, created_at, state, agent_version)
		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'enrolled', ?)`, id, "host-"+id, local, testStart.Format(timeLayout), version)
}

// setHostState sets a host's enrollment state.
func setHostState(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, id, state string) {
	t.Helper()
	exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_hosts SET state = ? WHERE id = ?", state, id)
}

// createRollout runs Create in a write transaction of its own.
func createRollout(db *store.DB, in core.RolloutInput, controllerVersion string) (core.Rollout, error) {
	var r core.Rollout
	err := db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		r, err = Create(context.Background(), tx, CreateParams{
			Input: in, ControllerVersion: controllerVersion, Now: testStart,
		})
		return err
	})
	return r, err
}

// mustCreate creates a rollout of version to hosts on a controller running
// controllerVersion.
func mustCreate(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, version, controllerVersion string, hosts ...string) core.Rollout {
	t.Helper()
	r, err := createRollout(db, core.RolloutInput{Hosts: hosts, Version: version, Channel: core.ChannelStable},
		controllerVersion)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r
}

// wantCode fails unless err carries code.
func wantCode(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) {
	t.Helper()
	var e *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &e) || e.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
}

// hostOrder lists a rollout's host ids in position order.
func hostOrder(r core.Rollout) []string {
	var ids []string
	for _, h := range r.Hosts {
		ids = append(ids, h.HostID)
	}
	return ids
}

func TestRolloutMigration(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	at := testStart.Format(timeLayout)
	insert := `INSERT INTO core_rollouts (id, version, channel, state, outcome, created_at, updated_at)
		VALUES (?, '1.3.0', 'stable', ?, ?, ?, ?)`
	exec(t, db, insert, "R1", core.RolloutSucceeded, nil, at, at)
	exec(t, db, insert, "R2", core.RolloutFailed, core.RolloutFailed, at, at)
	exec(t, db, insert, "R3", core.RolloutRunning, nil, at, at)
	refused := func(what, query string, args ...any) {
		t.Helper()
		err := db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
			_, err := tx.Exec(query, args...)
			return err
		})
		if err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s was accepted", what)
		}
	}
	refused("a second running rollout", insert, "R4", core.RolloutRunning, nil, at, at)
	refused("a draining rollout beside a running one", insert, "R4", core.RolloutDraining, core.RolloutCancelled, at, at)
	refused("an unknown state", insert, "R4", "paused", nil, at, at)
	exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'cancelled' WHERE id = 'R3'")
	refused("a draining rollout without an outcome", insert, "R4", core.RolloutDraining, nil, at, at)
	exec(t, db, insert, "R4", core.RolloutDraining, core.RolloutCancelled, at, at)

	hostInsert := `INSERT INTO core_rollout_hosts (rollout_id, host_id, position, state) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)`
	exec(t, db, hostInsert, "R4", "a", 0, core.RolloutHostUpdating)
	refused("a second updating host", hostInsert, "R4", "b", 1, core.RolloutHostUpdating)
	refused("a repeated position", hostInsert, "R4", "b", 0, core.RolloutHostWaiting)
	refused("an unknown host state", hostInsert, "R4", "b", 1, "paused")
	refused("a host of no rollout", hostInsert, "R9", "b", 0, core.RolloutHostWaiting)
	exec(t, db, hostInsert, "R4", "b", 1, core.RolloutHostWaiting)
}

func TestCreate(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "B", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	var r core.Rollout
	err := db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		r, err = Create(context.Background(), tx, CreateParams{
			Input:             core.RolloutInput{Hosts: []string{"B", "OWN", "A"}, Version: "1.3.0", Channel: core.ChannelBeta},
			ControllerVersion: "1.2.0", ChangeID: "CHANGE1", Now: testStart,
		})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r.ID == "" || r.Version != "1.3.0" || r.Channel != core.ChannelBeta || r.State != core.RolloutRunning ||
		r.Error != nil || !r.CreatedAt.Equal(testStart) || !r.UpdatedAt.Equal(testStart) {
		t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", r)
	}
	if got := hostOrder(r); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"OWN", "B", "A"}) {
		t.Errorf("hosts = %v, want the own host first, then the given order", got)
	}
	for i, h := range r.Hosts {
		if h.Position != i || h.State != core.RolloutHostWaiting || h.PlanID != nil || h.OperationID != nil || h.Error != nil {
			t.Errorf("host %d = %+v", i, h)
		}
	}
	rec, err := load(context.Background(), db.Read(), r.ID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if rec.ChangeID != "CHANGE1" || rec.Outcome != "" {
		t.Errorf("record = %+v", rec)
	}
	if _, err := load(context.Background(), db.Read(), "MISSING"); err == nil {
		t.Error("load found a missing rollout")
	} else {
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
	}
}

func TestCreateRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "P", false, "")
	setHostState(t, db, "P", core.HostPending)
	addHost(t, db, "R", false, "1.2.0")
	setHostState(t, db, "R", core.HostRevoked)
	tests := []struct {
		name       string
		hosts      []string
		version    string
		channel    string
		controller string
		code       errcode.Code // "" when the rollout is created
	}{
		{"no hosts", nil, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"a host twice", []string{"A", "A"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"an unknown host", []string{"A", "X"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.NotFound},
		{"a pending host", []string{"P"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"a revoked host", []string{"R"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"a malformed version", []string{"A"}, "v1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"an unknown channel", []string{"A"}, "1.3.0", "nightly", "1.3.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"ahead of the controller", []string{"A"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.2.0", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"ahead of a development controller", []string{"A"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "dev", errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"ahead of the controller with the own host", []string{"A", "OWN"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.2.0", ""},
		{"the controller's version", []string{"A"}, "1.3.0", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", ""},
		{"behind the controller", []string{"A"}, "1.2.5", core.ChannelStable, "1.3.0", ""},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			r, err := createRollout(db, core.RolloutInput{Hosts: tt.hosts, Version: tt.version, Channel: tt.channel}, tt.controller)
			if tt.code != "" {
				wantCode(t, err, tt.code)
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'succeeded' WHERE id = ?", r.ID)
		})
	}
}

func TestCreateRefusesASecondActiveRollout(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	errs := make([]error, 4)
	for i := range errs {
		wg.Go(func() {
			_, errs[i] = createRollout(db, core.RolloutInput{Hosts: []string{"OWN", "A"}, Version: "1.3.0",
				Channel: core.ChannelStable}, "1.2.0")
		})
	}
	wg.Wait()
	created := 0
	for _, err := range errs {
		if err == nil {
			created++
			continue
		}
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.RolloutInProgress)
	}
	if created != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d concurrent rollouts were created, want 1", created)
	}
	r, ok, err := active(context.Background(), db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("active = %v, %v", ok, err)
	}
	exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'draining', outcome = 'cancelled' WHERE id = ?", r.ID)
	_, err = createRollout(db, core.RolloutInput{Hosts: []string{"A"}, Version: "1.2.0", Channel: core.ChannelStable}, "1.2.0")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.RolloutInProgress)
	exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'cancelled' WHERE id = ?", r.ID)
	mustCreate(t, db, "1.2.0", "1.2.0", "A")
}

func TestOlderThan(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		current, target string
		want            bool
	}{
		{"1.2.0", "1.3.0", true},
		{"1.3.0", "1.3.0", false},
		{"1.3.1", "1.3.0", false},
		{"1.3.0-beta.1", "1.3.0", true},
		{"dev", "1.3.0", true},
		{"dev", "dev", false},
		{"1.3.0", "dev", false},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		if got := olderThan(tt.current, tt.target); got != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("olderThan(%q, %q) = %v, want %v", tt.current, tt.target, got, tt.want)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/core/store/migrations/controller/0007_rollout.sql`:

```sql
-- Fleet rollouts (M2 spec section 8, Fleet rollout), read and written only
-- through internal/controller/rollout. Times are fixed-width RFC 3339 UTC
-- text, so they compare as text.

-- outcome is the state a draining rollout ends in once its host's update is
-- over: failed after a timeout, cancelled after core.update.rollout-cancel.
-- change_id is the core.update.rollout change that created the rollout; a
-- scheduled rollout is not a change and has none.
CREATE TABLE core_rollouts (
    id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    version       TEXT NOT NULL,
    channel       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('stable', 'beta')),
    state         TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (state IN ('running', 'draining', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    outcome       TEXT CHECK (outcome IN ('failed', 'cancelled')),
    error_code    TEXT,
    error_message TEXT,
    change_id     TEXT,
    created_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
    CHECK (state <> 'draining' OR outcome IS NOT NULL)
) STRICT;

-- Every active rollout has the same index key, so the database itself
-- refuses a second one (rollout.in_progress).
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX core_rollouts_one_active ON core_rollouts ((1))
    WHERE state IN ('running', 'draining');
CREATE INDEX core_rollouts_created ON core_rollouts (created_at);

-- The hosts of a rollout, updated one at a time in position order.
-- followed_at is when the driver stored the host's update operation and
-- began polling it, for the ten-minute limit.
-- host_id has no foreign key, so a finished rollout keeps naming a host
-- that was deleted later.
CREATE TABLE core_rollout_hosts (
    rollout_id    TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES core_rollouts (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    host_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    position      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (position >= 0),
    state         TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (state IN ('waiting', 'updating', 'committed', 'failed', 'skipped')),
    plan_id       TEXT,
    operation_id  TEXT,
    error_code    TEXT,
    error_message TEXT,
    followed_at   TEXT,
    PRIMARY KEY (rollout_id, host_id),
    UNIQUE (rollout_id, position)
) STRICT;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX core_rollout_hosts_one_updating ON core_rollout_hosts (rollout_id)
    WHERE state = 'updating';
```

Create `internal/controller/rollout/store.go`:

```go
// Package rollout updates hosts one at a time (spec section 8, Fleet rollout
// and Channels and schedule): the rollout tables, the controller-side
// rollout actions, the driver that applies core.update.apply on each host in
// turn, and the scheduler that starts a one-host rollout in a host's update
// window.
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

// timeLayout is the fixed-width RFC 3339 UTC layout of every controller
// table, so stored times compare as text.
const timeLayout = "2006-01-02T15:04:05.000000000Z"

// querier reads from the database's reader pool or inside a write
// transaction.
type querier interface {
	QueryContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*sql.Rows, error)
	QueryRowContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) *sql.Row
}

// CreateParams describe a new rollout.
type CreateParams struct {
	Input             core.RolloutInput
	ControllerVersion string // the controller's buildinfo.Version
	ChangeID          string // the core.update.rollout change; empty for a scheduled rollout
	Now               time.Time
}

// Create checks p and inserts a running rollout with its hosts inside tx:
// the controller's own host first when it is listed, the others in the order
// given, all waiting. Every host must exist and be enrolled. A target newer
// than the controller's version is refused unless the controller's own host
// is listed, so the controller is never older than an agent it manages. A
// second active rollout is rollout.in_progress.
func Create(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, p CreateParams) (core.Rollout, error) {
	hosts, err := check(ctx, tx, p.Input, p.ControllerVersion)
	if err != nil {
		return core.Rollout{}, err
	}
	now := p.Now.UTC()
	id := ulid.MustNew(ulid.Timestamp(now), rand.Reader).String()
	at := now.Format(timeLayout)
	var change any
	if p.ChangeID != "" {
		change = p.ChangeID
	}
	if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
		`INSERT INTO core_rollouts (id, version, channel, state, change_id, created_at, updated_at)
		 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
		id, p.Input.Version, p.Input.Channel, core.RolloutRunning, change, at, at); err != nil {
		return core.Rollout{}, fmt.Errorf("rollout: insert rollout: %w", err)
	}
	for i, h := range hosts {
		if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
			"INSERT INTO core_rollout_hosts (rollout_id, host_id, position, state) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
			id, h, i, core.RolloutHostWaiting); err != nil {
			return core.Rollout{}, fmt.Errorf("rollout: insert host %s: %w", h, err)
		}
	}
	r, err := load(ctx, tx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return core.Rollout{}, err
	}
	return r.view(), nil
}

// check validates a rollout of in and returns its hosts in update order.
func check(ctx context.Context, q querier, in core.RolloutInput, controllerVersion string) ([]string, error) {
	if _, err := update.ParseVersion(in.Version); err != nil {
		return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf("version %q is not a semantic version", in.Version))
	}
	if in.Channel != core.ChannelStable && in.Channel != core.ChannelBeta {
		return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf("unknown channel %q", in.Channel))
	}
	if len(in.Hosts) == 0 {
		return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, "a rollout needs at least one host")
	}
	var own string
	err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM core_hosts WHERE is_local = 1").Scan(&own)
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the controller's host: %w", err)
	}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	var ordered []string
	for _, h := range in.Hosts {
		if seen[h] {
			return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf("host %s is listed twice", h))
		}
		seen[h] = true
		var state string
		err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT state FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?", h).Scan(&state)
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
			return nil, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no host "+h)
		case err != nil:
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read host %s: %w", h, err)
		case state != core.HostEnrolled:
			return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf("host %s is %s; only an enrolled host can be updated", h, state))
		}
		if h == own {
			ordered = append([]string{h}, ordered...)
		} else {
			ordered = append(ordered, h)
		}
	}
	if (own == "" || !seen[own]) && olderThan(controllerVersion, in.Version) {
		return nil, errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf(
			"the controller runs %s, older than %s; list the controller's own host, which updates first",
			controllerVersion, in.Version))
	}
	var activeID, state string
	err = q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id, state FROM core_rollouts WHERE state IN (?, ?)",
		core.RolloutRunning, core.RolloutDraining).Scan(&activeID, &state)
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		return nil, errcode.New(errcode.RolloutInProgress, fmt.Sprintf("rollout %s is still %s", activeID, state))
	case !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the active rollout: %w", err)
	}
	return ordered, nil
}

// olderThan reports whether version current is older than target. A version
// that does not parse, such as a development build's "dev", equals only the
// same string and otherwise counts as older, so a controller whose version
// is unknown never lets another host get ahead of it.
func olderThan(current, target string) bool {
	if current == target {
		return false
	}
	c, err := update.ParseVersion(current)
	if err != nil {
		return true
	}
	t, err := update.ParseVersion(target)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	return c.Compare(t) < 0
}

// hostRecord is a rollout host with the column the API does not show.
type hostRecord struct {
	core.RolloutHost
	FollowedAt time.Time // when the host's update operation was stored; zero before
}

// record is a rollout with the columns the API does not show.
type record struct {
	ID, Version, Channel, State string
	Outcome                     string // the state a draining rollout ends in
	ChangeID                    string // the creating change; empty for a scheduled rollout
	Error                       *core.ErrorDetail
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt        time.Time
	Hosts                       []hostRecord
}

// view is the rollout as the actions return it.
func (r record) view() core.Rollout {
	v := core.Rollout{
		ID: r.ID, Version: r.Version, Channel: r.Channel, State: r.State, Error: r.Error,
		CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt, Hosts: make([]core.RolloutHost, 0, len(r.Hosts)),
	}
	for _, h := range r.Hosts {
		v.Hosts = append(v.Hosts, h.RolloutHost)
	}
	return v
}

const rolloutColumns = `id, version, channel, state, coalesce(outcome, ''), error_code, error_message,
	coalesce(change_id, ''), created_at, updated_at`

type scanner interface {
	Scan(dest ...any) error
}

func scanRollout(s scanner) (record, error) {
	var r record
	var code, message sql.NullString
	var created, updated string
	if err := s.Scan(&r.ID, &r.Version, &r.Channel, &r.State, &r.Outcome, &code, &message, &r.ChangeID,
		&created, &updated); err != nil {
		return r, err
	}
	r.Error = errorDetail(code, message)
	var err error
	if r.CreatedAt, err = time.Parse(timeLayout, created); err != nil {
		return r, fmt.Errorf("rollout: rollout %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	if r.UpdatedAt, err = time.Parse(timeLayout, updated); err != nil {
		return r, fmt.Errorf("rollout: rollout %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	return r, nil
}

// errorDetail is the error stored in a code and a message column.
func errorDetail(code, message sql.NullString) *core.ErrorDetail {
	if !code.Valid {
		return nil
	}
	return &core.ErrorDetail{Code: code.String, Message: message.String}
}

// load reads rollout id with its hosts; a missing rollout is not_found.
func load(ctx context.Context, q querier, id string) (record, error) {
	r, err := scanRollout(q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+rolloutColumns+" FROM core_rollouts WHERE id = ?", id))
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return r, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no rollout "+id)
	case err != nil:
		return r, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read rollout %s: %w", id, err)
	}
	return r, loadHosts(ctx, q, &r)
}

// active reads the running or draining rollout, if there is one.
func active(ctx context.Context, q querier) (record, bool, error) {
	r, err := scanRollout(q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+rolloutColumns+" FROM core_rollouts WHERE state IN (?, ?)",
		core.RolloutRunning, core.RolloutDraining))
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return r, false, nil
	case err != nil:
		return r, false, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the active rollout: %w", err)
	}
	return r, true, loadHosts(ctx, q, &r)
}

// loadHosts reads r's hosts in position order.
func loadHosts(ctx context.Context, q querier, r *record) error {
	rows, err := q.QueryContext(ctx,
		`SELECT host_id, position, state, plan_id, operation_id, error_code, error_message, followed_at
		 FROM core_rollout_hosts WHERE rollout_id = ? ORDER BY position`, r.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the hosts of %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	r.Hosts = nil
	for rows.Next() {
		var h hostRecord
		var plan, op, code, message, followed sql.NullString
		if err := rows.Scan(&h.HostID, &h.Position, &h.State, &plan, &op, &code, &message, &followed); err != nil {
			_ = rows.Close()
			return fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the hosts of %s: %w", r.ID, err)
		}
		if plan.Valid {
			h.PlanID = &plan.String
		}
		if op.Valid {
			h.OperationID = &op.String
		}
		h.Error = errorDetail(code, message)
		if followed.Valid {
			if h.FollowedAt, err = time.Parse(timeLayout, followed.String); err != nil {
				_ = rows.Close()
				return fmt.Errorf("rollout: host %s of %s: %w", h.HostID, r.ID, err)
			}
		}
		r.Hosts = append(r.Hosts, h)
	}
	if err := errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the hosts of %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"reflect"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// fakeSessions records the hosts whose live sessions were closed and, with
// db set, the state each host had in the committed database at that moment.
type fakeSessions struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	hosts []string
	db    *store.DB
	seen  []string // "deleted" for a host without a row
}

func (f *fakeSessions) Disconnect(hostID string) {
	state := ""
	if f.db != nil {
		err := f.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT state FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, hostID).Scan(&state)
		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			state = "deleted"
		}
	}
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.hosts = append(f.hosts, hostID)
	f.seen = append(f.seen, state)
}

func (f *fakeSessions) committed() []string {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), f.seen...)
}

func (f *fakeSessions) closed() []string {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), f.hosts...)
}

// newHostEnv is a test env whose core module closes sessions through the
// returned fake, which reads the host's committed state, with the local host
// h1 named alpha.
func newHostEnv(t *testing.T) (*testEnv, *fakeSessions) {
	t.Helper()
	s := &fakeSessions{}
	e := newTestEnvWith(t, func(e *testEnv, d *Deps) { s.db, d.Sessions = e.db, s }, fakeModule{"demo", 1})
	e.addHost("h1", "alpha", true)
	return e, s
}

func (e *testEnv) setHostState(hostID, state string) {
	e.t.Helper()
	e.exec(`UPDATE core_hosts SET state = ? WHERE id = ?`, state, hostID)
}

func (e *testEnv) addCert(hostID, serial string) {
	e.t.Helper()
	e.exec(`INSERT INTO core_agent_certs (serial, host_id, not_before, not_after, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
		serial, hostID, formatTime(t0), formatTime(t0.Add(365*24*time.Hour)), formatTime(t0))
}

func (e *testEnv) count(query string, args ...any) int {
	e.t.Helper()
	var n int
	if err := e.db.Read().QueryRow(query, args...).Scan(&n); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return n
}

// applyDirect calls name's Apply handler without the engine, as if the
// state changed after the engine's recheck.
func (e *testEnv) applyDirect(name string, in any) error {
	e.t.Helper()
	def, ok := e.reg.Action(ref(name))
	if !ok {
		e.t.Fatalf("%s is not registered", name)
	}
	env := core.Env{DB: e.db, Clock: e.clock, Agents: e.hosts, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), Principal: operator}
	_, err := def.Handler.Apply(context.Background(), env, encodeInput(e.t, in))
	return err
}

func TestHostRevoke(t *testing.T) {
	e, sessions := newHostEnv(t)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	e.setHostState("h2", core.HostEnrolled)
	e.addCert("h2", "a1")
	e.addCert("h1", "b1")
	e.exec(`INSERT INTO core_enroll_tokens (host_id, hash, expires_at, created_at) VALUES ('h2', x'00', ?, ?)`,
		formatTime(t0.Add(time.Hour)), formatTime(t0))
	in := core.HostIDInput{HostID: "h2"}

	if err := e.verify("core.host.revoke", in); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed for an enrolled host")
	}
	pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.revoke", in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if pv.Confirmation != "web1" {
		t.Errorf("confirmation = %q, want the host name web1", pv.Confirmation)
	}
	_, err = e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, "")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanConfirmationNeeded)
	v, err := e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, "web1")
	if err != nil || v.State != core.StateCompleted {
		t.Fatalf("apply: %+v, %v", v, err)
	}
	var got core.Host
	if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.ID != "h2" || got.State != core.HostRevoked || got.Online {
		t.Errorf("output = %+v, want h2 revoked and offline", got)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_agent_certs WHERE host_id = 'h2' AND revoked_at IS NOT NULL`); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("revoked certificates of h2 = %d, want 1", n)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_agent_certs WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("unrevoked certificates = %d, want the local host's one", n)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_enroll_tokens`); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("enrollment tokens = %d, want none", n)
	}
	if got := sessions.closed(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"h2"}) {
		t.Errorf("closed sessions = %v, want [h2]", got)
	}
	// The agent listener re-checks the host when the agent redials, so the
	// session closes only once the revocation is committed.
	if got := sessions.committed(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{core.HostRevoked}) {
		t.Errorf("committed state at Disconnect = %v, want [revoked]", got)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.revoke", in); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify after the revocation: %v", err)
	}

	_, err = e.plan(operator, "core.host.revoke", core.HostIDInput{HostID: "nope"})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
}

func TestHostDelete(t *testing.T) {
	e, sessions := newHostEnv(t)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	e.setModuleState("h2", "demo", true, true, "")
	e.addCert("h2", "a1")
	e.exec(`INSERT INTO core_host_update (host_id, channel) VALUES ('h2', 'beta')`)
	in := core.HostIDInput{HostID: "h2"}

	if err := e.verify("core.host.delete", in); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed while the host exists")
	}
	pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.delete", in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if pv.Confirmation != "web1" {
		t.Errorf("confirmation = %q, want the host name web1", pv.Confirmation)
	}
	v, err := e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, "web1")
	if err != nil || v.State != core.StateCompleted {
		t.Fatalf("apply: %+v, %v", v, err)
	}
	var got core.Host
	if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.ID != "h2" || got.Name != "web1" || got.State != core.HostPending || got.Online {
		t.Errorf("output = %+v, want the deleted pending host web1, offline", got)
	}
	for _, table := range []string{"core_hosts WHERE id", "core_module_state WHERE host_id", "core_agent_certs WHERE host_id",
		"core_host_update WHERE host_id"} {
		if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM ` + table + ` = 'h2'`); n != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s = 'h2': %d rows left", table, n)
		}
	}
	if got := sessions.closed(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"h2"}) {
		t.Errorf("closed sessions = %v, want [h2]", got)
	}
	if got := sessions.committed(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"deleted"}) {
		t.Errorf("committed state at Disconnect = %v, want [deleted]", got)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.delete", in); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify after the deletion: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestHostDeleteRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	e, sessions := newHostEnv(t)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	e.setHostState("h2", core.HostEnrolled)
	e.addHost("h3", "web2", false)
	e.setHostState("h3", core.HostRevoked)

	for _, id := range []string{"h1", "h2"} {
		pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.delete", core.HostIDInput{HostID: id})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		_, err = e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, pv.Confirmation)
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanGuardFailed)
	}

	// A host enrolled again between the engine's recheck and the delete.
	e.setHostState("h3", core.HostEnrolled)
	wantCode(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.delete", core.HostIDInput{HostID: "h3"}), errcode.PlanStateChanged)
	wantCode(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.delete", core.HostIDInput{HostID: "h1"}), errcode.PlanStateChanged)
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_hosts`); n != 3 {
		t.Errorf("hosts = %d, want all 3 kept", n)
	}
	if got := sessions.closed(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("closed sessions = %v, want none", got)
	}
	_, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.delete", core.HostIDInput{HostID: "nope"})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
}

func TestHostRevokeWithoutSessions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTestEnv(t)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	e.hosts["h2"] = &coretest.FakeAgent{}
	e.run(operator, "core.host.revoke", core.HostIDInput{HostID: "h2"}, nil)
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_hosts WHERE state = 'revoked'`); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("revoked hosts = %d, want 1", n)
	}
}
```

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

func (m module) hostRevokeActions() []core.ActionDef {
	return []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineMutating[core.HostIDInput, core.Host](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.revoke", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideController,
			Summary: "Revoke a host's agent certificates and enrollment token and close its live session; the confirmation phrase is the host name.",
		}, hostRevoke{m}),
		core.DefineMutating[core.HostIDInput, core.Host](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.delete", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideController,
			Summary: "Delete a pending or revoked host other than the local one; the confirmation phrase is the host name.",
		}, hostDelete{m}),
	}
}

// hostRecord is the part of a core_hosts row the host state rules read.
type hostRecord struct {
	name  string
	state string
	local bool
}

// readHost returns hostID's record, or not_found.
func readHost(ctx context.Context, q querier, hostID string) (hostRecord, error) {
	var r hostRecord
	err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT name, state, is_local FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, hostID).
		Scan(&r.name, &r.state, &r.local)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return hostRecord{}, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, fmt.Sprintf("no host %s", hostID))
	case err != nil:
		return hostRecord{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read host: %w", err)
	}
	return r, nil
}

// hostView returns hostID as core.host.list shows it, or not_found.
func (m module) hostView(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string) (core.Host, error) {
	q := env.DB.Read()
	h, err := scanHost(q.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT `+hostColumns+` FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, hostID))
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return core.Host{}, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, fmt.Sprintf("no host %s", hostID))
	case err != nil:
		return core.Host{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read host: %w", err)
	}
	if err := m.complete(ctx, env, q, &h); err != nil {
		return core.Host{}, err
	}
	return h, nil
}

// disconnect closes hostID's live session once its revocation or deletion
// has committed, so the agent's next dial meets the new state.
func (m module) disconnect(hostID string) {
	if m.d.Sessions != nil {
		m.d.Sessions.Disconnect(hostID)
	}
}

type hostRevoke struct{ m module }

func (h hostRevoke) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	r, err := readHost(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	subject := fmt.Sprintf("host %s (%s)", r.name, in.HostID)
	diff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: subject + ": " + r.state},
		{Op: "+", Text: subject + ": revoked; its certificates and enrollment token are revoked and its live session closes"},
	}
	if r.state == core.HostRevoked {
		diff = []core.DiffLine{{Op: " ", Text: subject + ": already revoked; any live session closes"}}
	}
	diff = append(diff, core.DiffLine{Op: " ", Text: "core.host.token issues the host a new enrollment token"})
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: diff, Guards: []core.Guard{},
		StateHash:    stateHash(in.HostID, r.state),
		Confirmation: r.name,
	}, nil
}

func (h hostRevoke) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput) (core.Host, error) {
	err := env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return fleet.RevokeHost(ctx, tx, in.HostID, env.Clock.Now())
	})
	if err != nil {
		return core.Host{}, err
	}
	h.m.disconnect(in.HostID)
	return h.m.hostView(ctx, env, in.HostID)
}

func (h hostRevoke) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput, _ core.Host) error {
	r, err := readHost(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if r.state != core.HostRevoked {
		return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: host %s is %s, want revoked", in.HostID, r.state)
	}
	return nil
}

type hostDelete struct{ m module }

// deletable refuses the local host, which the controller always manages,
// and an enrolled host, whose agent still holds valid credentials.
func deletable(r hostRecord) (local, enrolled core.Guard) {
	local = core.Guard{ID: "not-local", OK: !r.local, Message: r.name + " is not the local host"}
	if r.local {
		local.Message = r.name + " is the local host, which the controller always manages"
	}
	enrolled = core.Guard{ID: "not-enrolled", OK: r.state != core.HostEnrolled, Message: r.name + " is " + r.state}
	if r.state == core.HostEnrolled {
		enrolled.Message = r.name + " is enrolled; revoke it first"
	}
	return local, enrolled
}

func (h hostDelete) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	r, err := readHost(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	local, enrolled := deletable(r)
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "-", Text: fmt.Sprintf("host %s (%s, %s), with its certificates, enrollment token, module state and update policy",
				r.name, in.HostID, r.state)},
			{Op: " ", Text: "its audit entries are kept"},
		},
		Guards:       []core.Guard{local, enrolled},
		StateHash:    stateHash(in.HostID, r.state, strconv.FormatBool(r.local)),
		Confirmation: r.name,
	}, nil
}

func (h hostDelete) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput) (core.Host, error) {
	view, err := h.m.hostView(ctx, env, in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.Host{}, err
	}
	err = env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		r, err := readHost(ctx, tx, in.HostID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if local, enrolled := deletable(r); !local.OK || !enrolled.OK {
			return errcode.New(errcode.PlanStateChanged, fmt.Sprintf("host %s changed since the plan; plan again", in.HostID))
		}
		_, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, in.HostID)
		return err
	})
	var coded *errcode.Error
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &coded):
		return core.Host{}, err
	case err != nil:
		return core.Host{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: delete host: %w", err)
	}
	h.m.disconnect(in.HostID)
	view.Online = false
	return view, nil
}

func (h hostDelete) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostIDInput, _ core.Host) error {
	_, err := readHost(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	var coded *errcode.Error
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &coded) && coded.Code == errcode.NotFound:
		return nil
	case err != nil:
		return err
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: host %s still exists", in.HostID)
}
```

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// testAdvertise is the --agent-advertise address of newEnrollEnv.
const testAdvertise = "198.18.0.10:7443"

// enrollEnv is a test env whose core module has a real agent authority,
// recording sessions, and the local host h1 named alpha.
type enrollEnv struct {
	*testEnv
	authority *fleet.Authority
	sessions  *fakeSessions
}

func newEnrollEnv(t *testing.T, advertise string) *enrollEnv {
	t.Helper()
	out := &enrollEnv{sessions: &fakeSessions{}}
	out.testEnv = newTestEnvWith(t, func(e *testEnv, d *Deps) {
		a, err := fleet.LoadOrCreateAuthority(context.Background(), e.db, coretest.Sealer{}, e.clock)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		out.authority = a
		d.Authority, d.AgentAdvertise, d.Sessions = a, advertise, out.sessions
	}, fakeModule{"demo", 1})
	out.addHost("h1", "alpha", true)
	return out
}

// hostIDRule is the host id rule of agent certificates (transport.HostIDOf).
var hostIDRule = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9A-Za-z]{1,64}$`)

// checkGrant decodes a grant and checks that its token enrolls the grant's
// host at address against this controller's authority, expires an hour
// after t0, and is the host's one stored token.
func (e *enrollEnv) checkGrant(raw json.RawMessage, address string) core.EnrollmentGrant {
	e.t.Helper()
	var g core.EnrollmentGrant
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &g); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tok, err := transport.ParseToken(g.Token)
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatalf("the granted token does not parse: %v", err)
	}
	if tok.HostID != g.Host.ID || tok.Address != address || tok.Fingerprint != e.authority.Fingerprint() ||
		tok.ControllerSAN != e.authority.ControllerSAN() {
		e.t.Errorf("token for host %s at %s, want host %s at %s of this controller", tok.HostID, tok.Address, g.Host.ID, address)
	}
	if !g.ExpiresAt.Equal(t0.Add(fleet.TokenTTL)) {
		e.t.Errorf("expiresAt = %v, want %v", g.ExpiresAt, t0.Add(fleet.TokenTTL))
	}
	hash, err := tok.SecretHash()
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var stored []byte
	if err := e.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT hash FROM core_enroll_tokens WHERE host_id = ? AND consumed_at IS NULL`,
		g.Host.ID).Scan(&stored); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatalf("stored token of %s: %v", g.Host.ID, err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(stored, hash) {
		e.t.Error("the stored token is not the granted one")
	}
	return g
}

func wantField(t *testing.T, err error, path string) {
	t.Helper()
	var e *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &e) || e.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(e.Fields) != 1 || e.Fields[0].Path != path {
		t.Fatalf("err = %#v, want request.invalid_input naming %s", err, path)
	}
}

func TestHostCreate(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	in := core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web1"}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.create", in); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed before the host exists")
	}
	v := e.run(operator, "core.host.create", in, nil)
	g := e.checkGrant(v.Output, testAdvertise)
	h := g.Host
	if !hostIDRule.MatchString(h.ID) || h.Name != "web1" || h.State != core.HostPending || h.IsLocal || h.Online ||
		len(h.Modules) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("host = %+v, want a pending remote host web1 with the demo module", h)
	}
	noSecret(t, []string{g.Token}, e.change(v.ID), e.auditEntries())
	if err := e.verify("core.host.create", in); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}

	v = e.run(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web2", ControllerAddress: "10.77.0.10:7443"}, nil)
	e.checkGrant(v.Output, "10.77.0.10:7443")
}

func TestHostCreateRefusesATakenName(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "alpha"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err = e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, "")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanGuardFailed)

	// A host of that name created between the engine's recheck and the apply.
	e.run(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web1"}, nil)
	wantCode(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web1"}), errcode.PlanStateChanged)
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_hosts WHERE name = 'web1'`); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("hosts named web1 = %d, want 1", n)
	}
}

func TestHostEnrollmentNeedsAnAddress(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, "")
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	_, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web2"})
	wantField(t, err, "/controllerAddress")
	_, err = e.plan(operator, "core.host.token", core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h2"})
	wantField(t, err, "/controllerAddress")
	v := e.run(operator, "core.host.token", core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h2", ControllerAddress: "[fd7a::1]:7443"}, nil)
	e.checkGrant(v.Output, "[fd7a::1]:7443")
}

// TestHostEnrollmentChecksTheAddress: a token is only as good as the address
// it carries, and no schema pattern covers --agent-advertise.
func TestHostEnrollmentChecksTheAddress(t *testing.T) {
	for _, advertise := range []string{"198.18.0.10", "198.18.0.10:0", "198.18.0.10:65536", "198.18.0.10:http", ":7443"} {
		e := newEnrollEnv(t, advertise)
		e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
		_, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web2"})
		wantField(t, err, "/controllerAddress")
		_, err = e.plan(operator, "core.host.token", core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h2"})
		wantField(t, err, "/controllerAddress")
		wantField(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.token", core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h2"}), "/controllerAddress")
		if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_enroll_tokens`); n != 0 {
			t.Errorf("advertise %q: tokens = %d, want none", advertise, n)
		}
	}
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	for _, addr := range []string{"web.example:0", "web.example:65536", "web.example:99999", "web.example:007443"} {
		_, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web2", ControllerAddress: addr})
		wantField(t, err, "/controllerAddress")
	}
	v := e.run(operator, "core.host.create", core.HostCreateInput{Name: "web2", ControllerAddress: "web.example:65535"}, nil)
	e.checkGrant(v.Output, "web.example:65535")
}

func TestHostToken(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	in := core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h2"}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.token", in); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed before a token exists")
	}
	first := e.checkGrant(e.run(operator, "core.host.token", in, nil).Output, testAdvertise)
	second := e.checkGrant(e.run(operator, "core.host.token", in, nil).Output, testAdvertise)
	if first.Token == second.Token || second.Host.ID != "h2" {
		t.Errorf("the second grant = %+v, want a new token for h2", second.Host)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_enroll_tokens`); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("tokens = %d, want the replacement only", n)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.token", in); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}

	e.setHostState("h2", core.HostEnrolled)
	pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.token", in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if pv.Confirmation != "web1" {
		t.Errorf("confirmation = %q, want the host name web1", pv.Confirmation)
	}
	_, err = e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, pv.Confirmation)
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanGuardFailed)
	wantCode(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.token", in), errcode.PlanStateChanged)

	e.setHostState("h2", core.HostRevoked)
	e.checkGrant(e.run(operator, "core.host.token", in, nil).Output, testAdvertise)

	_, err = e.plan(operator, "core.host.token", core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "nope"})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
}

// TestHostTokenRefusesTheLocalHost: a network caller must not enroll another
// machine under the controller's own host id, in any state of that host.
func TestHostTokenRefusesTheLocalHost(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	local := core.HostTokenInput{HostID: "h1"}
	for _, state := range []string{core.HostPending, core.HostRevoked} {
		e.setHostState("h1", state)
		pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.token", local)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if g := pv.Guards[0]; g.ID != "not-local" || g.OK || !strings.Contains(g.Message, "core.host.enroll-local") {
			t.Errorf("%s local host: guard %+v, want not-local failing and naming core.host.enroll-local", state, g)
		}
		_, err = e.apply(operator, pv.PlanID, pv.Confirmation)
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanGuardFailed)
		wantCode(t, e.applyDirect("core.host.token", local), errcode.PlanGuardFailed)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_enroll_tokens`); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("tokens = %d, want none for the local host", n)
	}
}

func TestHostEnrollLocal(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnrollEnv(t, "")
	e.setHostState("h1", core.HostEnrolled)
	e.addCert("h1", "b1")
	if err := e.verify("core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{}); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed before a token exists")
	}
	v := e.run(root, "core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{}, nil)
	g := e.checkGrant(v.Output, LocalAgentAddress)
	if g.Host.ID != "h1" || g.Host.State != core.HostRevoked || !g.Host.IsLocal {
		t.Errorf("host = %+v, want the local host h1, revoked", g.Host)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_agent_certs WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("unrevoked certificates = %d, want none", n)
	}
	if got := e.sessions.closed(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"h1"}) {
		t.Errorf("closed sessions = %v, want [h1]", got)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{}); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}

	// Every state is allowed, so a repeated repair works too.
	e.checkGrant(e.run(root, "core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{}, nil).Output, LocalAgentAddress)

	fresh := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	g = fresh.checkGrant(fresh.run(root, "core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{}, nil).Output, LocalAgentAddress)
	if g.Host.State != core.HostRevoked {
		t.Errorf("a fresh local host is %s after enroll-local, want revoked", g.Host.State)
	}

	none := newEnrollEnv(t, testAdvertise)
	none.exec(`DELETE FROM core_hosts`)
	_, err := none.plan(root, "core.host.enroll-local", NoInput{})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
}
```

Create `internal/controller/coreactions/hostenroll.go`:

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

// LocalAgentAddress is the address the controller's own agent dials: the
// agent listener's loopback bind, which is always present (spec section 4).
const LocalAgentAddress = "127.0.0.1:7443"

func (m module) hostEnrollActions() []core.ActionDef {
	return []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineMutating[core.HostCreateInput, core.EnrollmentGrant](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.create", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideController,
			CompensatedBy: "core.host.delete",
			Summary:       "Create a pending host and return its one-hour enrollment token, shown only once.",
		}, hostCreate{m}),
		core.DefineMutating[core.HostTokenInput, core.EnrollmentGrant](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.token", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideController,
			Summary: "Issue a pending or revoked host other than the local one a new one-hour enrollment token, replacing any earlier one; shown only once. The confirmation phrase is the host name.",
		}, hostToken{m}),
		core.DefineMutating[NoInput, core.EnrollmentGrant](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.enroll-local", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideController, BreakGlass: true,
			Summary: "Revoke the local host's agent credentials and issue it a new enrollment token for 127.0.0.1:7443; served only on the controller socket. The confirmation phrase is the host name.",
		}, hostEnrollLocal{m}),
	}
}

func (m module) authority() (*fleet.Authority, error) {
	if m.d.Authority == nil {
		return nil, errUnset("Deps.Authority")
	}
	return m.d.Authority, nil
}

// address is the controller address a token carries: the requested one,
// else serve's --agent-advertise. It must be host:port with a port from 1 to
// 65535; the input's pattern admits ports up to 99999, and nothing checks the
// advertise flag before a token carries it to an agent.
func (m module) address(requested string) (string, error) {
	addr, source := requested, "controllerAddress"
	if addr == "" {
		addr, source = m.d.AgentAdvertise, "the --agent-advertise address"
	}
	if addr == "" {
		return "", badAddress("name the address the agent dials: the controller has no --agent-advertise address",
			"required when the controller has no --agent-advertise address")
	}
	host, port, err := net.SplitHostPort(addr)
	n, perr := strconv.Atoi(port)
	if err != nil || host == "" || perr != nil || strconv.Itoa(n) != port || n < 1 || n > 65535 {
		return "", badAddress(fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not host:port with a port from 1 to 65535", source, addr),
			"want host:port with a port from 1 to 65535")
	}
	return addr, nil
}

func badAddress(detail, message string) error {
	return &errcode.Error{
		Code: errcode.InvalidInput, Detail: detail,
		Fields: []errcode.FieldError{{Path: "/controllerAddress", Message: message}},
	}
}

// grant is hostID as core.host.list shows it, with the token an apply just
// issued it.
func (m module) grant(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID, token string, expires time.Time) (core.EnrollmentGrant, error) {
	host, err := m.hostView(ctx, env, hostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	return core.EnrollmentGrant{Host: host, Token: token, ExpiresAt: expires.UTC()}, nil
}

// issued is Verify of the token actions: hostID holds a token that no agent
// has consumed, which only the apply's own grant can have reached.
func issued(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string) error {
	var n int
	if err := env.DB.Read().QueryRowContext(ctx,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM core_enroll_tokens WHERE host_id = ? AND consumed_at IS NULL`, hostID).Scan(&n); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read enrollment token: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: host %s has no enrollment token", hostID)
	}
	return nil
}

func nameTaken(ctx context.Context, q querier, name string) (bool, error) {
	var n int
	if err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM core_hosts WHERE name = ?`, name).Scan(&n); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read hosts: %w", err)
	}
	return n > 0, nil
}

type hostCreate struct{ m module }

func (h hostCreate) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostCreateInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	if _, err := h.m.authority(); err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	addr, err := h.m.address(in.ControllerAddress)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	taken, err := nameTaken(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	guard := core.Guard{ID: "name-free", OK: !taken, Message: "no host is named " + in.Name}
	if taken {
		guard.Message = "a host named " + in.Name + " already exists"
	}
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("host %s: pending, with an enrollment token for %s, shown once and valid for one hour", in.Name, addr)},
			{Op: " ", Text: "compensated by core.host.delete"},
		},
		Guards:    []core.Guard{guard},
		StateHash: stateHash(in.Name, strconv.FormatBool(taken)),
	}, nil
}

func (h hostCreate) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostCreateInput) (core.EnrollmentGrant, error) {
	a, err := h.m.authority()
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	addr, err := h.m.address(in.ControllerAddress)
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	now := env.Clock.Now()
	id, err := ulid.New(ulid.Timestamp(now), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: host id: %w", err)
	}
	var token string
	var expires time.Time
	err = env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		taken, err := nameTaken(ctx, tx, in.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if taken {
			return errcode.New(errcode.PlanStateChanged, fmt.Sprintf("a host named %s was created since the plan; plan again", in.Name))
		}
		if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO core_hosts (id, name, is_local, state, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 0, ?, ?)`,
			id.String(), in.Name, core.HostPending, formatTime(now)); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: create host: %w", err)
		}
		token, expires, err = a.IssueToken(ctx, tx, id.String(), addr)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	return h.m.grant(ctx, env, id.String(), token, expires)
}

// Verify checks the name alone: recovery passes no output, so the id the
// apply chose is unknown.
func (h hostCreate) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostCreateInput, _ core.EnrollmentGrant) error {
	taken, err := nameTaken(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !taken {
		return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: no host is named %s", in.Name)
	}
	return nil
}

// localOnlyDetail is why core.host.token refuses the local host: a token
// issued over the network would let another machine enroll under the
// controller's own host id, which only the break-glass path may re-enroll.
const localOnlyDetail = " is the local host; enroll it with core.host.enroll-local over the controller socket"

// tokenGuards refuse a token for the local host and for an enrolled host,
// whose agent still holds valid credentials: core.host.revoke comes first.
func tokenGuards(r hostRecord) []core.Guard {
	local := core.Guard{ID: "not-local", OK: !r.local, Message: r.name + " is not the local host"}
	if r.local {
		local.Message = r.name + localOnlyDetail
	}
	enrolled := core.Guard{ID: "not-enrolled", OK: r.state != core.HostEnrolled, Message: r.name + " is " + r.state}
	if !enrolled.OK {
		enrolled.Message = r.name + " is enrolled; revoke it before issuing a new token"
	}
	return []core.Guard{local, enrolled}
}

type hostToken struct{ m module }

func (h hostToken) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostTokenInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	if _, err := h.m.authority(); err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	addr, err := h.m.address(in.ControllerAddress)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	r, err := readHost(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("host %s (%s): a new enrollment token for %s, shown once and valid for one hour; an earlier token stops working",
				r.name, in.HostID, addr)},
		},
		Guards:       tokenGuards(r),
		StateHash:    stateHash(in.HostID, r.state),
		Confirmation: r.name,
	}, nil
}

func (h hostToken) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostTokenInput) (core.EnrollmentGrant, error) {
	a, err := h.m.authority()
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	addr, err := h.m.address(in.ControllerAddress)
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	var token string
	var expires time.Time
	err = env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		r, err := readHost(ctx, tx, in.HostID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		switch {
		case r.local:
			return errcode.New(errcode.PlanGuardFailed, r.name+localOnlyDetail)
		case r.state == core.HostEnrolled:
			return errcode.New(errcode.PlanStateChanged, fmt.Sprintf("host %s was enrolled since the plan; plan again", in.HostID))
		}
		token, expires, err = a.IssueToken(ctx, tx, in.HostID, addr)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	return h.m.grant(ctx, env, in.HostID, token, expires)
}

func (h hostToken) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.HostTokenInput, _ core.EnrollmentGrant) error {
	return issued(ctx, env, in.HostID)
}

// localHost returns the id and record of the controller's own host.
func localHost(ctx context.Context, q querier) (string, hostRecord, error) {
	var id string
	r := hostRecord{local: true}
	err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT id, name, state FROM core_hosts WHERE is_local = 1`).Scan(&id, &r.name, &r.state)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return "", hostRecord{}, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "the controller has no local host")
	case err != nil:
		return "", hostRecord{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read the local host: %w", err)
	}
	return id, r, nil
}

type hostEnrollLocal struct{ m module }

func (h hostEnrollLocal) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ NoInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	if _, err := h.m.authority(); err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	id, r, err := localHost(ctx, env.DB.Read())
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	subject := fmt.Sprintf("host %s (%s)", r.name, id)
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "-", Text: subject + ": " + r.state + ", with its agent certificates and enrollment token"},
			{Op: "+", Text: subject + ": revoked, with a new enrollment token for " + LocalAgentAddress +
				", shown once and valid for one hour; its live session closes"},
		},
		Guards:       []core.Guard{},
		StateHash:    stateHash(id, r.state),
		Confirmation: r.name,
	}, nil
}

// Apply revokes and issues in one write transaction, so a crash leaves the
// local host either as it was or revoked with a fresh token.
func (h hostEnrollLocal) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ NoInput) (core.EnrollmentGrant, error) {
	a, err := h.m.authority()
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	var id, token string
	var expires time.Time
	err = env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		if id, _, err = localHost(ctx, tx); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := fleet.RevokeHost(ctx, tx, id, env.Clock.Now()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		token, expires, err = a.IssueToken(ctx, tx, id, LocalAgentAddress)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return core.EnrollmentGrant{}, err
	}
	h.m.disconnect(id)
	return h.m.grant(ctx, env, id, token, expires)
}

func (h hostEnrollLocal) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ NoInput, _ core.EnrollmentGrant) error {
	id, _, err := localHost(ctx, env.DB.Read())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return issued(ctx, env, id)
}
```

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

// policyRow is a core_host_update row; "<null>" stands for NULL.
type policyRow struct {
	Channel, Days, Time, LastWindow string
}

func (e *testEnv) policyRow(hostID string) policyRow {
	e.t.Helper()
	var r policyRow
	var days, at, window sql.NullString
	if err := e.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT channel, days, time_utc, last_window FROM core_host_update WHERE host_id = ?`,
		hostID).Scan(&r.Channel, &days, &at, &window); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, f := range []struct {
		dst *string
		src sql.NullString
	}{{&r.Days, days}, {&r.Time, at}, {&r.LastWindow, window}} {
		*f.dst = "<null>"
		if f.src.Valid {
			*f.dst = f.src.String
		}
	}
	return r
}

func TestHostUpdatePolicy(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTestEnv(t)
	e.addHost("h1", "alpha", true)
	e.addHost("h2", "web1", false)
	beta := core.UpdatePolicy{HostID: "h2", Channel: core.ChannelBeta,
		Schedule: &core.UpdateSchedule{Days: []string{"thu", "mon"}, Time: "03:00"}}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.update-policy", beta); err == nil {
		t.Error("Verify passed before the policy was set")
	}

	pv, err := e.plan(operator, "core.host.update-policy", beta)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantDiff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "host web1 (h2): channel stable, updates on demand only"},
		{Op: "+", Text: "host web1 (h2): channel beta, a two-hour window opening at 03:00 UTC on mon,thu"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "compensated by core.host.update-policy with the previous policy"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(pv.Diff, wantDiff) {
		t.Errorf("diff = %+v\nwant %+v", pv.Diff, wantDiff)
	}
	var got core.UpdatePolicy
	e.run(operator, "core.host.update-policy", beta, &got)
	want := core.UpdatePolicy{HostID: "h2", Channel: core.ChannelBeta,
		Schedule: &core.UpdateSchedule{Days: []string{"mon", "thu"}, Time: "03:00"}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("output = %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
	if r := e.policyRow("h2"); r != (policyRow{"beta", "mon,thu", "03:00", "<null>"}) {
		t.Errorf("row = %+v, want beta with days mon,thu at 03:00", r)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.update-policy", beta); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}

	// Setting the policy again keeps the scheduler's last window.
	e.exec(`UPDATE core_host_update SET last_window = '2026-10-15T03:00' WHERE host_id = 'h2'`)
	stable := core.UpdatePolicy{HostID: "h2", Channel: core.ChannelStable}
	v := e.run(operator, "core.host.update-policy", stable, nil)
	if !jsonEqual(t, v.Output, `{"hostId":"h2","channel":"stable","schedule":null}`) {
		t.Errorf("output = %s, want stable without a schedule", v.Output)
	}
	if r := e.policyRow("h2"); r != (policyRow{"stable", "<null>", "<null>", "2026-10-15T03:00"}) {
		t.Errorf("row = %+v, want stable without a schedule, keeping its last window", r)
	}
	if err := e.verify("core.host.update-policy", stable); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Verify: %v", err)
	}
	if n := e.count(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_host_update WHERE host_id = 'h1'`); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("h1 has %d policy rows, want none", n)
	}

	_, err = e.plan(operator, "core.host.update-policy", core.UpdatePolicy{HostID: "nope", Channel: core.ChannelStable})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
	for _, bad := range []string{
		`{"hostId":"h2","channel":"nightly","schedule":null}`,
		`{"hostId":"h2","channel":"beta","schedule":{"days":["mon","mon"],"time":"03:00"}}`,
		`{"hostId":"h2","channel":"beta","schedule":{"days":[],"time":"03:00"}}`,
		`{"hostId":"h2","channel":"beta","schedule":{"days":["mon"],"time":"24:00"}}`,
	} {
		_, err := e.engine.Plan(t.Context(), operator, ref("core.host.update-policy"), json.RawMessage(bad))
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
	}
}
```

```diff
+// documented reports whether prop has a description. The reflector writes a
+// nullable reference's description on its reference branch of oneOf; such
+// a field is nested, so the CLI takes it from --input rather than a flag.
+func documented(prop map[string]any) bool {
+	if d, _ := prop["description"].(string); d != "" {
+		return true
+	}
+	branches, _ := prop["oneOf"].([]any)
+	for _, b := range branches {
+		if branch, _ := b.(map[string]any); branch["$ref"] != nil {
+			d, _ := branch["description"].(string)
+			return d != ""
+		}
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
 // TestZeroDepsFailClosed calls the handlers of the registry.Build() core
 // module, which has no services: each refuses with internal instead of
 // panicking.
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/controller/coreactions/hostpolicy.go`:

```go
package coreactions

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
)

// weekdays is the order core_host_update.days stores its days in.
var weekdays = []string{"mon", "tue", "wed", "thu", "fri", "sat", "sun"}

func (m module) hostPolicyActions() []core.ActionDef {
	return []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineMutating[core.UpdatePolicy, core.UpdatePolicy](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: "core.host.update-policy", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideController,
			CompensatedBy: "core.host.update-policy",
			Summary:       "Set the release channel a host follows and its weekly two-hour update window in UTC, or none for on-demand updates only.",
		}, hostUpdatePolicy{m}),
	}
}

// normalPolicy is p with its days in weekday order, as it is stored.
func normalPolicy(p core.UpdatePolicy) core.UpdatePolicy {
	if p.Schedule != nil {
		days := []string{}
		for _, d := range weekdays {
			if slices.Contains(p.Schedule.Days, d) {
				days = append(days, d)
			}
		}
		p.Schedule = &core.UpdateSchedule{Days: days, Time: p.Schedule.Time}
	}
	return p
}

// readPolicy returns hostID's stored policy. A host without one follows the
// stable channel and updates on demand only.
func readPolicy(ctx context.Context, q querier, hostID string) (core.UpdatePolicy, error) {
	p := core.UpdatePolicy{HostID: hostID, Channel: core.ChannelStable}
	var days, at sql.NullString
	err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT channel, days, time_utc FROM core_host_update WHERE host_id = ?`, hostID).
		Scan(&p.Channel, &days, &at)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows):
		return p, nil
	case err != nil:
		return core.UpdatePolicy{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: read update policy: %w", err)
	}
	if days.Valid && at.Valid {
		p.Schedule = &core.UpdateSchedule{Days: strings.Split(days.String, ","), Time: at.String}
	}
	return p, nil
}

func describePolicy(p core.UpdatePolicy) string {
	if p.Schedule == nil {
		return "channel " + p.Channel + ", updates on demand only"
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("channel %s, a two-hour window opening at %s UTC on %s",
		p.Channel, p.Schedule.Time, strings.Join(p.Schedule.Days, ","))
}

type hostUpdatePolicy struct{ m module }

func (h hostUpdatePolicy) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.UpdatePolicy) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	q := env.DB.Read()
	r, err := readHost(ctx, q, in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	cur, err := readPolicy(ctx, q, in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	subject := fmt.Sprintf("host %s (%s): ", r.name, in.HostID)
	next := normalPolicy(in)
	diff := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: subject + describePolicy(cur)},
		{Op: "+", Text: subject + describePolicy(next)},
	}
	if reflect.DeepEqual(cur, next) {
		diff = []core.DiffLine{{Op: " ", Text: subject + describePolicy(cur) + " already"}}
	}
	diff = append(diff, core.DiffLine{Op: " ", Text: "compensated by core.host.update-policy with the previous policy"})
	return core.PlanOutcome{Diff: diff, Guards: []core.Guard{}, StateHash: stateHash(in.HostID, describePolicy(cur))}, nil
}

// Apply keeps last_window and last_rollout_id, so a changed policy never
// reopens a window the scheduler already used.
func (h hostUpdatePolicy) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.UpdatePolicy) (core.UpdatePolicy, error) {
	p := normalPolicy(in)
	var days, at any
	if p.Schedule != nil {
		days, at = strings.Join(p.Schedule.Days, ","), p.Schedule.Time
	}
	err := env.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		if _, err := readHost(ctx, tx, in.HostID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO core_host_update (host_id, channel, days, time_utc) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
			ON CONFLICT (host_id) DO UPDATE SET channel = excluded.channel, days = excluded.days, time_utc = excluded.time_utc`,
			in.HostID, p.Channel, days, at)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdatePolicy{}, fmt.Errorf("coreactions: set update policy: %w", err)
	}
	return readPolicy(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
}

func (h hostUpdatePolicy) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.UpdatePolicy, _ core.UpdatePolicy) error {
	got, err := readPolicy(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if want := normalPolicy(in); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		return fmt.Errorf("coreactions: host %s has %s, want %s", in.HostID, describePolicy(got), describePolicy(want))
	}
	return nil
}
```
