Create `internal/controller/rollout/actions_test.go`:

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"log/slog"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/lifecycle"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// admin is the operator who submits and cancels rollouts.
var admin = core.Principal{Actor: "user:admin", UserID: "admin", Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}

// actionModule is a core module holding only the given actions.
type actionModule struct{ defs []core.ActionDef }

func (m actionModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: core.ModuleIDCore, Version: 1, Actions: m.defs, DefaultEnabled: true}
}

// newEngine returns a controller engine over db serving the rollout actions
// of a controller running version.
func newEngine(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, clock core.Clock, version string) *lifecycle.Engine {
	t.Helper()
	reg, err := core.NewRegistry(actionModule{Actions(ActionsConfig{Version: version})})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	eng, err := lifecycle.New(lifecycle.Config{
		Side: core.SideController, Registry: reg, Validator: schema.NewValidator(), Journal: &coretest.MemJournal{},
		Redact: schema.Redact,
		Base:   core.Env{DB: db, Sealer: coretest.Sealer{}, Clock: clock, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(eng.Close)
	return eng
}

// planApply plans name/v1 with input and applies the plan.
func planApply(eng *lifecycle.Engine, name, input, confirmation string) (core.ChangeView, error) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	plan, err := eng.Plan(ctx, admin, core.ActionRef{Name: name, Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(input))
	if err != nil {
		return core.ChangeView{}, err
	}
	return eng.Apply(ctx, admin, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{Confirmation: confirmation})
}

// outputOf decodes a change's output.
func outputOf[T any](t *testing.T, v core.ChangeView) T {
	t.Helper()
	var out T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

// mustLoad reads rollout id.
func mustLoad(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, id string) record {
	t.Helper()
	r, err := load(context.Background(), db.Read(), id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r
}

// hostStates lists a rollout's host states in position order as
// "<host>=<state>".
func hostStates(r record) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, h := range r.Hosts {
		out = append(out, h.HostID+"="+h.State)
	}
	return out
}

func TestRolloutActionsRegister(t *testing.T) {
	defs := Actions(ActionsConfig{Version: "1.2.0"})
	reg, err := core.NewRegistry(actionModule{defs})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]struct {
		class         core.Class
		compensatedBy string
	}{
		ActionRollout:       {core.ClassCompensable, ActionRolloutCancel},
		ActionRolloutCancel: {core.ClassIrreversible, ""},
		ActionRollouts:      {core.ClassRead, ""},
	}
	if len(reg.Actions()) != len(want) {
		t.Fatalf("registered %d actions, want %d", len(reg.Actions()), len(want))
	}
	for _, def := range reg.Actions() {
		w, ok := want[def.Spec.Name]
		switch {
		case !ok:
			t.Errorf("unexpected action %s", def.Ref())
		case def.Spec.Version != 1 || def.Spec.Side != core.SideController || def.Spec.Class != w.class ||
			def.Spec.CompensatedBy != w.compensatedBy || len(def.Spec.LockKeys) != 0 || def.Spec.BreakGlass:
			t.Errorf("%s spec = %+v", def.Ref(), def.Spec)
		}
		if _, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(def); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s snapshot: %v", def.Ref(), err)
		}
	}
	for _, def := range defs {
		var err error
		if def.Spec.Class == core.ClassRead {
			_, err = def.Handler.Read(context.Background(), core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{}`))
		} else {
			_, err = def.Handler.Plan(context.Background(), core.Env{}, json.RawMessage(`{}`))
		}
		if err == nil || errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.Internal {
			t.Errorf("%s without a database: error = %v, want an internal error", def.Ref(), err)
		}
	}
}

func TestRolloutAction(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	clock := coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart)
	eng := newEngine(t, db, clock, "1.2.0")
	plan, err := eng.Plan(context.Background(), admin, core.ActionRef{Name: ActionRollout, Version: 1},
		json.RawMessage(`{"hosts":["A","OWN"],"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable"}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var diff strings.Builder
	for _, l := range plan.Diff {
		diff.WriteString(l.Op + " " + l.Text + "\n")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(diff.String(), "+ darkpanel 1.3.0 (stable) on 2 hosts, one at a time: OWN, A") {
		t.Errorf("diff = %q", diff.String())
	}
	v, err := eng.Apply(context.Background(), admin, plan.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if v.State != core.StateCompleted {
		t.Fatalf("change = %+v, want completed", v)
	}
	r := outputOf[core.Rollout](t, v)
	if r.State != core.RolloutRunning || r.Version != "1.3.0" || !slices.Equal(hostOrder(r), []string{"OWN", "A"}) {
		t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", r)
	}
	if rec := mustLoad(t, db, r.ID); rec.ChangeID != v.ID {
		t.Errorf("change id = %q, want %q", rec.ChangeID, v.ID)
	}
	if v.CompensatedBy != ActionRolloutCancel {
		t.Errorf("compensated by %q", v.CompensatedBy)
	}
	def := actionDef(t, Actions(ActionsConfig{Version: "1.2.0"}), ActionRollout)
	env := core.Env{DB: db, Clock: clock, ChangeID: v.ID}
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"hosts":["A","OWN"],"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable"}`)
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(context.Background(), env, in, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("recovery Verify = %v, want the rollout found by its change", err)
	}
	env.ChangeID = "OTHER"
	if err := def.Handler.Verify(context.Background(), env, in, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err == nil {
		t.Error("recovery Verify found a rollout for another change")
	}
}

// actionDef returns the action of defs named name.
func actionDef(t *testing.T, defs []core.ActionDef, name string) core.ActionDef {
	t.Helper()
	i := slices.IndexFunc(defs, func(d core.ActionDef) bool { return d.Spec.Name == name })
	if i < 0 {
		t.Fatalf("no action %s", name)
	}
	return defs[i]
}

func TestRolloutActionRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	eng := newEngine(t, db, coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart), "1.2.0")
	_, err := planApply(eng, ActionRollout, `{"hosts":["A"],"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable"}`, "")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)

	ctx := context.Background()
	ref := core.ActionRef{Name: ActionRollout, Version: 1}
	first, err := eng.Plan(ctx, admin, ref, json.RawMessage(`{"hosts":["OWN"],"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable"}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	second, err := eng.Plan(ctx, admin, ref, json.RawMessage(`{"hosts":["A"],"version":"1.2.0","channel":"stable"}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := eng.Apply(ctx, admin, first.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err = eng.Apply(ctx, admin, second.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.RolloutInProgress)
	_, err = eng.Plan(ctx, admin, ref, json.RawMessage(`{"hosts":["A"],"version":"1.2.0","channel":"stable"}`))
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.RolloutInProgress)
}

func TestRolloutCancel(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name        string
		setup       []string // statements run on rollout ? with hosts OWN, A, B
		force       bool
		state       string
		outcome     string
		hosts       []string
		updatingErr string // the error code of host OWN, if any
	}{
		{
			name:  "before any host started",
			state: core.RolloutCancelled, outcome: core.RolloutCancelled,
			hosts: []string{"OWN=skipped", "A=skipped", "B=skipped"},
		},
		{
			name:  "a host updating without a plan",
			setup: []string{"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating' WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = 'OWN'"},
			state: core.RolloutCancelled, outcome: core.RolloutCancelled,
			hosts: []string{"OWN=skipped", "A=skipped", "B=skipped"},
		},
		{
			name: "a host updating with a plan",
			setup: []string{
				"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'committed' WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = 'OWN'",
				"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating', plan_id = 'P1' WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = 'A'",
			},
			state: core.RolloutDraining, outcome: core.RolloutCancelled,
			hosts: []string{"OWN=committed", "A=updating", "B=skipped"},
		},
		{
			name: "with force while a host updates",
			setup: []string{"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating', plan_id = 'P1', operation_id = 'OP1' " +
				"WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = 'OWN'"},
			force: true,
			state: core.RolloutCancelled, outcome: core.RolloutCancelled,
			hosts:       []string{"OWN=failed", "A=skipped", "B=skipped"},
			updatingErr: string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted),
		},
		{
			name: "a draining rollout",
			setup: []string{
				"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating', plan_id = 'P1' WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = 'OWN'",
				"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'skipped' WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id <> 'OWN'",
				"UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'draining', outcome = 'failed' WHERE id = ?",
			},
			state: core.RolloutDraining, outcome: core.RolloutCancelled,
			hosts: []string{"OWN=updating", "A=skipped", "B=skipped"},
		},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			db := openDB(t)
			addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
			addHost(t, db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
			addHost(t, db, "B", false, "1.2.0")
			r := mustCreate(t, db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "A", "B", "OWN")
			for _, s := range tt.setup {
				exec(t, db, s, r.ID)
			}
			eng := newEngine(t, db, coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart), "1.2.0")
			input := `{"rolloutId":"` + r.ID + `","force":` + map[bool]string{true: "true", false: "false"}[tt.force] + `}`
			_, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, input, "")
			wantCode(t, err, errcode.PlanConfirmationNeeded)
			v, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, input, r.ID)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if v.State != core.StateCompleted {
				t.Fatalf("change = %+v, want completed", v)
			}
			if out := outputOf[core.Rollout](t, v); out.State != tt.state {
				t.Errorf("output state = %s, want %s", out.State, tt.state)
			}
			rec := mustLoad(t, db, r.ID)
			if rec.State != tt.state || rec.Outcome != tt.outcome || rec.Error != nil {
				t.Errorf("rollout = %s outcome %q error %v, want %s outcome %q", rec.State, rec.Outcome, rec.Error, tt.state, tt.outcome)
			}
			if got := hostStates(rec); !slices.Equal(got, tt.hosts) {
				t.Errorf("hosts = %v, want %v", got, tt.hosts)
			}
			var code string
			if e := rec.Hosts[0].Error; e != nil {
				code = e.Code
			}
			if code != tt.updatingErr {
				t.Errorf("host OWN error code = %q, want %q", code, tt.updatingErr)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestRolloutCancelRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	r := mustCreate(t, db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
	exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'succeeded' WHERE id = ?", r.ID)
	eng := newEngine(t, db, coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart), "1.2.0")
	_, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, `{"rolloutId":"`+r.ID+`","force":false}`, r.ID)
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.InvalidInput)
	_, err = planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, `{"rolloutId":"MISSING","force":true}`, "MISSING")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.NotFound)
}

func TestRolloutsAction(t *testing.T) {
	db := openDB(t)
	addHost(t, db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	clock := coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart)
	eng := newEngine(t, db, clock, "1.2.0")
	var ids []string
	for _, version := range []string{"1.2.1", "1.2.2", "1.2.3"} {
		v, err := planApply(eng, ActionRollout, `{"hosts":["OWN"],"version":"`+version+`","channel":"stable"}`, "")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		id := outputOf[core.Rollout](t, v).ID
		ids = append(ids, id)
		exec(t, db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'succeeded' WHERE id = ?", id)
		clock.Advance(1)
	}
	read := func(input string) []string {
		t.Helper()
		out, err := eng.Read(context.Background(), admin, core.ActionRef{Name: ActionRollouts, Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(input))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var l core.RolloutList
		if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &l); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var got []string
		for _, r := range l.Rollouts {
			if len(r.Hosts) != 1 || r.Hosts[0].HostID != "OWN" {
				t.Errorf("rollout %s hosts = %+v", r.ID, r.Hosts)
			}
			got = append(got, r.ID)
		}
		return got
	}
	if got := read(`{}`); !slices.Equal(got, []string{ids[2], ids[1], ids[0]}) {
		t.Errorf("rollouts = %v, want newest first %v", got, ids)
	}
	if got := read(`{"limit":2}`); !slices.Equal(got, []string{ids[2], ids[1]}) {
		t.Errorf("rollouts with limit 2 = %v", got)
	}
	exec(t, db, "DELETE FROM core_rollout_hosts")
	exec(t, db, "DELETE FROM core_rollouts")
	out, err := eng.Read(context.Background(), admin, core.ActionRef{Name: ActionRollouts, Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil || string(out) != `{"rollouts":[]}` {
		t.Errorf("empty list = %s, %v", out, err)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/controller/rollout/actions.go`:

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// The core module's controller-side rollout actions (spec section 8, Fleet
// rollout).
const (
	ActionRollout       = "core.update.rollout"
	ActionRolloutCancel = "core.update.rollout-cancel"
	ActionRollouts      = "core.update.rollouts"
)

// DefaultListLimit is how many rollouts core.update.rollouts returns when
// its input names no limit.
const DefaultListLimit = 20

// ActionsConfig is what the rollout actions need beyond their Env.
type ActionsConfig struct {
	// Version is the controller's buildinfo.Version: a rollout to a newer
	// version must list the controller's own host.
	Version string
}

// Actions returns core.update.rollout/v1, core.update.rollout-cancel/v1 and
// core.update.rollouts/v1, for coreactions.Deps.Extra. They run on the
// controller engine over its Env.DB and Env.Clock. The agent process
// registers them too but never runs them; a call without a database fails
// with an internal error.
func Actions(cfg ActionsConfig) []core.ActionDef {
	return []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineMutating[core.RolloutInput, core.Rollout](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionRollout, Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideController,
			CompensatedBy: ActionRolloutCancel,
			Summary: "Update the listed hosts to a release one host at a time, the controller's own host first; " +
				"the rollout stops at the first failure.",
		}, rolloutAction{cfg}),
		core.DefineMutating[core.RolloutCancelInput, core.Rollout](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionRolloutCancel, Version: 1, Class: core.ClassIrreversible, Side: core.SideController,
			Summary: "Stop a rollout: no further host is updated; the confirmation phrase is the rollout id.",
		}, cancelAction{}),
		core.DefineRead[core.RolloutsInput, core.RolloutList](core.ActionSpec{
			Name: ActionRollouts, Version: 1, Side: core.SideController,
			Summary: "List rollouts with their hosts, newest first.",
		}, listAction{}),
	}
}

// dbOf is the controller database of a rollout action's Env.
func dbOf(env core.Env) (*store.DB, error) {
	if env.DB == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: rollout actions run on the controller and need its database")
	}
	return env.DB, nil
}

// stateHash is a plan's state hash over the parts that decide what an apply
// would do.
func stateHash(parts ...string) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(parts, "\x00")))
	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// errForced is the error of a host whose update a forced cancel stopped
// following.
var errForced = &core.ErrorDetail{
	Code:    string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted),
	Message: "the rollout was cancelled with force while this host was updating; its update was not followed to the end",
}

// updating returns r's host whose turn it is, or nil.
func (r record) updating() *hostRecord {
	for i := range r.Hosts {
		if r.Hosts[i].State == core.RolloutHostUpdating {
			return &r.Hosts[i]
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// handedOff reports whether h was given its update: from the moment its
// plan id is stored, the host may be applying it, so stopping the rollout
// waits for the host's outcome.
func handedOff(h *hostRecord) bool {
	return h.PlanID != nil || h.OperationID != nil
}

// setRollout sets rollout id's state, outcome and error; an empty outcome and
// a nil error are stored as NULL.
func setRollout(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, id, state, outcome string, e *core.ErrorDetail, now time.Time) error {
	var out, code, message any
	if outcome != "" {
		out = outcome
	}
	if e != nil {
		code, message = e.Code, e.Message
	}
	_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
		"UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = ?, outcome = ?, error_code = ?, error_message = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
		state, out, code, message, now.UTC().Format(timeLayout), id)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: set rollout %s %s: %w", id, state, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// setRolloutHost sets the state and error of one host of rollout id.
func setRolloutHost(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, id, hostID, state string, e *core.ErrorDetail) error {
	var code, message any
	if e != nil {
		code, message = e.Code, e.Message
	}
	_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
		"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = ?, error_code = ?, error_message = ? WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = ?",
		state, code, message, id, hostID)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: set host %s of %s %s: %w", hostID, id, state, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// skipWaiting marks every host of rollout id that is still waiting skipped:
// the rollout stopped before it reached them.
func skipWaiting(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, id string) error {
	_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = ? WHERE rollout_id = ? AND state = ?",
		core.RolloutHostSkipped, id, core.RolloutHostWaiting)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: skip the waiting hosts of %s: %w", id, err)
	}
	return nil
}

type rolloutAction struct{ cfg ActionsConfig }

func (h rolloutAction) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	hosts, err := check(ctx, db.Read(), in, h.cfg.Version)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: []core.DiffLine{
			{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("darkpanel %s (%s) on %d hosts, one at a time: %s",
				in.Version, in.Channel, len(hosts), strings.Join(hosts, ", "))},
			{Op: " ", Text: "the rollout stops at the first host whose update fails"},
			{Op: " ", Text: "compensated by core.update.rollout-cancel with the new rollout's id"},
		},
		Guards:    []core.Guard{},
		StateHash: stateHash(append([]string{h.cfg.Version, in.Version, in.Channel}, hosts...)...),
	}, nil
}

func (h rolloutAction) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutInput) (core.Rollout, error) {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return core.Rollout{}, err
	}
	var out core.Rollout
	err = db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		out, err = Create(ctx, tx, CreateParams{
			Input: in, ControllerVersion: h.cfg.Version, ChangeID: env.ChangeID, Now: env.Clock.Now(),
		})
		return err
	})
	return out, err
}

// Verify finds the rollout by the id in out, or, for the Verify that
// startup recovery runs without an output, by the change that created it.
func (h rolloutAction) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, _ core.RolloutInput, out core.Rollout) error {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if out.ID != "" {
		_, err := load(ctx, db.Read(), out.ID)
		return err
	}
	if env.ChangeID == "" {
		return errors.New("rollout: verify without a change or a rollout")
	}
	var id string
	err = db.Read().QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM core_rollouts WHERE change_id = ?", env.ChangeID).Scan(&id)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: change %s created no rollout", env.ChangeID)
	}
	return err
}

type cancelAction struct{}

// cancellable refuses a rollout that already ended.
func cancellable(r record) error {
	if r.State != core.RolloutRunning && r.State != core.RolloutDraining {
		return errcode.New(errcode.InvalidInput, fmt.Sprintf("rollout %s is already %s", r.ID, r.State))
	}
	return nil
}

// cancelsNow reports whether cancelling r ends it at once: with force, or
// when no host has been handed its update.
func cancelsNow(r record, force bool) bool {
	h := r.updating()
	return force || h == nil || !handedOff(h)
}

func (cancelAction) Plan(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutCancelInput) (core.PlanOutcome, error) {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	r, err := load(ctx, db.Read(), in.RolloutID)
	if err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	if err := cancellable(r); err != nil {
		return core.PlanOutcome{}, err
	}
	diff := []core.DiffLine{{Op: "-", Text: fmt.Sprintf("rollout %s: %s", r.ID, r.State)}}
	cur, hostID := r.updating(), ""
	if cur != nil {
		hostID = cur.HostID
	}
	switch {
	case !cancelsNow(r, in.Force):
		diff = append(diff, core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf(
			"rollout %s: draining; no further host is updated, and it is cancelled once host %s's update ends",
			r.ID, hostID)})
	case cur != nil && handedOff(cur):
		diff = append(diff,
			core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("rollout %s: cancelled; hosts not yet updated are skipped", r.ID)},
			core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("host %s: failed; its update is no longer followed", hostID)})
	default:
		diff = append(diff,
			core.DiffLine{Op: "+", Text: fmt.Sprintf("rollout %s: cancelled; hosts not yet updated are skipped", r.ID)})
	}
	planID := ""
	if cur != nil && cur.PlanID != nil {
		planID = *cur.PlanID
	}
	return core.PlanOutcome{
		Diff: diff, Guards: []core.Guard{},
		StateHash:    stateHash(r.ID, r.State, r.Outcome, hostID, planID),
		Confirmation: r.ID,
	}, nil
}

func (cancelAction) Apply(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutCancelInput) (core.Rollout, error) {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return core.Rollout{}, err
	}
	var out core.Rollout
	err = db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		r, err := load(ctx, tx, in.RolloutID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := cancellable(r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := cancel(ctx, tx, r, in.Force, env.Clock.Now()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if r, err = load(ctx, tx, in.RolloutID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		out = r.view()
		return nil
	})
	return out, err
}

// cancel stops r inside tx. A rollout whose host was handed its update
// drains: dispatching stops and the driver marks it cancelled once that
// update ends. With force, or when no host was handed an update, it is
// cancelled at once; a forced cancel no longer follows the updating host,
// which fails.
func cancel(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, r record, force bool, now time.Time) error {
	if err := skipWaiting(ctx, tx, r.ID); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !cancelsNow(r, force) {
		return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, core.RolloutDraining, core.RolloutCancelled, r.Error, now)
	}
	if h := r.updating(); h != nil {
		state, e := core.RolloutHostSkipped, (*core.ErrorDetail)(nil)
		if handedOff(h) {
			state, e = core.RolloutHostFailed, errForced
		}
		if err := setRolloutHost(ctx, tx, r.ID, h.HostID, state, e); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, core.RolloutCancelled, core.RolloutCancelled, r.Error, now)
}

// Verify checks that the rollout is cancelled or draining towards
// cancelled.
func (cancelAction) Verify(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutCancelInput, _ core.Rollout) error {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	r, err := load(ctx, db.Read(), in.RolloutID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if r.State == core.RolloutCancelled || (r.State == core.RolloutDraining && r.Outcome == core.RolloutCancelled) {
		return nil
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("rollout: rollout %s is %s after its cancel", r.ID, r.State)
}

type listAction struct{}

func (listAction) Read(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in core.RolloutsInput) (core.RolloutList, error) {
	db, err := dbOf(env)
	if err != nil {
		return core.RolloutList{}, err
	}
	limit := in.Limit
	if limit <= 0 {
		limit = DefaultListLimit
	}
	records, err := list(ctx, db.Read(), limit)
	if err != nil {
		return core.RolloutList{}, err
	}
	out := core.RolloutList{Rollouts: make([]core.Rollout, 0, len(records))}
	for _, r := range records {
		out.Rollouts = append(out.Rollouts, r.view())
	}
	return out, nil
}

// list reads the newest limit rollouts with their hosts, newest first.
func list(ctx context.Context, q querier, limit int) ([]record, error) {
	rows, err := q.QueryContext(ctx,
		"SELECT "+rolloutColumns+" FROM core_rollouts ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?", limit)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: list rollouts: %w", err)
	}
	var out []record
	for rows.Next() {
		r, err := scanRollout(rows)
		if err != nil {
			_ = rows.Close()
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: list rollouts: %w", err)
		}
		out = append(out, r)
	}
	if err := errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: list rollouts: %w", err)
	}
	for i := range out {
		if err := loadHosts(ctx, q, &out[i]); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/dispatch"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/lifecycle"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

var _ Dispatcher = (*dispatch.Dispatcher)(nil)

// fakePlan is a core.update.apply plan the fake fleet made.
type fakePlan struct {
	host, version string
	expires       time.Time
}

// fakeFailure makes a host's next apply record a failed or interrupted
// change; with record the change still started an update operation.
type fakeFailure struct {
	state  string
	record bool
}

// fakeFleet is the controller's dispatcher with the agents behind it. It
// plans and applies core.update.apply as an agent engine does, keeping one
// change per plan whatever its state, and answers core.update.status from
// update records the test sets.
type fakeFleet struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	clock     core.Clock
	n         int
	offline   map[string]bool
	planErr   map[string]error       // every Plan of the host fails with it
	applyErr  map[string]error       // every Apply of the host fails with it
	statusErr map[string]error       // every status read of an operation of the host fails with it
	onApply   func()                 // runs at the start of every Apply, without the fleet's lock
	lose      map[string]bool        // the next Apply records its change and loses the answer
	failNext  map[string]fakeFailure // the next Apply records a failed change
	plans     map[string]fakePlan
	changes   map[string]core.ChangeView // by plan id
	changeOps map[string]string          // change id -> operation id
	lastOp    map[string]string          // host -> operation of its last change
	records   map[string][]core.UpdateTransaction
	calls     []string
}

func newFakeFleet(clock core.Clock) *fakeFleet {
	return &fakeFleet{
		clock: clock, offline: map[string]bool{}, planErr: map[string]error{}, applyErr: map[string]error{},
		statusErr: map[string]error{}, lose: map[string]bool{}, failNext: map[string]fakeFailure{},
		plans:   map[string]fakePlan{},
		changes: map[string]core.ChangeView{}, changeOps: map[string]string{}, lastOp: map[string]string{},
		records: map[string][]core.UpdateTransaction{},
	}
}

func (f *fakeFleet) call(format string, args ...any) {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, fmt.Sprintf(format, args...))
}

// callsOf lists the calls made to host.
func (f *fakeFleet) callsOf(host string) []string {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []string
	for _, c := range f.calls {
		if strings.Fields(c)[1] == host {
			out = append(out, c)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func offline(host string) error {
	return errcode.New(errcode.HostOffline, "host "+host+" is offline")
}

func (f *fakeFleet) Plan(_ context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, input json.RawMessage) (core.PlanView, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.call("plan %s", hostID)
	if p.Actor != core.ActorRollout || a != (core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionApply, Version: 1}) {
		return core.PlanView{}, fmt.Errorf("fake: plan %s by %s", a, p.Actor)
	}
	if f.offline[hostID] {
		return core.PlanView{}, offline(hostID)
	}
	if err := f.planErr[hostID]; err != nil {
		return core.PlanView{}, err
	}
	var in core.UpdateApplyInput
	if err := json.Unmarshal(input, &in); err != nil || in.Force || in.Channel != core.ChannelStable {
		return core.PlanView{}, fmt.Errorf("fake: input %s", input)
	}
	f.n++
	id := fmt.Sprintf("P%d", f.n)
	expires := f.clock.Now().Add(lifecycle.PlanTTL)
	f.plans[id] = fakePlan{host: hostID, version: in.Version, expires: expires}
	return core.PlanView{PlanID: id, HostID: hostID, Action: a.Name, Version: a.Version, ExpiresAt: expires}, nil
}

func (f *fakeFleet) Apply(_ context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, planID string, _ core.ApplyOptions) (core.ChangeView, error) {
	if f.onApply != nil {
		f.onApply()
	}
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.call("apply %s %s", hostID, planID)
	if p.Actor != core.ActorRollout {
		return core.ChangeView{}, fmt.Errorf("fake: apply by %s", p.Actor)
	}
	if f.offline[hostID] {
		return core.ChangeView{}, offline(hostID)
	}
	pl, ok := f.plans[planID]
	if !ok || pl.host != hostID {
		return core.ChangeView{}, errcode.New(errcode.PlanNotFound, "no plan "+planID)
	}
	if v, ok := f.changes[planID]; ok {
		return v, nil
	}
	if err := f.applyErr[hostID]; err != nil {
		return core.ChangeView{}, err
	}
	if !f.clock.Now().Before(pl.expires) {
		return core.ChangeView{}, errcode.New(errcode.PlanExpired, "the plan expired; plan again")
	}
	f.n++
	v := core.ChangeView{ID: fmt.Sprintf("C%d", f.n), HostID: hostID, Action: a.Name, Version: 1, State: core.StateCompleted}
	op := fmt.Sprintf("OP%d", f.n)
	tr := core.UpdateTransaction{ID: op, OperationID: op, Kind: core.UpdateKindUpdate, FromVersion: "1.2.0",
		ToVersion: pl.version, TargetVersion: pl.version, State: core.UpdatePending}
	failure, failed := f.failNext[hostID]
	delete(f.failNext, hostID)
	switch {
	case failed:
		v.State = failure.state
		v.Error = &core.ChangeError{Code: string(errcode.UpdateInProgress), Message: "update operation OP0 is not finished"}
		if failure.record {
			f.changeOps[v.ID], f.lastOp[hostID], f.records[op] = op, op, []core.UpdateTransaction{tr}
		}
	default:
		out, err := json.Marshal(tr)
		if err != nil {
			return core.ChangeView{}, err
		}
		v.Output = out
		f.changeOps[v.ID], f.lastOp[hostID], f.records[op] = op, op, []core.UpdateTransaction{tr}
	}
	f.changes[planID] = v
	if f.lose[hostID] {
		delete(f.lose, hostID)
		return core.ChangeView{}, errors.New("transport: stream reset")
	}
	return v, nil
}

func (f *fakeFleet) Read(_ context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, input json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var in core.UpdateStatusInput
	if err := json.Unmarshal(input, &in); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	f.call("status %s op=%s change=%s", hostID, in.OperationID, in.ChangeID)
	if p.Actor != core.ActorRollout || a != (core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionStatus, Version: 1}) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("fake: read %s by %s", a, p.Actor)
	}
	if f.offline[hostID] {
		return nil, offline(hostID)
	}
	if err := f.statusErr[hostID]; err != nil && in.OperationID != "" {
		return nil, err
	}
	op := in.OperationID
	if in.ChangeID != "" {
		op = f.changeOps[in.ChangeID]
	}
	return json.Marshal(core.UpdateStatus{Transactions: append([]core.UpdateTransaction{}, f.records[op]...)})
}

// setOp replaces the records of host's last operation, newest first; each
// record's id becomes the operation id plus its index from the oldest.
func (f *fakeFleet) setOp(host string, trs ...core.UpdateTransaction) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	op := f.lastOp[host]
	for i := range trs {
		trs[i].ID = fmt.Sprintf("%s.%d", op, len(trs)-1-i)
		trs[i].OperationID = op
	}
	f.records[op] = trs
}

// purge forgets every plan that expired without a change, as the agent's
// purge and the dispatcher's plan index retention do.
func (f *fakeFleet) purge() {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for id, pl := range f.plans {
		if _, applied := f.changes[id]; !applied && !f.clock.Now().Before(pl.expires) {
			delete(f.plans, id)
		}
	}
}

func (f *fakeFleet) setOffline(host string, off bool) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.offline[host] = off
}

// updateRecord is an update record of an operation.
func updateRecord(state, to, target string) core.UpdateTransaction {
	return core.UpdateTransaction{Kind: core.UpdateKindUpdate, FromVersion: "1.2.0", ToVersion: to, TargetVersion: target, State: state}
}

// driverEnv is a controller with hosts OWN (its own), A and B, all on 1.2.0,
// and a fake fleet.
type driverEnv struct {
	db    *store.DB
	clock *coretest.FakeClock
	fleet *fakeFleet
}

func newDriverEnv(t *testing.T) *driverEnv {
	t.Helper()
	e := &driverEnv{db: openDB(t), clock: coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart)}
	e.fleet = newFakeFleet(e.clock)
	addHost(t, e.db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, e.db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, e.db, "B", false, "1.2.0")
	return e
}

// start starts a driver, stopped when the test ends.
func (e *driverEnv) start(t *testing.T) *Driver {
	t.Helper()
	d, err := NewDriver(DriverConfig{DB: e.db, Dispatcher: e.fleet, Clock: e.clock})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	d.Start()
	t.Cleanup(d.Stop)
	return d
}

// passes runs n driver passes.
func (e *driverEnv) passes(n int) {
	for range n {
		e.clock.Advance(PollInterval)
	}
}

// want fails unless rollout id is in state with the given host states.
func (e *driverEnv) want(t *testing.T, id, state string, hosts ...string) record {
	t.Helper()
	r := mustLoad(t, e.db, id)
	if r.State != state || !slices.Equal(hostStates(r), hosts) {
		t.Fatalf("rollout = %s %v, want %s %v", r.State, hostStates(r), state, hosts)
	}
	return r
}

func TestNewDriverRequiresItsDependencies(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	for _, cfg := range []DriverConfig{
		{Dispatcher: e.fleet, Clock: e.clock},
		{DB: e.db, Clock: e.clock},
		{DB: e.db, Dispatcher: e.fleet},
	} {
		if _, err := NewDriver(cfg); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("NewDriver(%+v) succeeded", cfg)
		}
	}
}

func TestDriverUpdatesHostsInOrder(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "A", "OWN")
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(1)
	got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
	if h := got.Hosts[0]; h.PlanID == nil || h.OperationID == nil || !h.FollowedAt.Equal(testStart.Add(PollInterval)) {
		t.Fatalf("host OWN = %+v, want its plan and operation stored", h)
	}
	if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("A"); len(calls) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("host A was called before OWN committed: %v", calls)
	}
	e.passes(2)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
	e.passes(1)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=committed", "A=updating")
	e.fleet.setOp("A", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
	e.passes(1)
	done := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutSucceeded, "OWN=committed", "A=committed")
	if done.Error != nil || !done.UpdatedAt.Equal(testStart.Add(5*PollInterval)) {
		t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", done)
	}
	want := []string{"plan OWN", "apply OWN P1", "status OWN op=OP2 change=", "status OWN op=OP2 change=",
		"status OWN op=OP2 change=", "status OWN op=OP2 change="}
	if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"); !slices.Equal(calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("calls to OWN = %v, want %v", calls, want)
	}
}

func TestDriverStopsAtTheFirstFailure(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A", "B")
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(1)
	cause := updateRecord(core.UpdateRolledBack, "1.3.0", "1.3.0")
	cause.Error = &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted), Message: "the health check failed"}
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", cause)
	e.passes(1)
	got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, "OWN=failed", "A=skipped", "B=skipped")
	if e := got.Hosts[0].Error; e == nil || e.Message != "the health check failed" {
		t.Errorf("host error = %+v", e)
	}
	if got.Error == nil || got.Error.Code != string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted) || !strings.Contains(got.Error.Message, "OWN") {
		t.Errorf("rollout error = %+v", got.Error)
	}
	e.passes(3)
	for _, h := range []string{"A", "B"} {
		if calls := e.fleet.callsOf(h); len(calls) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("calls to %s = %v, want none", h, calls)
		}
	}
}

func TestDriverRefusalsFailTheHost(t *testing.T) {
	agentInternal := errcode.New(errcode.Internal, "update: this process has no updater; update actions run on the agent")
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		planErr  error
		applyErr error
		wantFail errcode.Code // "" when the host waits
	}{
		{"a refused plan", errcode.New(errcode.UpdateNotFound, "no release 1.3.0 in channel stable"), nil, errcode.UpdateNotFound},
		{"a plan answered internal", agentInternal, nil, errcode.Internal},
		{"an apply answered internal", nil, agentInternal, errcode.Internal},
		{"an uncoded plan error", errors.New("transport: connection reset"), nil, ""},
		{"an uncoded apply error", nil, errors.New("transport: connection reset"), ""},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newDriverEnv(t)
			r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
			if tt.planErr != nil {
				e.fleet.planErr["OWN"] = tt.planErr
			}
			if tt.applyErr != nil {
				e.fleet.applyErr["OWN"] = tt.applyErr
			}
			e.start(t)
			e.passes(3)
			if tt.wantFail == "" {
				e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
				return
			}
			got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, "OWN=failed", "A=skipped")
			if got.Hosts[0].Error == nil || got.Hosts[0].Error.Code != string(tt.wantFail) {
				t.Errorf("host error = %+v, want %s", got.Hosts[0].Error, tt.wantFail)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestDriverWaitsForAnOfflineHost(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
	e.fleet.setOffline("OWN", true)
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(200)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating")
	e.fleet.setOffline("OWN", false)
	e.passes(1)
	if h := mustLoad(t, e.db, r.ID).Hosts[0]; h.OperationID == nil {
		t.Fatalf("host = %+v, want its operation once it is back", h)
	}
	e.fleet.setOffline("OWN", true)
	e.passes(10)
	e.fleet.setOffline("OWN", false)
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
	e.passes(1)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutSucceeded, "OWN=committed")
}

func TestDriverResumesAfterARestart(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("before the stored plan was applied", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
		plan, err := e.fleet.Plan(context.Background(), core.Principal{Actor: core.ActorRollout}, "OWN",
			core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionApply, Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating', plan_id = ? WHERE rollout_id = ?", plan.PlanID, r.ID)
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		want := []string{"plan OWN", "apply OWN P1", "status OWN op=OP2 change="}
		if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"); !slices.Equal(calls, want) {
			t.Errorf("calls = %v, want the stored plan applied: %v", calls, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("after the stored plan was applied", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
		e.fleet.lose["OWN"] = true
		d := e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		h := mustLoad(t, e.db, r.ID).Hosts[0]
		if h.PlanID == nil || h.OperationID != nil {
			t.Fatalf("host after a lost answer = %+v", h)
		}
		d.Stop()
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		h = mustLoad(t, e.db, r.ID).Hosts[0]
		if h.OperationID == nil || *h.OperationID != "OP2" {
			t.Fatalf("host after the restart = %+v, want the first change's operation", h)
		}
		if n := len(e.fleet.changes); n != 1 {
			t.Errorf("%d changes, want the one change of the plan", n)
		}
		e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
		e.passes(1)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutSucceeded, "OWN=committed")
	})
	t.Run("while following the operation", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
		d := e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		d.Stop()
		e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
		e.passes(3)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=committed", "A=updating")
		want := []string{"plan OWN", "apply OWN P1", "status OWN op=OP2 change=", "status OWN op=OP2 change="}
		if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"); !slices.Equal(calls, want) {
			t.Errorf("calls to OWN = %v, want the stored operation polled without a new apply: %v", calls, want)
		}
	})
}

func TestDriverReplansAnExpiredPlan(t *testing.T) {
	for _, purged := range []bool{false, true} {
		t.Run(fmt.Sprintf("purged=%v", purged), func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newDriverEnv(t)
			r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
			plan, err := e.fleet.Plan(context.Background(), core.Principal{Actor: core.ActorRollout}, "OWN",
				core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionApply, Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.3.0","channel":"stable","force":false}`))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET state = 'updating', plan_id = ? WHERE rollout_id = ?", plan.PlanID, r.ID)
			e.clock.Advance(lifecycle.PlanTTL + time.Minute)
			if purged {
				e.fleet.purge()
			}
			e.start(t)
			e.passes(1)
			want := []string{"plan OWN", "apply OWN P1", "plan OWN", "apply OWN P2", "status OWN op=OP3 change="}
			if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"); !slices.Equal(calls, want) {
				t.Errorf("calls = %v, want %v", calls, want)
			}
			e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating")
		})
	}
}

func TestDriverFailedApply(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("without an update record", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
		e.fleet.failNext["OWN"] = fakeFailure{state: core.StateFailed}
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, "OWN=failed", "A=skipped")
		if h := got.Hosts[0]; h.Error == nil || h.Error.Code != string(errcode.UpdateInProgress) || h.OperationID != nil {
			t.Errorf("host = %+v", h)
		}
		if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"); !slices.Contains(calls, "status OWN op= change=C2") {
			t.Errorf("calls = %v, want the status of the failed change", calls)
		}
	})
	t.Run("with an update record", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
		e.fleet.failNext["OWN"] = fakeFailure{state: core.StateInterrupted, record: true}
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating")
		if h := got.Hosts[0]; h.OperationID == nil || *h.OperationID != "OP2" {
			t.Fatalf("host = %+v, want the operation the change started", h)
		}
		e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
		e.passes(1)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutSucceeded, "OWN=committed")
	})
}

func TestDriverSteppedUpdateAdvancesOnlyOnTheTarget(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.4.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(1)
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.1", "1.4.0"))
	e.passes(2)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdatePending, "1.4.0", "1.4.0"), updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.1", "1.4.0"))
	e.passes(2)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.4.0", "1.4.0"), updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.1", "1.4.0"))
	e.passes(1)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=committed", "A=updating")
}

func TestDriverTimesOutAHost(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(1)
	e.passes(int(HostTimeout/PollInterval) - 1)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
	e.passes(1)
	got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutDraining, "OWN=updating", "A=skipped")
	if got.Outcome != core.RolloutFailed || got.Error == nil || got.Error.Code != string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted) {
		t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", got)
	}
	_, err := createRollout(e.db, core.RolloutInput{Hosts: []string{"A"}, Version: "1.2.0", Channel: core.ChannelStable}, "1.2.0")
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.RolloutInProgress)
	e.passes(5)
	e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
	e.passes(1)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, "OWN=committed", "A=skipped")
	if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("A"); len(calls) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("calls to A = %v, want none", calls)
	}
}

func TestDriverFollowWaitsThroughAStatusRefusal(t *testing.T) {
	for _, refusal := range []error{
		errcode.New(errcode.Internal, "update: read the update records"),
		errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no update operation OP2"),
	} {
		t.Run(string(errcode.From(refusal).Code), func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newDriverEnv(t)
			r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
			e.start(t)
			e.passes(1)
			e.fleet.mu.Lock()
			e.fleet.statusErr["OWN"] = refusal
			e.fleet.mu.Unlock()
			e.passes(3)
			e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=updating", "A=waiting")
			e.passes(int(HostTimeout/PollInterval) - 3)
			got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutDraining, "OWN=updating", "A=skipped")
			if got.Outcome != core.RolloutFailed || got.Error == nil || got.Error.Code != string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted) {
				t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", got)
			}
			e.fleet.mu.Lock()
			delete(e.fleet.statusErr, "OWN")
			e.fleet.mu.Unlock()
			e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
			e.passes(1)
			e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, "OWN=committed", "A=skipped")
		})
	}
}

func TestDriverCancel(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("while a host updates", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		eng := newEngine(t, e.db, e.clock, "1.2.0")
		if _, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, `{"rolloutId":"`+r.ID+`","force":false}`, r.ID); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		e.passes(3)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutDraining, "OWN=updating", "A=skipped")
		e.fleet.setOp("OWN", updateRecord(core.UpdateCommitted, "1.3.0", "1.3.0"))
		e.passes(1)
		got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutCancelled, "OWN=committed", "A=skipped")
		if got.Error != nil {
			t.Errorf("rollout error = %+v", got.Error)
		}
		if calls := e.fleet.callsOf("A"); len(calls) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("calls to A = %v, want none", calls)
		}
	})
	t.Run("with force", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newDriverEnv(t)
		r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
		e.start(t)
		e.passes(1)
		e.fleet.setOffline("OWN", true)
		e.passes(2)
		eng := newEngine(t, e.db, e.clock, "1.2.0")
		if _, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, `{"rolloutId":"`+r.ID+`","force":true}`, r.ID); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		before := len(e.fleet.callsOf("OWN"))
		e.passes(3)
		e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutCancelled, "OWN=failed", "A=skipped")
		if n := len(e.fleet.callsOf("OWN")); n != before {
			t.Errorf("the driver called OWN %d more times after a forced cancel", n-before)
		}
		mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
	})
}

func TestDriverYieldsToACancelDuringACall(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN", "A")
	eng := newEngine(t, e.db, e.clock, "1.2.0")
	e.fleet.applyErr["OWN"] = errcode.New(errcode.UpdateInProgress, "update operation OP0 is not finished")
	e.fleet.onApply = func() {
		e.fleet.onApply = nil
		if _, err := planApply(eng, ActionRolloutCancel, `{"rolloutId":"`+r.ID+`","force":false}`, r.ID); err != nil {
			t.Error(err)
		}
	}
	e.start(t)
	e.passes(1)
	got := e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutCancelled, "OWN=failed", "A=skipped")
	if got.Error != nil || got.Hosts[0].Error == nil || got.Hosts[0].Error.Code != string(errcode.UpdateInProgress) {
		t.Errorf("rollout error %+v, host error %+v; want the cancel's outcome and the host's refusal", got.Error, got.Hosts[0].Error)
	}
}

func TestDriverStop(t *testing.T) {
	e := newDriverEnv(t)
	d := e.start(t)
	d.Start()
	if n := e.clock.Pending(); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pending timers = %d after two Starts, want 1", n)
	}
	d.Stop()
	if n := e.clock.Pending(); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("pending timers = %d after Stop, want 0", n)
	}
	r := mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.3.0", "1.2.0", "OWN")
	d.Start()
	e.passes(2)
	e.want(t, r.ID, core.RolloutRunning, "OWN=waiting")
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/controller/rollout/driver.go`:

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

const (
	// PollInterval is how often the driver advances the active rollout and
	// polls the updating host's operation.
	PollInterval = 5 * time.Second
	// HostTimeout is how long the driver follows one host's update operation
	// before the rollout drains.
	HostTimeout = 10 * time.Minute
	// callTimeout bounds one dispatcher call.
	callTimeout = 30 * time.Second
	// passSteps bounds the transitions of one pass; a pass that needs more
	// continues at the next tick.
	passSteps = 16
)

// driverPrincipal is the driver's principal on every dispatcher call
// (ADR 0012: system:rollout).
var driverPrincipal = core.Principal{Actor: core.ActorRollout, Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}

var (
	applyRef  = core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionApply, Version: 1}
	statusRef = core.ActionRef{Name: update.ActionStatus, Version: 1}
)

// Dispatcher is what the driver calls on the controller's dispatcher;
// *dispatch.Dispatcher implements it.
type Dispatcher interface {
	Read(ctx context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, input json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error)
	Plan(ctx context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, input json.RawMessage) (core.PlanView, error)
	Apply(ctx context.Context, p core.Principal, hostID string, a core.ActionRef, planID string, opts core.ApplyOptions) (core.ChangeView, error)
}

// DriverConfig wires a Driver.
type DriverConfig struct {
	DB         *store.DB // the controller database
	Dispatcher Dispatcher
	Clock      core.Clock
	Log        *slog.Logger // nil discards
}

// Driver drives the active rollout from its rows, one host at a time (spec
// section 8, Rollout driver). Every transition is one controller write
// transaction that first checks the rollout is still as the driver read it,
// so a cancel committed in between wins and the driver reads it again. A
// restarted controller resumes from the rows.
type Driver struct {
	cfg    DriverConfig
	ctx    context.Context
	cancel context.CancelFunc

	// mu is held for a whole pass, so passes never overlap and Stop waits
	// for a running one.
	mu      sync.Mutex
	timer   core.Timer
	started bool
	stopped bool
}

// NewDriver returns a driver for cfg; DB, Dispatcher and Clock are required.
// Call Start to drive rollouts.
func NewDriver(cfg DriverConfig) (*Driver, error) {
	switch {
	case cfg.DB == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: DriverConfig.DB is required")
	case cfg.Dispatcher == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: DriverConfig.Dispatcher is required")
	case cfg.Clock == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: DriverConfig.Clock is required")
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	return &Driver{cfg: cfg, ctx: ctx, cancel: cancel}, nil
}

// Start drives the active rollout every PollInterval until Stop. A second
// Start, or a Start after Stop, does nothing.
func (d *Driver) Start() {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	if d.started || d.stopped {
		return
	}
	d.started = true
	d.timer = d.cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(PollInterval, d.tick)
}

// Stop ends the loop: it cancels the calls of a running pass and waits for
// the pass to end. The rollout stays in its rows for the next start.
func (d *Driver) Stop() {
	d.cancel()
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.stopped = true
	if d.timer != nil {
		d.timer.Stop()
	}
}

func (d *Driver) tick() {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	if d.stopped {
		return
	}
	if err := d.pass(d.ctx); err != nil {
		d.cfg.Log.Warn("rollout: drive the active rollout", "error", err)
	}
	d.timer = d.cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(PollInterval, d.tick)
}

// pass advances the active rollout until it has to wait.
func (d *Driver) pass(ctx context.Context) error {
	for range passSteps {
		r, ok, err := active(ctx, d.cfg.DB.Read())
		if err != nil || !ok {
			return err
		}
		more, err := d.step(ctx, r)
		if err != nil || !more {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// step makes one transition of r and reports whether another may follow at
// once.
func (d *Driver) step(ctx context.Context, r record) (bool, error) {
	h := r.updating()
	switch {
	case h == nil && r.State == core.RolloutDraining:
		return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error {
			return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, r.Outcome, r.Outcome, r.Error, now)
		})
	case h == nil:
		for _, w := range r.Hosts {
			if w.State == core.RolloutHostWaiting {
				return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, _ time.Time) error {
					return setRolloutHost(ctx, tx, r.ID, w.HostID, core.RolloutHostUpdating, nil)
				})
			}
		}
		return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error {
			return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, core.RolloutSucceeded, "", nil, now)
		})
	case h.OperationID == nil:
		return d.handOff(ctx, r, h)
	default:
		return d.follow(ctx, r, h)
	}
}

// write runs fn in one controller write transaction when rollout r is still
// as the driver read it, and stamps the rollout's updated_at. It reports
// whether fn ran: a cancel committed since the read makes the driver read
// the rollout again instead.
func (d *Driver) write(ctx context.Context, r record, fn func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error) (bool, error) {
	now := d.cfg.Clock.Now().UTC()
	err := d.cfg.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var state, outcome, updated string
		if err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx,
			"SELECT state, coalesce(outcome, ''), updated_at FROM core_rollouts WHERE id = ?", r.ID).
			Scan(&state, &outcome, &updated); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("rollout: read rollout %s: %w", r.ID, err)
		}
		if state != r.State || outcome != r.Outcome || updated != r.UpdatedAt.UTC().Format(timeLayout) {
			return nil
		}
		if err := fn(tx, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", now.Format(timeLayout), r.ID)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	return true, nil
}

// notYet reports whether a dispatcher error only means the host cannot
// answer now: it is offline, another apply of the same plan is running, or
// the call failed in transport or in this process (an error that carries no
// *errcode.Error). errcode.From reports an uncoded error as internal too, but
// an agent that answers internal has answered, and would answer the same
// forever, so a coded internal fails the host.
func notYet(err error) bool {
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) {
		return true
	}
	return coded.Code == errcode.HostOffline || coded.Code == errcode.LockHeld
}

// detailOf is the error a host records for a dispatcher refusal.
func detailOf(err error) *core.ErrorDetail {
	e := errcode.From(err)
	msg := e.Detail
	if msg == "" {
		msg = e.Code.Title()
	}
	return &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(e.Code), Message: msg}
}

// handOff plans core.update.apply on h and applies the plan (spec section 8,
// Rollout driver, steps 1 and 2): the plan id is stored before the apply, so
// after a lost answer or a restart the same plan is applied again and the
// engine returns its one change. A change's update operation is stored for
// follow.
func (d *Driver) handOff(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord) (bool, error) {
	if h.PlanID == nil {
		if r.State == core.RolloutDraining {
			return d.endHost(ctx, r, h, core.RolloutHostSkipped, nil)
		}
		input, err := json.Marshal(core.UpdateApplyInput{Version: r.Version, Channel: r.Channel})
		if err != nil {
			return false, fmt.Errorf("rollout: encode the apply input: %w", err)
		}
		cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, callTimeout)
		plan, err := d.cfg.Dispatcher.Plan(cctx, driverPrincipal, h.HostID, applyRef, input)
		cancel()
		if err != nil {
			return d.refused(ctx, r, h, err)
		}
		return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, _ time.Time) error {
			return d.setPlan(ctx, tx, r.ID, h.HostID, plan.PlanID)
		})
	}
	cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, callTimeout)
	v, err := d.cfg.Dispatcher.Apply(cctx, driverPrincipal, h.HostID, applyRef, *h.PlanID, core.ApplyOptions{})
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		switch errcode.From(err).Code {
		case errcode.PlanExpired, errcode.PlanNotFound, errcode.PlanStateChanged:
			// No change was made of this plan, so nothing is running on
			// the host: plan again, or, while draining, stop here.
			return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, _ time.Time) error {
				return d.setPlan(ctx, tx, r.ID, h.HostID, "")
			})
		}
		return d.refused(ctx, r, h, err)
	}
	switch v.State {
	case core.StateCompleted:
		var tr core.UpdateTransaction
		if err := json.Unmarshal(v.Output, &tr); err != nil || tr.OperationID == "" {
			return d.resolveChange(ctx, r, h, v)
		}
		return d.storeOperation(ctx, r, h, tr.OperationID)
	case core.StateFailed, core.StateInterrupted:
		return d.resolveChange(ctx, r, h, v)
	default:
		return false, nil
	}
}

// resolveChange finds the update operation a change started through
// core.update.status with its change id: none means the change failed
// before any handoff, so the host fails; otherwise the operation is
// followed.
func (d *Driver) resolveChange(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord, v core.ChangeView) (bool, error) {
	st, err := d.status(ctx, h.HostID, core.UpdateStatusInput{ChangeID: v.ID})
	if err != nil {
		return d.refused(ctx, r, h, err)
	}
	if len(st.Transactions) > 0 {
		return d.storeOperation(ctx, r, h, st.Transactions[0].OperationID)
	}
	e := &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(errcode.Internal), Message: fmt.Sprintf("change %s is %s", v.ID, v.State)}
	if v.Error != nil {
		e = &core.ErrorDetail{Code: v.Error.Code, Message: v.Error.Message}
	}
	return d.failHost(ctx, r, h, e)
}

// refused handles a dispatcher error: the host fails unless the error only
// means it cannot answer now.
func (d *Driver) refused(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord, err error) (bool, error) {
	if notYet(err) {
		d.cfg.Log.Debug("rollout: host not ready", "rollout", r.ID, "host", h.HostID, "error", err)
		return false, nil
	}
	return d.failHost(ctx, r, h, detailOf(err))
}

// setPlan stores or, with an empty planID, clears the plan of a host.
func (d *Driver) setPlan(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, id, hostID, planID string) error {
	var plan any
	if planID != "" {
		plan = planID
	}
	_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET plan_id = ? WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = ?",
		plan, id, hostID)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rollout: store the plan of host %s: %w", hostID, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// storeOperation stores the update operation of h; the ten-minute limit
// runs from now.
func (d *Driver) storeOperation(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord, op string) (bool, error) {
	return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error {
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
			"UPDATE core_rollout_hosts SET operation_id = ?, followed_at = ? WHERE rollout_id = ? AND host_id = ?",
			op, now.Format(timeLayout), r.ID, h.HostID)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("rollout: store the operation of host %s: %w", h.HostID, err)
		}
		return nil
	})
}

// status reads core.update.status on hostID.
func (d *Driver) status(ctx context.Context, hostID string, in core.UpdateStatusInput) (core.UpdateStatus, error) {
	input, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdateStatus{}, fmt.Errorf("rollout: encode the status input: %w", err)
	}
	cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, callTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	out, err := d.cfg.Dispatcher.Read(cctx, driverPrincipal, hostID, statusRef, input)
	if err != nil {
		return core.UpdateStatus{}, err
	}
	var st core.UpdateStatus
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &st); err != nil {
		return core.UpdateStatus{}, fmt.Errorf("rollout: decode the update status of host %s: %w", hostID, err)
	}
	return st, nil
}

// follow polls h's update operation (spec section 8, Rollout driver, step
// 3): the host commits when the newest record is committed at the
// rollout's version, and fails when any record failed or rolled back. Any
// error of the status read is "not yet", a refusal included: the operation
// may still be running, and the host fails only once it is over. While a
// running rollout's host is not done HostTimeout after its operation was
// stored, the rollout drains.
func (d *Driver) follow(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord) (bool, error) {
	st, err := d.status(ctx, h.HostID, core.UpdateStatusInput{OperationID: *h.OperationID})
	if err == nil {
		done, failure := operationResult(st.Transactions, r.Version)
		switch {
		case failure != nil:
			return d.failHost(ctx, r, h, failure)
		case done:
			return d.endHost(ctx, r, h, core.RolloutHostCommitted, nil)
		}
	} else {
		d.cfg.Log.Debug("rollout: operation status unavailable", "rollout", r.ID, "host", h.HostID, "error", err)
	}
	if r.State != core.RolloutRunning || d.cfg.Clock.Now().Sub(h.FollowedAt) < HostTimeout {
		return false, nil
	}
	e := &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted),
		Message: fmt.Sprintf("host %s: its update did not finish within %s", h.HostID, HostTimeout)}
	return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error {
		if err := skipWaiting(ctx, tx, r.ID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, core.RolloutDraining, core.RolloutFailed, e, now)
	})
}

// operationResult reads an operation's records, newest first: failure is
// set when any record failed or rolled back, and done when the newest
// record committed the rollout's version. A committed intermediate step of
// a stepped update is neither.
func operationResult(trs []core.UpdateTransaction, version string) (done bool, failure *core.ErrorDetail) {
	for _, tr := range trs {
		if tr.State == core.UpdateFailed || tr.State == core.UpdateRolledBack {
			if tr.Error != nil {
				return false, tr.Error
			}
			return false, &core.ErrorDetail{Code: string(errcode.UpdateInterrupted),
				Message: fmt.Sprintf("update transaction %s is %s", tr.ID, tr.State)}
		}
	}
	return len(trs) > 0 && trs[0].State == core.UpdateCommitted && sameVersion(trs[0].ToVersion, version), nil
}

// sameVersion reports whether two versions are equal by semantic version
// precedence; a version that does not parse equals only the same string.
func sameVersion(a, b string) bool {
	if a == b {
		return true
	}
	va, errA := update.ParseVersion(a)
	vb, errB := update.ParseVersion(b)
	return errA == nil && errB == nil && va.Compare(vb) == 0
}

// failHost records h failed with e. A running rollout fails with it at once
// and skips its waiting hosts; a draining one ends in its outcome.
func (d *Driver) failHost(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord, e *core.ErrorDetail) (bool, error) {
	return d.endHost(ctx, r, h, core.RolloutHostFailed, e)
}

// endHost records the end of h's turn. A draining rollout then ends in its
// outcome; a running one fails when the host failed and otherwise moves on
// to its next host.
func (d *Driver) endHost(ctx context.Context, r record, h *hostRecord, state string, e *core.ErrorDetail) (bool, error) {
	return d.write(ctx, r, func(tx *sql.Tx, now time.Time) error {
		if err := setRolloutHost(ctx, tx, r.ID, h.HostID, state, e); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		switch {
		case r.State == core.RolloutDraining:
			if err := skipWaiting(ctx, tx, r.ID); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, r.Outcome, r.Outcome, r.Error, now)
		case state == core.RolloutHostFailed:
			if err := skipWaiting(ctx, tx, r.ID); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			re := &core.ErrorDetail{Code: e.Code, Message: fmt.Sprintf("host %s: %s", h.HostID, e.Message)}
			return setRollout(ctx, tx, r.ID, core.RolloutFailed, core.RolloutFailed, re, now)
		}
		return nil
	})
}
```

```go
package daemon

import (
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"errors"
	"math/big"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// testAuthority is a stand-in agent root for tests that need certificates
// but no controller.
type testAuthority struct {
	cert *x509.Certificate
	key  ed25519.PrivateKey
}

func newTestAuthority(t *testing.T) *testAuthority {
	t.Helper()
	pub, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(1), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "test agent root"},
		NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour),
		IsCA: true, BasicConstraintsValid: true, KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageCertSign,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, pub, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return &testAuthority{cert: cert, key: key}
}

// credentials returns hostID's credentials with a fresh key, as an
// enrollment or a renewal produces them.
func (a *testAuthority) credentials(t *testing.T, hostID string) *Credentials {
	t.Helper()
	return a.issue(t, transport.HostURI(hostID), x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth)
}

// issue returns credentials whose certificate names uri and has usage eku.
func (a *testAuthority) issue(t *testing.T, uri string, eku x509.ExtKeyUsage) *Credentials {
	t.Helper()
	pub, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	serial, err := rand.Int(rand.Reader, new(big.Int).Lsh(big.NewInt(1), 128))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(uri)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: serial, NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(12 * time.Hour),
		ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{eku}, URIs: []*url.URL{u},
		KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, a.cert, pub, a.key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return &Credentials{
		Key: key, Cert: cert, CA: a.cert,
		Controller: Endpoint{Address: "127.0.0.1:7443", SAN: transport.ControllerURI("C1")},
	}
}

// sameCredentials reports whether got holds want's key and certificate.
func sameCredentials(got, want *Credentials) bool {
	return got != nil && got.Key.Equal(want.Key) && got.Cert.Equal(want.Cert) && got.CA.Equal(want.CA) &&
		got.Controller == want.Controller
}

// generations lists the gen-<n> entries of dir.
func generations(t *testing.T, dir string) []string {
	t.Helper()
	entries, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out []string
	for _, e := range entries {
		if e.Name() != currentLink {
			out = append(out, e.Name())
		}
	}
	return out
}

func TestCredentialsRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
	if _, err := LoadCredentials(dir); !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
		t.Fatalf("LoadCredentials of an empty directory = %v, want ErrNotEnrolled", err)
	}
	ca := newTestAuthority(t)
	first := ca.credentials(t, "H1")
	n, err := SaveGeneration(dir, first)
	if err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("SaveGeneration = %d, %v; want generation 1", n, err)
	}
	got, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
	if err != nil || !sameCredentials(got, first) || got.Generation != 1 || got.HostID() != "H1" {
		t.Fatalf("LoadCredentials = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if c := got.TLSCertificate(); c.Leaf != got.Cert || len(c.Certificate) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("TLSCertificate = %+v", c)
	}
	for path, mode := range map[string]os.FileMode{
		dir: 0o700, filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1"): 0o700, filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1", keyFile): 0o600,
		filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1", certFile): 0o600, filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1", caFile): 0o600,
		filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1", controllerFile): 0o600,
	} {
		if st, err := os.Stat(path); err != nil || st.Mode().Perm() != mode {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v, %v; want mode %v", path, st.Mode(), err, mode)
		}
	}
	second := ca.credentials(t, "H1")
	if n, err := SaveGeneration(dir, second); err != nil || n != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("second SaveGeneration = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	if got, err := LoadCredentials(dir); err != nil || !sameCredentials(got, second) || got.Generation != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("LoadCredentials after the second generation = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	// The previous generation stays until a handshake with the new one.
	if gens := generations(t, dir); !slices.Equal(gens, []string{"gen-1", "gen-2"}) {
		t.Errorf("generations = %v, want both", gens)
	}
	if err := PruneGenerations(dir, 2); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if gens := generations(t, dir); !slices.Equal(gens, []string{"gen-2"}) {
		t.Errorf("generations after pruning = %v, want gen-2 alone", gens)
	}
}

func TestSaveGenerationRefusesBadCredentials(t *testing.T) {
	ca := newTestAuthority(t)
	other := newTestAuthority(t)
	cases := map[string]func(c *Credentials){
		"another key":       func(c *Credentials) { c.Key = ca.credentials(t, "H1").Key },
		"another authority": func(c *Credentials) { c.CA = other.cert },
		"no address":        func(c *Credentials) { c.Controller.Address = "" },
		"address port":      func(c *Credentials) { c.Controller.Address = "controller" },
		"controller SAN":    func(c *Credentials) { c.Controller.SAN = "darkpanel://host/C1" },
		"server usage": func(c *Credentials) {
			*c = *ca.issue(t, transport.HostURI("H1"), x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth)
		},
		"no host id": func(c *Credentials) {
			*c = *ca.issue(t, transport.ControllerURI("H1"), x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth)
		},
	}
	for name, spoil := range cases {
		dir := t.TempDir()
		c := ca.credentials(t, "H1")
		spoil(c)
		if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, c); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: SaveGeneration accepted it", name)
		}
		if _, err := LoadCredentials(dir); !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
			t.Errorf("%s: a refused generation became current: %v", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestLoadCredentialsRefusesAForeignCurrent(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	ca := newTestAuthority(t)
	if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, ca.credentials(t, "H1")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, target := range []string{"../gen-1", "/etc", "gen-x"} {
		if err := os.Remove(filepath.Join(dir, currentLink)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := os.Symlink(target, filepath.Join(dir, currentLink)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := LoadCredentials(dir); err == nil || errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
			t.Errorf("current -> %s: LoadCredentials = %v, want a refusal", target, err)
		}
	}
}

// Spec Verification "credentials": an enrollment or a renewal interrupted
// at any step before, during or after the switch of current leaves a
// complete old or a complete new generation, and a restarted agent can
// write the next one.
func TestSaveGenerationInterrupted(t *testing.T) {
	ca := newTestAuthority(t)
	crash := errors.New("power cut")
	switched := slices.Index(generationSteps, stepRename) + 1
	for _, renewal := range []bool{false, true} {
		for i, step := range generationSteps {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
			var old *Credentials
			if renewal {
				old = ca.credentials(t, "H1")
				if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, old); err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
			}
			next := ca.credentials(t, "H1")
			_, err := saveGeneration(dir, next, func(s string) error {
				if s == step {
					return crash
				}
				return nil
			})
			if !errors.Is(err, crash) {
				t.Fatalf("renewal %v, step %s: saveGeneration = %v, want the crash", renewal, step, err)
			}
			// The restarted agent reads one complete generation.
			got, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
			switch {
			case i >= switched:
				if err != nil || !sameCredentials(got, next) {
					t.Errorf("renewal %v, crash at %s: loaded %+v, %v; want the new generation", renewal, step, got, err)
				}
			case renewal:
				if err != nil || !sameCredentials(got, old) {
					t.Errorf("renewal %v, crash at %s: loaded %+v, %v; want the old generation", renewal, step, got, err)
				}
			default:
				if !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
					t.Errorf("enrollment, crash at %s: LoadCredentials = %v, want ErrNotEnrolled", step, err)
				}
			}
			// It enrolls or renews again, and the first handshake with the
			// result prunes everything else.
			again := ca.credentials(t, "H1")
			n, err := SaveGeneration(dir, again)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("renewal %v, crash at %s: SaveGeneration after the restart: %v", renewal, step, err)
			}
			if got, err := LoadCredentials(dir); err != nil || !sameCredentials(got, again) || got.Generation != n {
				t.Fatalf("renewal %v, crash at %s: loaded %+v, %v after the restart", renewal, step, got, err)
			}
			if err := PruneGenerations(dir, n); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if gens := generations(t, dir); !slices.Equal(gens, []string{generationName(n)}) {
				t.Errorf("renewal %v, crash at %s: left %v after pruning", renewal, step, gens)
			}
		}
	}
}
```
