- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/daemon/creds.go`:

```go
// Package daemon is darkpanel agent run (spec sections 2 to 5): the agent's
// credential generations, its enrollment and certificate renewal, and the
// daemon that holds the agent lock, serves the agent socket and keeps one
// mTLS session to the controller.
package daemon

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"net"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// The files of one generation and the names in AgentTLSDir.
const (
	keyFile        = "agent.key"
	certFile       = "agent.crt"
	caFile         = "ca.crt"
	controllerFile = "controller.json"
	currentLink    = "current"
	currentTemp    = "current.tmp"
)

// ErrNotEnrolled is LoadCredentials' answer when AgentTLSDir has no current
// generation.
var ErrNotEnrolled = errors.New("daemon: this agent is not enrolled")

var generationPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^gen-([1-9][0-9]{0,8})$`)

// Endpoint is controller.json: where the agent dials and the URI SAN the
// controller's certificate must carry.
type Endpoint struct {
	Address string `json:"address"`
	SAN     string `json:"san"`
}

// Credentials is one generation: the agent's key and certificate, the agent
// root it trusts and the controller it dials.
type Credentials struct {
	Generation int // 0 until saved
	Key        ed25519.PrivateKey
	Cert       *x509.Certificate
	CA         *x509.Certificate
	Controller Endpoint
}

// HostID is the host id the certificate names.
func (c *Credentials) HostID() string {
	id, _ := transport.HostIDOf(c.Cert)
	return id
}

// TLSCertificate is the certificate and key as a TLS client certificate.
func (c *Credentials) TLSCertificate() tls.Certificate {
	return tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{c.Cert.Raw}, PrivateKey: c.Key, Leaf: c.Cert}
}

// check refuses credentials the agent could not use: a certificate that is
// not an agent certificate of the key, signed by CA, or a controller.json
// that names no dialable address or no controller SAN. Validity dates are
// the controller's to check.
func (c *Credentials) check() error {
	if len(c.Key) != ed25519.PrivateKeySize || c.Cert == nil || c.CA == nil {
		return errors.New("daemon: credentials need an Ed25519 key, a certificate and the agent root")
	}
	if _, err := transport.HostIDOf(c.Cert); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: agent certificate: %w", err)
	}
	pub, ok := c.Cert.PublicKey.(ed25519.PublicKey)
	if !ok || !pub.Equal(c.Key.Public()) {
		return errors.New("daemon: the agent certificate is not for the agent key")
	}
	if !slices.Contains(c.Cert.ExtKeyUsage, x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth) {
		return errors.New("daemon: the agent certificate lacks the clientAuth usage")
	}
	if err := c.Cert.CheckSignatureFrom(c.CA); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: the agent certificate is not signed by the agent root: %w", err)
	}
	if _, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(c.Controller.Address); err != nil || c.Controller.Address == "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: controller address %q is not host:port", c.Controller.Address)
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(c.Controller.SAN, transport.ControllerURI("")) || c.Controller.SAN == transport.ControllerURI("") {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %q is not a controller SAN", c.Controller.SAN)
	}
	return nil
}

// LoadCredentials reads the current generation of dir (Paths.AgentTLSDir).
// It resolves current once and reads all four files from that generation, so
// a concurrent SaveGeneration never mixes two generations. Without current it
// answers ErrNotEnrolled.
func LoadCredentials(dir string) (*Credentials, error) {
	target, err := os.Readlink(filepath.Join(dir, currentLink))
	if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, ErrNotEnrolled
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: read the current credentials: %w", err)
	}
	m := generationPattern.FindStringSubmatch(target)
	if m == nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s points to %q, not to a generation", filepath.Join(dir, currentLink), target)
	}
	n, _ := strconv.Atoi(m[1])
	gen := filepath.Join(dir, target)
	read := func(name string) []byte {
		if err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		var data []byte
		data, err = os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(gen, name)) //nolint:gosec // G304: gen matched generationPattern under Paths.AgentTLSDir
		return data
	}
	keyPEM, certPEM, caPEM, endpoint := read(keyFile), read(certFile), read(caFile), read(controllerFile)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: read generation %d: %w", n, err)
	}
	c := &Credentials{Generation: n}
	if c.Key, err = parseKey(keyPEM); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generation %d: %s: %w", n, keyFile, err)
	}
	if c.Cert, err = parseCertificate(certPEM); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generation %d: %s: %w", n, certFile, err)
	}
	if c.CA, err = parseCertificate(caPEM); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generation %d: %s: %w", n, caFile, err)
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(endpoint))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(&c.Controller); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generation %d: %s: %w", n, controllerFile, err)
	}
	if err := c.check(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generation %d: %w", n, err)
	}
	return c, nil
}

func parseKey(data []byte) (ed25519.PrivateKey, error) {
	block, rest := pem.Decode(data)
	if block == nil || block.Type != "PRIVATE KEY" || len(bytes.TrimSpace(rest)) != 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("not one PEM PRIVATE KEY block")
	}
	key, err := x509.ParsePKCS8PrivateKey(block.Bytes)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	k, ok := key.(ed25519.PrivateKey)
	if !ok {
		return nil, errors.New("not an Ed25519 key")
	}
	return k, nil
}

// parseCertificate reads one PEM CERTIFICATE block.
func parseCertificate(data []byte) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	block, rest := pem.Decode(data)
	if block == nil || block.Type != "CERTIFICATE" || len(bytes.TrimSpace(rest)) != 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("not one PEM CERTIFICATE block")
	}
	return x509.ParseCertificate(block.Bytes)
}

// The steps of SaveGeneration, in order; tests interrupt it before each.
const (
	stepCreate     = "create the generation directory"
	stepKey        = "write " + keyFile
	stepCert       = "write " + certFile
	stepCA         = "write " + caFile
	stepController = "write " + controllerFile
	stepSyncGen    = "sync the generation directory"
	stepLink       = "link " + currentTemp
	stepRename     = "rename " + currentTemp + " over " + currentLink
	stepSyncDir    = "sync the credentials directory"
)

var generationSteps = []string{
	stepCreate, stepKey, stepCert, stepCA, stepController, stepSyncGen, stepLink, stepRename, stepSyncDir,
}

// SaveGeneration writes c as a new generation of dir and makes it current
// (spec section 2): each file is written and fsynced, then the generation
// directory, then current is replaced atomically through current.tmp and
// dir is fsynced. A crash at any point leaves a complete old or a complete
// new generation; a partly written one is never current and is removed by
// PruneGenerations. It returns the new generation's number, which is
// greater than every generation in dir, complete or not.
func SaveGeneration(dir string, c *Credentials) (int, error) {
	return saveGeneration(dir, c, func(string) error { return nil })
}

func saveGeneration(dir string, c *Credentials, before func(step string) error) (int, error) {
	if err := c.check(); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	keyDER, err := x509.MarshalPKCS8PrivateKey(c.Key)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("daemon: encode the agent key: %w", err)
	}
	endpoint, err := json.Marshal(c.Controller)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("daemon: encode %s: %w", controllerFile, err)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("daemon: create %s: %w", dir, err)
	}
	gens, err := listGenerations(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	n := 1
	if len(gens) > 0 {
		n = slices.Max(gens) + 1
	}
	gen := filepath.Join(dir, generationName(n))
	files := map[string][]byte{
		stepKey:        pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "PRIVATE KEY", Bytes: keyDER}),
		stepCert:       pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: c.Cert.Raw}),
		stepCA:         pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: c.CA.Raw}),
		stepController: endpoint,
	}
	names := map[string]string{stepKey: keyFile, stepCert: certFile, stepCA: caFile, stepController: controllerFile}
	current, temp := filepath.Join(dir, currentLink), filepath.Join(dir, currentTemp)
	for _, step := range generationSteps {
		if err := before(step); err != nil {
			return 0, err
		}
		switch step {
		case stepCreate:
			err = os.Mkdir(gen, 0o700)
		case stepKey, stepCert, stepCA, stepController:
			err = writeSynced(filepath.Join(gen, names[step]), files[step])
		case stepSyncGen:
			err = syncDir(gen)
		case stepLink:
			if err = os.Remove(temp); errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
				err = nil
			}
			if err == nil {
				err = os.Symlink(generationName(n), temp)
			}
		case stepRename:
			err = os.Rename(temp, current)
		case stepSyncDir:
			err = syncDir(dir)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("daemon: save generation %d: %s: %w", n, step, err)
		}
	}
	c.Generation = n
	return n, nil
}

// PruneGenerations deletes every generation of dir except keep, and a
// leftover current.tmp. The daemon calls it after the first successful
// handshake with generation keep.
func PruneGenerations(dir string, keep int) error {
	gens, err := listGenerations(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var errs []error
	for _, n := range gens {
		if n != keep {
			errs = append(errs, os.RemoveAll(filepath.Join(dir, generationName(n))))
		}
	}
	if err := os.Remove(filepath.Join(dir, currentTemp)); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		errs = append(errs, err)
	}
	if err := errors.Join(errs...); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: prune credential generations: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func generationName(n int) string { return "gen-" + strconv.Itoa(n) }

// listGenerations returns the numbers of the generation directories in
// dir, complete or not.
func listGenerations(dir string) ([]int, error) {
	entries, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: list %s: %w", dir, err)
	}
	var out []int
	for _, e := range entries {
		if m := generationPattern.FindStringSubmatch(e.Name()); m != nil {
			n, _ := strconv.Atoi(m[1])
			out = append(out, n)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// writeSynced creates path with mode 0600, writes data and fsyncs it.
func writeSynced(path string, data []byte) error {
	f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o600) //nolint:gosec // G304: a file of a new generation under Paths.AgentTLSDir
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := f.Write(data); err != nil {
		_ = f.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := f.Sync(); err != nil {
		_ = f.Close()
		return err
	}
	return f.Close()
}

func syncDir(dir string) error {
	d, err := os.Open(dir) //nolint:gosec // G304: dir is Paths.AgentTLSDir or one of its generations
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	err = d.Sync()
	if cerr := d.Close(); err == nil {
		err = cerr
	}
	return err
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/agent/socket/socket_test.go`:

```go
package socket

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

// socketPath returns a socket path in a new directory under os.TempDir,
// short enough for a Unix socket.
func socketPath(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	dir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "dp")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(dir) })
	return filepath.Join(dir, "run", "agent.sock")
}

// serve listens on path with allow and serves a handler that counts its
// calls until the test ends.
func serve(t *testing.T, path string, allow func(uint32) bool) *atomic.Int32 {
	t.Helper()
	var calls atomic.Int32
	h := http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		calls.Add(1)
		_, _ = io.WriteString(w, "hello")
	})
	s, err := Listen(Config{Path: path, Handler: h, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), Allow: allow})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		if err := s.Serve(); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Serve = %v", err)
		}
	}()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Close = %v", err)
		}
		wg.Wait()
	})
	return &calls
}

// get sends one request over the socket at path.
func get(path string) (string, error) {
	c := &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Second, Transport: &http.Transport{
		DialContext: func(ctx context.Context, _, _ string) (net.Conn, error) {
			var d net.Dialer
			return d.DialContext(ctx, "unix", path)
		},
	}}
	resp, err := c.Get("http://agent/agent/v1/hello")
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	return string(body), err
}

func TestSocketAdmitsAllowedPeers(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	var seen atomic.Int64
	seen.Store(-1)
	calls := serve(t, path, func(uid uint32) bool {
		seen.Store(int64(uid))
		return true
	})
	if body, err := get(path); err != nil || body != "hello" {
		t.Fatalf("request = %q, %v; want served", body, err)
	}
	if got := seen.Load(); got != int64(os.Getuid()) {
		t.Errorf("Allow saw uid %d, want this process's %d", got, os.Getuid())
	}
	if calls.Load() != 1 {
		t.Errorf("handler ran %d times, want 1", calls.Load())
	}
}

func TestSocketRefusesOtherPeers(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	calls := serve(t, path, func(uint32) bool { return false })
	for range 3 {
		if body, err := get(path); err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("a refused peer was served %q", body)
		}
	}
	if calls.Load() != 0 {
		t.Errorf("handler ran %d times for refused peers", calls.Load())
	}
}

func TestSocketAdmitsRootByDefault(t *testing.T) {
	if !rootOnly(0) || rootOnly(1) || rootOnly(1000) {
		t.Error("rootOnly must admit uid 0 alone")
	}
	path := socketPath(t)
	serve(t, path, nil)
	_, err := get(path)
	if root := os.Getuid() == 0; (err == nil) != root {
		t.Errorf("as uid %d the request answered %v", os.Getuid(), err)
	}
}

func TestListenModes(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	// A socket left by an agent that crashed is replaced, and a directory
	// created with a wider mode is narrowed.
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G301: the wider mode Listen must narrow
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	old, err := net.Listen("unix", path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	old.(*net.UnixListener).SetUnlinkOnClose(false)
	_ = old.Close()
	serve(t, path, func(uint32) bool { return true })
	for p, want := range map[string]os.FileMode{filepath.Dir(path): os.ModeDir | 0o700, path: os.ModeSocket | 0o600} {
		st, err := os.Lstat(p)
		if err != nil || st.Mode() != want {
			t.Errorf("%s: mode %v, %v; want %v", p, st.Mode(), err, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := get(path); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("request over the replaced socket = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestListenRefusesAnotherFile(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("not a socket"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Listen(Config{Path: path, Handler: http.NotFoundHandler(), Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Listen replaced a regular file")
	}
	if data, err := os.ReadFile(path); err != nil || string(data) != "not a socket" { //nolint:gosec // G304: a test's temporary path
		t.Errorf("the file is now %q, %v", data, err)
	}
}

func TestCloseRemovesTheSocket(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	s, err := Listen(Config{Path: path, Handler: http.NotFoundHandler(), Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- s.Serve() }()
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Serve after Close = %v, want nil", err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Lstat(path); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Errorf("the socket is still there: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestListenWithoutALog(t *testing.T) {
	path := socketPath(t)
	s, err := Listen(Config{Path: path, Handler: http.NotFoundHandler(), Allow: func(uint32) bool { return false }})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- s.Serve() }()
	// A refused peer is logged; without a logger the refusal is discarded.
	if _, err := get(path); err == nil {
		t.Error("a refused peer was served")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Serve after Close = %v, want nil", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/socket/socket.go`:

```go
// Package socket is the agent's break-glass socket (spec section 5, R31):
// a Unix socket in a directory only root can enter, serving the agent API
// to local root alone, whether or not the controller is reachable.
package socket

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"
)

// Config is what Listen needs.
type Config struct {
	Path    string       // Paths.AgentSocket
	Handler http.Handler // api.NewSocketHandler over the agent runtime
	Log     *slog.Logger // nil discards
	// Allow decides whether a peer whose SO_PEERCRED uid is uid may use the
	// socket; nil admits uid 0 alone. Tests that do not run as root replace
	// it.
	Allow func(uid uint32) bool
}

// Server serves the agent API on the socket.
type Server struct {
	srv *http.Server
	ln  *peerListener
}

// Listen creates the socket's directory with mode 0700 (narrowing an
// existing one), replaces a socket an earlier agent left behind, and listens
// with mode 0600. The caller holds the agent lock, so no other agent serves
// the old socket.
func Listen(cfg Config) (*Server, error) {
	if cfg.Allow == nil {
		cfg.Allow = rootOnly
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	dir := filepath.Dir(cfg.Path)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: create %s: %w", dir, err)
	}
	if err := os.Chmod(dir, 0o700); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G302: a directory needs its search bit
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: %w", err)
	}
	st, err := os.Lstat(cfg.Path)
	switch {
	case err == nil && st.Mode().Type() != fs.ModeSocket:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: %s exists and is not a socket", cfg.Path)
	case err == nil:
		if err := os.Remove(cfg.Path); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: remove the old socket: %w", err)
		}
	case !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist):
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: %w", err)
	}
	ln, err := net.Listen("unix", cfg.Path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: %w", err)
	}
	// The directory already keeps everyone but root out; the mode keeps the
	// socket private should the directory ever be shared.
	if err := os.Chmod(cfg.Path, 0o600); err != nil {
		_ = ln.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("socket: %w", err)
	}
	return &Server{
		srv: &http.Server{Handler: cfg.Handler, ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second, ErrorLog: slog.NewLogLogger(cfg.Log.Handler(), slog.LevelWarn)},
		ln:  &peerListener{Listener: ln, allow: cfg.Allow, log: cfg.Log},
	}, nil
}

// Serve serves until Close and then returns nil.
func (s *Server) Serve() error {
	if err := s.srv.Serve(s.ln); !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
		return fmt.Errorf("socket: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// Close stops serving, closes every connection and removes the socket.
func (s *Server) Close() error {
	return s.srv.Close()
}

func rootOnly(uid uint32) bool { return uid == 0 }

// peerListener hands over only connections whose peer Allow admits; it
// closes the others before a byte is read from them.
type peerListener struct {
	net.Listener
	allow func(uint32) bool
	log   *slog.Logger
}

func (l *peerListener) Accept() (net.Conn, error) {
	for {
		c, err := l.Listener.Accept()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		uid, err := peerUID(c)
		switch {
		case err != nil:
			l.log.Warn("socket: refused a peer whose credentials cannot be read", "error", err)
		case !l.allow(uid):
			l.log.Warn("socket: refused a peer that is not root", "uid", uid)
		default:
			return c, nil
		}
		_ = c.Close()
	}
}

// peerUID is the uid the kernel recorded for the peer of c when it
// connected (SO_PEERCRED).
func peerUID(c net.Conn) (uint32, error) {
	uc, ok := c.(*net.UnixConn)
	if !ok {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("socket: %T is not a Unix connection", c)
	}
	raw, err := uc.SyscallConn()
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	var cred *unix.Ucred
	var credErr error
	if err := raw.Control(func(fd uintptr) {
		cred, credErr = unix.GetsockoptUcred(int(fd), unix.SOL_SOCKET, unix.SO_PEERCRED)
	}); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if credErr != nil {
		return 0, credErr
	}
	return cred.Uid, nil
}
```

When the tests run as root, `TestSocketAdmitsRootByDefault` takes its root branch. To also see the default refuse a non-root peer, run the compiled tests as uid 65534 from a directory that user can write; this needs root and `setpriv`, so it is skipped without them:
```bash
if [ "$(id -u)" = 0 ] && command -v setpriv >/dev/null; then
	go test -c -race -o "$TMPDIR/socket.test" ./internal/agent/socket/
	d=$(mktemp -d /var/tmp/dpsock.XXXX) && cp "$TMPDIR/socket.test" "$d/" && chmod 777 "$d"
	(cd "$d" && setpriv --reuid=65534 --regid=65534 --clear-groups env TMPDIR="$d" HOME="$d" ./socket.test)
	rm -rf "$d" "$TMPDIR/socket.test"
fi
```
Expected: `PASS` when it runs; nothing when it is skipped.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

The test controller stands in for P8's agent listener: the real `fleet.Authority` behind `transport.Handshake`, answering as P8 must (problem details carrying the `errcode` code). Its renewal handler and `renewErr` serve Task 28.

Create `internal/agent/daemon/controller_test.go`:

```go
package daemon

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/secret"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// testController is the agent listener of a controller, built from the real
// fleet.Authority behind transport.Handshake: enrollment connections serve
// one POST transport.EnrollPath, agent connections become controller
// sessions that serve POST transport.RenewPath.
type testController struct {
	t     *testing.T
	db    *store.DB
	clock *coretest.FakeClock
	auth  *fleet.Authority
	ln    net.Listener
	addr  string
	ctx   context.Context
	stop  context.CancelFunc
	wg    sync.WaitGroup

	mu       sync.Mutex
	sessions []*agentSession // every accepted agent session, oldest first
	refused  int             // handshakes that failed
	// rewrite, when set, alters an enrollment answer before it is sent.
	rewrite func(req transport.EnrollRequest, resp *transport.EnrollResponse)
	// renewErr, when set, refuses every renewal.
	renewErr error
	renewals int // renewal requests received
}

// agentSession is one accepted agent connection.
type agentSession struct {
	hostID, serial string
	sess           *transport.Session
}

// newTestController starts a controller whose authority's clock reads start;
// it stops when the test ends.
func newTestController(t *testing.T, start time.Time) *testController {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	db, err := store.Open(ctx, filepath.Join(dir, "controller.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	set, err := store.CoreMigrations(store.SideController)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := store.Migrate(ctx, db, store.MigrateOptions{
		Side: store.SideController, BinaryVersion: "test", BackupRoot: filepath.Join(dir, "backups"), Keep: 5, Now: start,
	}, set, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	key := make([]byte, secret.KeySize)
	if _, err := rand.Read(key); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sealer, err := secret.NewSealer(key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	clock := coretest.NewFakeClock(start)
	auth, err := fleet.LoadOrCreateAuthority(ctx, db, sealer, clock)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ln, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c := &testController{t: t, db: db, clock: clock, auth: auth, ln: ln, addr: ln.Addr().String()}
	c.ctx, c.stop = context.WithCancel(ctx)
	c.wg.Add(1)
	go c.accept()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		c.stop()
		_ = ln.Close()
		c.mu.Lock()
		for _, s := range c.sessions {
			_ = s.sess.Close()
		}
		c.mu.Unlock()
		c.wg.Wait()
		_ = db.Close()
	})
	return c
}

func (c *testController) accept() {
	defer c.wg.Done()
	for {
		raw, err := c.ln.Accept()
		if err != nil {
			return
		}
		c.wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer c.wg.Done()
			c.serve(raw)
		}()
	}
}

func (c *testController) serve(raw net.Conn) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.ctx, 10*time.Second)
	peer, err := transport.Handshake(ctx, raw, transport.ServerConfig{
		Certificate: c.auth.GetCertificate, AgentRoots: c.auth.Pool(), CheckPeer: c.auth.CheckPeer,
	})
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		c.mu.Lock()
		c.refused++
		c.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	if peer.Protocol == transport.ALPNEnroll {
		c.serveEnroll(peer.Conn)
		return
	}
	if err := c.auth.Handshake(c.ctx, peer.HostID, peer.Serial); err != nil {
		c.t.Errorf("controller: record the handshake: %v", err)
	}
	sess, err := transport.ControllerSession(peer.Conn)
	if err != nil {
		c.t.Errorf("controller: %v", err)
		return
	}
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.sessions = append(c.sessions, &agentSession{hostID: peer.HostID, serial: peer.Serial, sess: sess})
	c.mu.Unlock()
	srv := &http.Server{Handler: c.renewHandler(peer.HostID), ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second}
	_ = srv.Serve(sess)
}

// serveEnroll answers the one request of an enrollment connection.
func (c *testController) serveEnroll(conn net.Conn) {
	defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
	req, err := http.ReadRequest(bufio.NewReader(conn))
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	rec := newRecorder()
	var in transport.EnrollRequest
	switch {
	case req.Method != http.MethodPost || req.URL.Path != transport.EnrollPath:
		rec.problem(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no such route"))
	case json.NewDecoder(req.Body).Decode(&in) != nil:
		rec.problem(errcode.New(errcode.InvalidJSON, "bad body"))
	default:
		e, err := c.auth.Enroll(c.ctx, in.Token, []byte(in.CSR))
		if err != nil {
			rec.problem(err)
			break
		}
		out := transport.EnrollResponse{Certificate: string(e.Certificate), CA: string(e.CA)}
		c.mu.Lock()
		rewrite := c.rewrite
		c.mu.Unlock()
		if rewrite != nil {
			rewrite(in, &out)
		}
		rec.json(out)
	}
	_ = rec.response().Write(conn)
}

// renewHandler serves the renewal of hostID's certificate on its session.
func (c *testController) renewHandler(hostID string) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		rec := newRecorder()
		var in transport.RenewRequest
		switch {
		case r.Method != http.MethodPost || r.URL.Path != transport.RenewPath:
			rec.problem(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "no such route"))
		case json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&in) != nil:
			rec.problem(errcode.New(errcode.InvalidJSON, "bad body"))
		default:
			c.mu.Lock()
			c.renewals++
			refuse := c.renewErr
			c.mu.Unlock()
			if refuse != nil {
				rec.problem(refuse)
				break
			}
			cert, err := c.auth.Renew(r.Context(), hostID, []byte(in.CSR))
			if err != nil {
				rec.problem(err)
				break
			}
			rec.json(transport.RenewResponse{Certificate: string(cert)})
		}
		for k, v := range rec.header {
			w.Header()[k] = v
		}
		w.WriteHeader(rec.status)
		_, _ = w.Write(rec.body.Bytes())
	})
}

// recorder builds one answer, as P8's endpoints answer: JSON, or problem
// details carrying the errcode code.
type recorder struct {
	status int
	header http.Header
	body   bytes.Buffer
}

func newRecorder() *recorder { return &recorder{status: http.StatusOK, header: http.Header{}} }

func (r *recorder) json(v any) {
	r.header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	_ = json.NewEncoder(&r.body).Encode(v)
}

func (r *recorder) problem(err error) {
	code, detail := errcode.Internal, "internal error"
	var e *errcode.Error
	if errors.As(err, &e) {
		code, detail = e.Code, e.Detail
	}
	r.status = code.Status()
	r.header.Set("Content-Type", "application/problem+json")
	_ = json.NewEncoder(&r.body).Encode(map[string]any{
		"type": "about:blank", "title": code.Title(), "status": r.status, "detail": detail, "code": code,
	})
}

func (r *recorder) response() *http.Response {
	r.header.Set("Content-Length", strconv.Itoa(r.body.Len()))
	return &http.Response{
		StatusCode: r.status, ProtoMajor: 1, ProtoMinor: 1, Header: r.header,
		Body: io.NopCloser(bytes.NewReader(r.body.Bytes())), ContentLength: int64(r.body.Len()), Close: true,
	}
}

// addHost inserts a host row in state.
func (c *testController) addHost(id, state string) {
	c.t.Helper()
	if _, err := c.db.Writer().ExecContext(context.Background(),
		`INSERT INTO core_hosts (id, name, created_at, state) VALUES (?, ?, '2026-10-19T00:00:00.000000000Z', ?)`,
		id, "host-"+id, state); err != nil {
		c.t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// token issues hostID an enrollment token naming this controller.
func (c *testController) token(hostID string) string {
	c.t.Helper()
	var token string
	err := c.db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		token, _, err = c.auth.IssueToken(context.Background(), tx, hostID, c.addr)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		c.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return token
}

// hostState is the host's core_hosts state.
func (c *testController) hostState(hostID string) string {
	c.t.Helper()
	var state string
	if err := c.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT state FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, hostID).Scan(&state); err != nil {
		c.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return state
}

// agentSessions returns a copy of the accepted sessions.
func (c *testController) agentSessions() []*agentSession {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]*agentSession(nil), c.sessions...)
}
```

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"math/big"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

func wantCode(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) {
	t.Helper()
	var e *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &e) || e.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
}

// Register row 42: an enrollment against the real authority behind
// transport.Handshake, then an mTLS session with what it saved.
func TestEnrollEndToEnd(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	token := ctrl.token("H1")
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
	ctx := context.Background()
	creds, err := Enroll(ctx, dir, "  "+token+"\n")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Enroll: %v", err)
	}
	saved, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
	if err != nil || !sameCredentials(saved, creds) || saved.Generation != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("LoadCredentials = %+v, %v", saved, err)
	}
	if saved.HostID() != "H1" || !saved.CA.Equal(ctrl.auth.Root()) ||
		saved.Controller != (Endpoint{Address: ctrl.addr, SAN: ctrl.auth.ControllerSAN()}) {
		t.Errorf("saved %s, root %v, controller %+v", saved.HostID(), saved.CA.Equal(ctrl.auth.Root()), saved.Controller)
	}
	if st := ctrl.hostState("H1"); st != "enrolled" {
		t.Errorf("host state %q, want enrolled", st)
	}
	conn, err := transport.Dial(ctx, saved.Controller.Address,
		transport.AgentConfig(saved.TLSCertificate(), saved.CA, saved.Controller.SAN))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sess, err := transport.AgentSession(conn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = sess.Close() }()
	if _, err := sess.Ping(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the controller did not accept the saved credentials: %v", err)
	}
	if s := ctrl.agentSessions(); len(s) != 1 || s[0].hostID != "H1" {
		t.Errorf("controller sessions = %+v", s)
	}

	// A token is used once; a refused enrollment writes nothing.
	_, err = Enroll(ctx, dir, token)
	wantCode(t, err, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid)
	if gens := generations(t, dir); len(gens) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("generations after the refused enrollment = %v", gens)
	}
}

func TestEnrollRefusesAMalformedToken(t *testing.T) {
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
	for _, token := range []string{"", "dpe1.", "dpe1.e30", "nope"} {
		_, err := Enroll(context.Background(), dir, token)
		wantCode(t, err, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(dir); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Errorf("a malformed token created %s: %v", dir, err)
	}
}

// tamper rewrites one member of token's JSON.
func tamper(t *testing.T, token, member, value string) string {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(strings.TrimPrefix(token, transport.TokenPrefix))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var m map[string]string
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &m); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m[member] = value
	if raw, err = json.Marshal(m); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return transport.TokenPrefix + base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(raw)
}

func TestEnrollRefusesAnotherController(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	token := ctrl.token("H1")
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
	ctx := context.Background()
	other := newTestAuthority(t)
	for name, bad := range map[string]string{
		"root fingerprint": tamper(t, token, "f", transport.Fingerprint(other.cert)),
		"controller SAN":   tamper(t, token, "s", transport.ControllerURI("SOMEONEELSE")),
	} {
		if _, err := Enroll(ctx, dir, bad); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "transport:") {
			t.Errorf("%s: Enroll = %v, want the handshake refused", name, err)
		}
	}
	// Nothing reached the controller, so the token still enrolls.
	if _, err := Enroll(ctx, dir, token); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Enroll with the real token: %v", err)
	}
}

// signCSR signs the CSR's key with a's root for uri, as a controller of
// another authority would.
func (a *testAuthority) signCSR(t *testing.T, csrPEM, uri string) string {
	t.Helper()
	block, _ := pem.Decode([]byte(csrPEM))
	csr, err := x509.ParseCertificateRequest(block.Bytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(uri)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(7), NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(time.Hour),
		ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}, URIs: []*url.URL{u},
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, a.cert, csr.PublicKey, a.key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return string(pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: der}))
}

// Spec Verification "enrollment": a returned ca with the wrong fingerprint,
// and certificates the agent must not accept.
func TestEnrollChecksTheAnswer(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H2", "pending")
	other := newTestAuthority(t)
	otherRoot := string(pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: other.cert.Raw}))
	cases := []struct {
		name, host string
		rewrite    func(host string, req transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse)
	}{
		// A consistent pair from another authority: only the fingerprint
		// check refuses it.
		{"another ca", "HA", func(host string, req transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse) {
			r.CA, r.Certificate = otherRoot, other.signCSR(t, req.CSR, transport.HostURI(host))
		}},
		{"another authority's certificate", "HB", func(host string, req transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse) {
			r.Certificate = other.signCSR(t, req.CSR, transport.HostURI(host))
		}},
		{"another host's certificate", "HC", func(_ string, req transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse) {
			err := ctrl.db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
				cert, err := ctrl.auth.IssueAgentCert(context.Background(), tx, "H2", []byte(req.CSR))
				r.Certificate = string(cert)
				return err
			})
			if err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
			}
		}},
		{"no certificate", "HD", func(_ string, _ transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse) { r.Certificate = "" }},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		ctrl.addHost(c.host, "pending")
		token := ctrl.token(c.host)
		ctrl.mu.Lock()
		ctrl.rewrite = func(req transport.EnrollRequest, r *transport.EnrollResponse) { c.rewrite(c.host, req, r) }
		ctrl.mu.Unlock()
		dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
		if _, err := Enroll(context.Background(), dir, token); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: Enroll accepted the answer", c.name)
		}
		if _, err := LoadCredentials(dir); !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
			t.Errorf("%s: credentials were saved: %v", c.name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestReadTokenFile(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	uid := os.Getuid()
	write := func(name, content string, mode os.FileMode) string {
		t.Helper()
		p := filepath.Join(dir, name)
		if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(content), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := os.Chmod(p, mode); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return p
	}
	for _, mode := range []os.FileMode{0o600, 0o400} {
		p := write("ok"+mode.String(), "  dpe1.token\n", mode)
		if got, err := ReadTokenFile(p, uid); err != nil || got != "dpe1.token" {
			t.Errorf("mode %v: ReadTokenFile = %q, %v", mode, got, err)
		}
	}
	fifo := filepath.Join(dir, "fifo")
	if err := syscall.Mkfifo(fifo, 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	refused := map[string]struct {
		path string
		uid  int
	}{
		"group readable": {write("group", "dpe1.token", 0o640), uid},
		"world readable": {write("world", "dpe1.token", 0o604), uid},
		"another owner":  {write("owner", "dpe1.token", 0o600), uid + 1},
		"empty":          {write("empty", " \n", 0o600), uid},
		"directory":      {dir, uid},
		"fifo":           {fifo, uid},
		"missing":        {filepath.Join(dir, "missing"), uid},
	}
	for name, c := range refused {
		if got, err := ReadTokenFile(c.path, c.uid); err == nil || !strings.HasPrefix(err.Error(), "daemon: ") {
			t.Errorf("%s: ReadTokenFile = %q, %v; want a refusal", name, got, err)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/daemon/enroll.go`:

```go
package daemon

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"mime"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/wire"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// EnrollTimeout bounds one enrollment: the dial, the handshake and the
// request.
const EnrollTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// maxTokenFile is more than any token: a token is about 250 bytes.
const maxTokenFile = 4096

// Enroll consumes token at the controller it names and saves the answer as a
// new credential generation of dir (Paths.AgentTLSDir), spec section 3: the
// key is generated here and never leaves the host; the controller must
// present a chain whose root has the token's fingerprint and a leaf naming
// the token's controller SAN before anything is sent; and the answer's root
// must be that root and its certificate must name the token's host and chain
// to it. A malformed token and every refusal by the controller keep the
// controller's code (enroll.token_invalid, enroll.rate_limited).
func Enroll(ctx context.Context, dir, token string) (*Credentials, error) {
	tok, err := transport.ParseToken(token)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	_, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generate the agent key: %w", err)
	}
	csr, err := csrPEM(key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, EnrollTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	conn, err := transport.Dial(ctx, tok.Address, transport.EnrollConfig(tok.Fingerprint, tok.ControllerSAN))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var used bool
	dial := func(context.Context) (net.Conn, error) {
		// The enrollment connection carries exactly one request.
		if used {
			return nil, errors.New("daemon: the enrollment connection is used")
		}
		used = true
		return conn, nil
	}
	var out transport.EnrollResponse
	err = post(ctx, dial, transport.EnrollPath, transport.EnrollRequest{Token: tok.String(), CSR: csr}, &out)
	_ = conn.Close()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ca, err := parseCertificate([]byte(out.CA))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: the controller's agent root: %w", err)
	}
	if transport.Fingerprint(ca) != tok.Fingerprint {
		return nil, errors.New("daemon: the controller answered with an agent root other than the one the token names")
	}
	cert, err := checkIssued(out.Certificate, ca, tok.HostID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	c := &Credentials{Key: key, Cert: cert, CA: ca, Controller: Endpoint{Address: tok.Address, SAN: tok.ControllerSAN}}
	if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, c); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return c, nil
}

// csrPEM is a certificate request signed by key. The controller ignores
// every identity field, so it carries none.
func csrPEM(key ed25519.PrivateKey) (string, error) {
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: create the certificate request: %w", err)
	}
	return string(pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: der})), nil
}

// checkIssued parses an issued agent certificate and requires it to name
// hostID and to verify against ca alone for client authentication.
func checkIssued(certPEM string, ca *x509.Certificate, hostID string) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	cert, err := parseCertificate([]byte(certPEM))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: the issued certificate: %w", err)
	}
	if id, err := transport.HostIDOf(cert); err != nil || id != hostID {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: the issued certificate does not name host %s alone", hostID)
	}
	roots := x509.NewCertPool()
	roots.AddCert(ca)
	if _, err := cert.Verify(x509.VerifyOptions{Roots: roots, KeyUsages: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}}); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: the issued certificate: %w", err)
	}
	return cert, nil
}

// post sends one JSON request over a connection from dial and decodes a 200
// answer into out. A refusal in problem details keeps the controller's code.
func post(ctx context.Context, dial func(context.Context) (net.Conn, error), path string, in, out any) error {
	body, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: encode %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	tr := &http.Transport{
		DialContext:            func(ctx context.Context, _, _ string) (net.Conn, error) { return dial(ctx) },
		DisableKeepAlives:      true,
		DisableCompression:     true,
		MaxResponseHeaderBytes: 64 << 10,
	}
	defer tr.CloseIdleConnections()
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, "http://controller"+path, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	resp, err := (&http.Client{Transport: tr}).Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, wire.MaxBody+1))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	if len(data) > wire.MaxBody {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: the answer exceeds %d bytes", path, wire.MaxBody)
	}
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		if mt, _, err := mime.ParseMediaType(resp.Header.Get("Content-Type")); err == nil && mt == "application/problem+json" {
			var p struct {
				Code   errcode.Code `json:"code"`
				Detail string       `json:"detail"`
			}
			if json.Unmarshal(data, &p) == nil && p.Code != "" {
				return &errcode.Error{Code: p.Code, Detail: p.Detail}
			}
		}
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: unexpected answer %s", path, resp.Status)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, out); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %s: decode the answer: %w", path, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// ReadTokenFile reads an enrollment token from path for darkpanel agent
// enroll --token-file (spec section 3): path must be a regular file owned by
// uid (0 in production) with no permission bits for group or others, so
// mode 0600 or stricter. The checks run on the opened file, so a file
// swapped in after them is never read.
func ReadTokenFile(path string, uid int) (string, error) {
	// O_NONBLOCK keeps a FIFO at path from blocking the open.
	f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_RDONLY|syscall.O_NONBLOCK|syscall.O_NOCTTY, 0) //nolint:gosec // G304: the operator names the token file
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
	st, err := f.Stat()
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file: %w", err)
	}
	sys, ok := st.Sys().(*syscall.Stat_t)
	switch {
	case !st.Mode().IsRegular():
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file %s is not a regular file", path)
	case !ok || int(sys.Uid) != uid:
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file %s must be owned by uid %d", path, uid)
	case st.Mode().Perm()&0o077 != 0:
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file %s has mode %04o; want 0600 or stricter", path, st.Mode().Perm())
	}
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(f, maxTokenFile+1))
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file: %w", err)
	}
	token := strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
	switch {
	case len(data) > maxTokenFile:
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file %s is larger than a token", path)
	case token == "":
		return "", fmt.Errorf("daemon: token file %s is empty", path)
	}
	return token, nil
}
```

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"net"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/install"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// revoke revokes every certificate of hostID.
func (c *testController) revoke(hostID string) {
	c.t.Helper()
	err := c.db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		return fleet.RevokeHost(context.Background(), tx, hostID, c.clock.Now())
	})
	if err != nil {
		c.t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// refusals is the number of failed handshakes.
func (c *testController) refusals() int {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.refused
}

// waitFor polls cond every 10 ms for up to 10 s.
func waitFor(t *testing.T, what string, cond func() bool) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second)
	for !cond() {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatalf("timed out waiting for %s", what)
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
}

// enrolled returns a controller with host H1 enrolled through Agent, and
// the agent's credentials directory.
func enrolled(t *testing.T) (*testController, string) {
	t.Helper()
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")
	if err := (Agent{TLSDir: dir}).Enroll(context.Background(), ctrl.token("H1")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Enroll: %v", err)
	}
	return ctrl, dir
}

// resave saves the current credentials again with another endpoint.
func resave(t *testing.T, dir string, e Endpoint) {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c.Controller = e
	if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, c); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// Register row 67: darkpanel agent check exits 0 accepted, 1 refused, 2
// unreachable.
func TestCheck(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	if res, err := Check(ctx, filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent")); res != install.CheckRefused || err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Check without credentials = %d, %v; want refused", res, err)
	}
	ctrl, dir := enrolled(t)
	if res, err := (Agent{TLSDir: dir}).Check(ctx); res != install.CheckAccepted || err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Check = %d, %v; want accepted", res, err)
	}
	if s := ctrl.agentSessions(); len(s) != 1 || s[0].hostID != "H1" {
		t.Errorf("controller sessions = %+v, want one of H1", s)
	}

	ctrl.revoke("H1")
	before := ctrl.refusals()
	if res, err := Check(ctx, dir); res != install.CheckRefused || err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Check of a revoked host = %d, %v; want refused", res, err)
	}
	// The agent reads the alert before the controller's goroutine counts it.
	waitFor(t, "the controller to count the refusal", func() bool { return ctrl.refusals() == before+1 })

	// A controller that is not the pinned one never completes a handshake.
	resave(t, dir, Endpoint{Address: ctrl.addr, SAN: transport.ControllerURI("SOMEONEELSE")})
	if res, err := Check(ctx, dir); res != install.CheckUnreachable || err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Check against another controller = %d, %v; want unreachable", res, err)
	}
	ln, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	closed := ln.Addr().String()
	_ = ln.Close()
	resave(t, dir, Endpoint{Address: closed, SAN: ctrl.auth.ControllerSAN()})
	if res, err := Check(ctx, dir); res != install.CheckUnreachable || err != nil {
		t.Errorf("Check against a closed port = %d, %v; want unreachable", res, err)
	}
}

func TestCheckReportsUnreadableCredentials(t *testing.T) {
	_, dir := enrolled(t)
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "gen-1", certFile), []byte("garbage"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if res, err := Check(context.Background(), dir); res != install.CheckRefused || err == nil {
		t.Errorf("Check = %d, %v; want refused with an error", res, err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/daemon/check.go`:

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/install"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// DialTimeout bounds one connection attempt: the dial, the handshake and the
// ping that proves the controller accepted the credentials.
const DialTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// ErrRefused wraps connect's error when the controller refused the agent's
// certificate.
var ErrRefused = errors.New("daemon: the controller refused this agent's credentials")

// Agent is install.Agent over the credentials in TLSDir (Paths.AgentTLSDir).
type Agent struct {
	TLSDir string
}

var _ install.Agent = Agent{}

// Check is darkpanel agent check.
func (a Agent) Check(ctx context.Context) (install.CheckResult, error) {
	return Check(ctx, a.TLSDir)
}

// Enroll consumes token and saves a new credential generation.
func (a Agent) Enroll(ctx context.Context, token string) error {
	_, err := Enroll(ctx, a.TLSDir, token)
	return err
}

// Check is darkpanel agent check (register row 67): one mTLS handshake with
// the current credentials of dir. Without credentials it answers
// CheckRefused, because only a new enrollment helps; credentials that cannot
// be read answer CheckRefused with the error. A controller that is not the
// pinned one, or no answer within DialTimeout, is CheckUnreachable. The
// controller treats the check as the host's session, so it replaces a
// running daemon's session, which redials.
func Check(ctx context.Context, dir string) (install.CheckResult, error) {
	c, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
	if errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
		return install.CheckRefused, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return install.CheckRefused, err
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, DialTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	sess, err := connect(ctx, c)
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		_ = sess.Close()
		return install.CheckAccepted, nil
	case errors.Is(err, ErrRefused):
		return install.CheckRefused, nil
	default:
		return install.CheckUnreachable, nil
	}
}

// connect dials the controller with c and returns a session the controller
// accepted. In TLS 1.3 the client's handshake completes before the
// controller has checked the client certificate, so a refusal only arrives
// as an alert on the first read; the ping waits for either. A refusal wraps
// ErrRefused.
func connect(ctx context.Context, c *Credentials) (*transport.Session, error) {
	conn, err := transport.Dial(ctx, c.Controller.Address, transport.AgentConfig(c.TLSCertificate(), c.CA, c.Controller.SAN))
	if err != nil {
		if isRefusal(err) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", ErrRefused, err)
		}
		return nil, err
	}
	rc := &readErrConn{Conn: conn}
	sess, err := transport.AgentSession(rc)
	if err != nil {
		_ = conn.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	// Buffered, so the ping's goroutine ends even when nobody waits for it.
	pinged := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		_, err := sess.Ping()
		pinged <- err
	}()
	select {
	case err = <-pinged:
	case <-ctx.Done():
		_ = sess.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: ping the controller: %w", ctx.Err())
	}
	if err == nil {
		return sess, nil
	}
	_ = sess.Close()
	if readErr := rc.err(); isRefusal(readErr) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", ErrRefused, readErr)
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: ping the controller: %w", err)
}

// isRefusal reports whether err is a TLS alert the controller sent.
func isRefusal(err error) bool {
	var op *net.OpError
	return errors.As(err, &op) && op.Op == "remote error"
}

// readErrConn keeps the first read error, which the yamux session hides
// behind its own shutdown error.
type readErrConn struct {
	net.Conn
	mu    sync.Mutex
	first error
}

func (c *readErrConn) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	n, err := c.Conn.Read(p)
	if err != nil {
		c.mu.Lock()
		if c.first == nil {
			c.first = err
		}
		c.mu.Unlock()
	}
	return n, err
}

func (c *readErrConn) err() error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.first
}
```

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/dispatch"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

var (
	_ dispatch.ClientSource = (*fakeClients)(nil)
	_ ReleaseSource         = (*update.IndexSource)(nil)
)

// fakeClients tells which hosts have a live session.
type fakeClients struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	online map[string]bool
}

func (c *fakeClients) Client(_ context.Context, hostID string) (core.AgentClient, uint64, error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if !c.online[hostID] {
		return nil, 0, offline(hostID)
	}
	return &coretest.FakeAgent{}, 1, nil
}

func (c *fakeClients) set(hostID string, online bool) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.online[hostID] = online
}

// fakeIndex is the release index.
type fakeIndex struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	releases []update.Release
	err      error
}

func (x *fakeIndex) Releases(context.Context) ([]update.Release, error) {
	x.mu.Lock()
	defer x.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(x.releases), x.err
}

// schedEnv is a controller with hosts OWN (its own), A and B on 1.2.0, all
// online, and an index publishing 1.2.0 and 1.3.0 stable and 1.4.0-beta.1.
// Its clock starts at testStart, Monday 12:00 UTC.
type schedEnv struct {
	db      *store.DB
	clock   *coretest.FakeClock
	clients *fakeClients
	index   *fakeIndex
	audit   *coretest.MemJournal
}

func newSchedEnv(t *testing.T) *schedEnv {
	t.Helper()
	e := &schedEnv{
		db: openDB(t), clock: coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart),
		clients: &fakeClients{online: map[string]bool{"OWN": true, "A": true, "B": true}},
		index: &fakeIndex{releases: []update.Release{
			{Version: "1.2.0", Channel: core.ChannelStable},
			{Version: "1.3.0", Channel: core.ChannelStable},
			{Version: "1.4.0-beta.1", Channel: core.ChannelBeta},
		}},
		audit: &coretest.MemJournal{},
	}
	addHost(t, e.db, "OWN", true, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, e.db, "A", false, "1.2.0")
	addHost(t, e.db, "B", false, "1.2.0")
	return e
}

// schedule gives host a weekly window.
func (e *schedEnv) schedule(t *testing.T, host, channel, days, hhmm string) {
	t.Helper()
	exec(t, e.db, `INSERT INTO core_host_update (host_id, channel, days, time_utc) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
		ON CONFLICT (host_id) DO UPDATE SET channel = excluded.channel, days = excluded.days, time_utc = excluded.time_utc`,
		host, channel, days, hhmm)
}

// start starts a scheduler of a controller running version, stopped when
// the test ends.
func (e *schedEnv) start(t *testing.T, version string) *Scheduler {
	t.Helper()
	s, err := NewScheduler(SchedulerConfig{
		DB: e.db, Clients: e.clients, Index: e.index, Audit: e.audit, Clock: e.clock, Version: version,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.Start()
	t.Cleanup(s.Stop)
	return s
}

// minutes runs n scheduler passes.
func (e *schedEnv) minutes(n int) {
	for range n {
		e.clock.Advance(ScheduleInterval)
	}
}

// claim reads a host's last_window and last_rollout_id.
func (e *schedEnv) claim(t *testing.T, host string) (window, rolloutID string) {
	t.Helper()
	var w, r sql.NullString
	if err := e.db.Read().QueryRow("SELECT last_window, last_rollout_id FROM core_host_update WHERE host_id = ?", host).
		Scan(&w, &r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return w.String, r.String
}

// rollouts lists every rollout, newest first.
func (e *schedEnv) rollouts(t *testing.T) []record {
	t.Helper()
	rs, err := list(context.Background(), e.db.Read(), 100)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rs
}

// finishAll marks every rollout succeeded.
func (e *schedEnv) finishAll(t *testing.T) {
	t.Helper()
	exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_rollouts SET state = 'succeeded' WHERE state IN ('running', 'draining')")
}

func TestOpenWindow(t *testing.T) {
	monday := testStart // 2026-10-19 12:00 UTC
	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		now       time.Time
		days, at  string
		window    string
		wantFound bool
	}{
		{"before it opens", monday, "mon", "12:01", "", false},
		{"as it opens", monday, "mon", "12:00", "2026-10-19T12:00", true},
		{"inside", monday.Add(119 * time.Minute), "mon,thu", "12:00", "2026-10-19T12:00", true},
		{"as it closes", monday.Add(2 * time.Hour), "mon", "12:00", "", false},
		{"another day", monday, "tue,sun", "12:00", "", false},
		{"across midnight", time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 0, 45, 0, 0, time.UTC), "mon", "23:30", "2026-10-19T23:30", true},
		{"after midnight on the next listed day", time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 0, 45, 0, 0, time.UTC), "mon,tue", "00:30", "2026-10-20T00:30", true},
		{"a day not listed after midnight", time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 0, 45, 0, 0, time.UTC), "tue", "23:30", "", false},
		{"sunday", time.Date(2026, 10, 25, 6, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), "sun", "05:00", "2026-10-25T05:00", true},
		{"a malformed time", monday, "mon", "1200", "", false},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			w, ok := openWindow(tt.now, tt.days, tt.at)
			if w != tt.window || ok != tt.wantFound {
				t.Errorf("openWindow = %q, %v; want %q, %v", w, ok, tt.window, tt.wantFound)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSchedulerClaimsTheOwnHostAheadOfTheController(t *testing.T) {
	e := newSchedEnv(t)
	e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon,thu", "12:00")
	e.start(t, "1.2.0")
	e.minutes(1)
	rs := e.rollouts(t)
	if len(rs) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d rollouts, want 1", len(rs))
	}
	r := rs[0]
	if r.Version != "1.3.0" || r.Channel != core.ChannelStable || r.State != core.RolloutRunning || r.ChangeID != "" ||
		!slices.Equal(hostStates(r), []string{"OWN=waiting"}) {
		t.Errorf("rollout = %+v", r)
	}
	if w, id := e.claim(t, "OWN"); w != "2026-10-19T12:00" || id != r.ID {
		t.Errorf("claim = %q, %q; want the window and rollout %s", w, id, r.ID)
	}
	entries := e.audit.Entries()
	if len(entries) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("audit entries = %+v, want one", entries)
	}
	a := entries[0]
	var in core.RolloutInput
	if err := json.Unmarshal(a.Input, &in); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if a.Actor != core.ActorUpdateSchedule || a.Action != ActionRollout || a.Version != 1 || a.Phase != "apply" ||
		a.Result != "ok" || a.ChangeID != "" || !slices.Equal(in.Hosts, []string{"OWN"}) || in.Version != "1.3.0" {
		t.Errorf("audit entry = %+v, input %+v", a, in)
	}
	e.finishAll(t)
	e.minutes(30)
	if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("%d rollouts after the window was claimed, want 1", n)
	}
}

func TestSchedulerHoldsAnAgentHostBehindTheController(t *testing.T) {
	e := newSchedEnv(t)
	e.schedule(t, "A", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
	s := e.start(t, "1.2.0")
	e.minutes(5)
	if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%d rollouts while the controller is older than the target, want 0", n)
	}
	if w, _ := e.claim(t, "A"); w != "" {
		t.Fatalf("last window = %q, want none claimed", w)
	}
	s.Stop()
	e.start(t, "1.3.0")
	e.minutes(1)
	rs := e.rollouts(t)
	if len(rs) != 1 || !slices.Equal(hostStates(rs[0]), []string{"A=waiting"}) || rs[0].Version != "1.3.0" {
		t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want one for A once the controller runs 1.3.0", rs)
	}
}

func TestSchedulerRetryAndSkipRules(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("offline host", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.clients.set("OWN", false)
		e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(3)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts for an offline host", n)
		}
		e.clients.set("OWN", true)
		e.minutes(1)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts once the host is back in its window, want 1", n)
		}
	})
	t.Run("active rollout", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		mustCreate(t, e.db, "1.2.0", "1.2.0", "B")
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(3)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts while another is active, want only that one", n)
		}
		e.finishAll(t)
		e.minutes(1)
		if rs := e.rollouts(t); len(rs) != 2 || rs[0].Hosts[0].HostID != "OWN" {
			t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want the window claimed once the other ended", rs)
		}
	})
	t.Run("one claim per pass", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "B", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.start(t, "1.3.0")
		e.minutes(1)
		rs := e.rollouts(t)
		if len(rs) != 1 || rs[0].Hosts[0].HostID != "OWN" {
			t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want only the own host's", rs)
		}
		e.finishAll(t)
		e.minutes(1)
		if rs := e.rollouts(t); len(rs) != 2 || rs[0].Hosts[0].HostID != "B" {
			t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want B's next", rs)
		}
	})
	t.Run("nothing newer", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_hosts SET agent_version = '1.3.0' WHERE id = 'OWN'")
		e.start(t, "1.3.0")
		e.minutes(3)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts without a newer release", n)
		}
	})
	t.Run("beta channel", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelBeta, "mon", "12:00")
		e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(1)
		if rs := e.rollouts(t); len(rs) != 1 || rs[0].Version != "1.4.0-beta.1" || rs[0].Channel != core.ChannelBeta {
			t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want the newest beta", rs)
		}
	})
	t.Run("unknown agent version", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.schedule(t, "A", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_hosts SET agent_version = 'dev' WHERE id = 'OWN'")
		exec(t, e.db, "UPDATE core_hosts SET agent_version = NULL WHERE id = 'A'")
		e.start(t, "1.3.0")
		e.minutes(3)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts for hosts of unknown versions", n)
		}
	})
	t.Run("missed window", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "09:00")
		s := e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(60)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts for a window that closed at 11:00", n)
		}
		s.Stop()
		e.clock.Advance(7*24*time.Hour - 4*time.Hour - time.Minute)
		e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(1)
		if rs := e.rollouts(t); len(rs) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("rollouts = %+v, want next week's window claimed", rs)
		}
		if w, _ := e.claim(t, "OWN"); w != "2026-10-26T09:00" {
			t.Errorf("last window = %q", w)
		}
	})
	t.Run("index unavailable", func(t *testing.T) {
		e := newSchedEnv(t)
		e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
		e.index.err = errors.New("index: connection refused")
		e.start(t, "1.2.0")
		e.minutes(2)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts without an index", n)
		}
		e.index.mu.Lock()
		e.index.err = nil
		e.index.mu.Unlock()
		e.minutes(1)
		if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d rollouts once the index answers, want 1", n)
		}
	})
}

func TestSchedulerClaimIsAtomic(t *testing.T) {
	e := newSchedEnv(t)
	e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
	e.audit.Err = errors.New("audit: disk full")
	e.start(t, "1.2.0")
	e.minutes(2)
	if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%d rollouts after a failed audit entry, want the claim rolled back", n)
	}
	if w, id := e.claim(t, "OWN"); w != "" || id != "" {
		t.Fatalf("claim = %q, %q after a failed audit entry", w, id)
	}
	e.audit.Err = nil
	e.minutes(1)
	rs := e.rollouts(t)
	if w, id := e.claim(t, "OWN"); len(rs) != 1 || w != "2026-10-19T12:00" || id != rs[0].ID {
		t.Fatalf("claim = %q, %q, rollouts %d", w, id, len(rs))
	}
}

func TestNewSchedulerRequiresItsDependencies(t *testing.T) {
	e := newSchedEnv(t)
	full := SchedulerConfig{DB: e.db, Clients: e.clients, Index: e.index, Audit: e.audit, Clock: e.clock, Version: "1.2.0"}
	for _, drop := range []func(*SchedulerConfig){
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.DB = nil },
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.Clients = nil },
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.Index = nil },
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.Audit = nil },
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.Clock = nil },
		func(c *SchedulerConfig) { c.Version = "" },
	} {
		cfg := full
		drop(&cfg)
		if _, err := NewScheduler(cfg); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("NewScheduler(%+v) succeeded", cfg)
		}
	}
}

func TestSchedulerStop(t *testing.T) {
	e := newSchedEnv(t)
	e.schedule(t, "OWN", core.ChannelStable, "mon", "12:00")
	s := e.start(t, "1.2.0")
	s.Start()
	if n := e.clock.Pending(); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pending timers = %d after two Starts, want 1", n)
	}
	s.Stop()
	if n := e.clock.Pending(); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("pending timers = %d after Stop, want 0", n)
	}
	e.minutes(3)
	if n := len(e.rollouts(t)); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%d rollouts after Stop", n)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/controller/rollout/scheduler.go`:

```go
package rollout

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/dispatch"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

const (
	// ScheduleInterval is how often the scheduler looks for open update
	// windows.
	ScheduleInterval = time.Minute
	// WindowLength is how long an update window stays open after its time.
	WindowLength = 2 * time.Hour
)

// ReleaseSource lists the published releases; *update.IndexSource
// implements it.
type ReleaseSource interface {
	Releases(ctx context.Context) ([]update.Release, error)
}

// SchedulerConfig wires a Scheduler; every field is required.
type SchedulerConfig struct {
	DB *store.DB // the controller database
	// Clients tells which hosts are online: a host whose client answers an
	// error is offline. The controller passes its session registry.
	Clients dispatch.ClientSource
	Index   ReleaseSource
	// Audit is the controller's audit log; a claim's entry is written in
	// the claim's transaction.
	Audit   core.Journal
	Clock   core.Clock
	Log     *slog.Logger // nil discards
	Version string       // the controller's buildinfo.Version
}

// Scheduler starts a one-host rollout in a host's update window (spec
// section 8, Channels and schedule): once a minute, for a host inside an
// open window it has not claimed, when the index has a newer version in the
// host's channel and the host is online, it creates the rollout, records
// the window and writes the audit entry in one controller write
// transaction. A window is claimed once whatever the rollout's outcome, and
// a window that closed while the scheduler was not running is not caught
// up.
type Scheduler struct {
	cfg    SchedulerConfig
	ctx    context.Context
	cancel context.CancelFunc

	// mu is held for a whole pass, so passes never overlap and Stop waits
	// for a running one.
	mu      sync.Mutex
	timer   core.Timer
	started bool
	stopped bool
}

// NewScheduler returns a scheduler for cfg. Call Start to check the windows.
func NewScheduler(cfg SchedulerConfig) (*Scheduler, error) {
	switch {
	case cfg.DB == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.DB is required")
	case cfg.Clients == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.Clients is required")
	case cfg.Index == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.Index is required")
	case cfg.Audit == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.Audit is required")
	case cfg.Clock == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.Clock is required")
	case cfg.Version == "":
		return nil, errors.New("rollout: SchedulerConfig.Version is required")
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	return &Scheduler{cfg: cfg, ctx: ctx, cancel: cancel}, nil
}

// Start checks the windows every ScheduleInterval until Stop. A second
// Start, or a Start after Stop, does nothing.
func (s *Scheduler) Start() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.started || s.stopped {
		return
	}
	s.started = true
	s.timer = s.cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(ScheduleInterval, s.tick)
}

// Stop ends the loop: it cancels a running pass and waits for it to end.
func (s *Scheduler) Stop() {
	s.cancel()
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.stopped = true
	if s.timer != nil {
		s.timer.Stop()
	}
}

func (s *Scheduler) tick() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.stopped {
		return
	}
	if err := s.pass(s.ctx); err != nil {
		s.cfg.Log.Warn("rollout: scheduled updates", "error", err)
	}
	s.timer = s.cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(ScheduleInterval, s.tick)
}

// openWindow returns the id <date>T<HH:MM> of the update window open at now
// for a schedule of days (lower-case three-letter names joined by ",") at
// hhmm UTC: a window opens at hhmm on each listed day and stays open
// WindowLength, so it may still be open after midnight. ok is false outside
// every window.
func openWindow(now time.Time, days, hhmm string) (string, bool) {
	at, err := time.Parse("15:04", hhmm)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false
	}
	now = now.UTC()
	listed := strings.Split(days, ",")
	for back := range 2 {
		day := now.AddDate(0, 0, -back)
		open := time.Date(day.Year(), day.Month(), day.Day(), at.Hour(), at.Minute(), 0, 0, time.UTC)
		name := strings.ToLower(open.Weekday().String()[:3])
		if slices.Contains(listed, name) && !now.Before(open) && now.Before(open.Add(WindowLength)) {
			return open.Format("2006-01-02") + "T" + hhmm, true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}

// scheduledHost is an enrolled host with an update schedule.
type scheduledHost struct {
	id, version, channel, days, time, lastWindow string
	local                                        bool
}

// scheduledHosts reads the enrolled hosts with a schedule, the controller's
// own host first.
func (s *Scheduler) scheduledHosts(ctx context.Context) ([]scheduledHost, error) {
	rows, err := s.cfg.DB.Read().QueryContext(ctx,
		`SELECT u.host_id, h.is_local, coalesce(h.agent_version, ''), u.channel, u.days, u.time_utc,
		 coalesce(u.last_window, '')
		 FROM core_host_update u JOIN core_hosts h ON h.id = u.host_id
		 WHERE u.days IS NOT NULL AND u.time_utc IS NOT NULL AND h.state = ?
		 ORDER BY h.is_local DESC, h.id`, core.HostEnrolled)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the update schedules: %w", err)
	}
	var out []scheduledHost
	for rows.Next() {
		var h scheduledHost
		if err := rows.Scan(&h.id, &h.local, &h.version, &h.channel, &h.days, &h.time, &h.lastWindow); err != nil {
			_ = rows.Close()
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the update schedules: %w", err)
		}
		out = append(out, h)
	}
	if err := errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the update schedules: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// pass claims at most one open window: a claim starts a rollout, and no
// other can start until it ends.
func (s *Scheduler) pass(ctx context.Context) error {
	if _, ok, err := active(ctx, s.cfg.DB.Read()); err != nil || ok {
		return err
	}
	hosts, err := s.scheduledHosts(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	now := s.cfg.Clock.Now().UTC()
	var releases []update.Release
	fetched := false
	for _, h := range hosts {
		window, open := openWindow(now, h.days, h.time)
		if !open || window == h.lastWindow {
			continue
		}
		// A host whose version is unknown or does not parse, such as a
		// development build, cannot be compared with a release.
		if _, err := update.ParseVersion(h.version); err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if !fetched {
			if releases, err = s.cfg.Index.Releases(ctx); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("rollout: read the release index: %w", err)
			}
			fetched = true
		}
		candidates := update.InChannel(releases, h.channel)
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		target := candidates[len(candidates)-1].Version
		if !olderThan(h.version, target) {
			continue
		}
		// The controller's own host updates first; every other host waits
		// until the controller runs the target.
		if !h.local && olderThan(s.cfg.Version, target) {
			continue
		}
		if _, _, err := s.cfg.Clients.Client(ctx, h.id); err != nil {
			continue
		}
		claimed, err := s.claim(ctx, h, window, target, now)
		if err != nil || claimed {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// errNotClaimed rolls a claim back when its window was claimed meanwhile.
var errNotClaimed = errors.New("rollout: the window was claimed already")

// claim creates the one-host rollout of h to target, records window as h's
// last and writes the audit entry, in one controller write transaction. It
// reports false without an error when another rollout is active or the
// window was claimed meanwhile.
func (s *Scheduler) claim(ctx context.Context, h scheduledHost, window, target string, now time.Time) (bool, error) {
	in := core.RolloutInput{Hosts: []string{h.id}, Version: target, Channel: h.channel}
	input, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("rollout: encode the scheduled rollout: %w", err)
	}
	err = s.cfg.DB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		r, err := Create(ctx, tx, CreateParams{Input: in, ControllerVersion: s.cfg.Version, Now: now})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		res, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
			`UPDATE core_host_update SET last_window = ?, last_rollout_id = ?
			 WHERE host_id = ? AND (last_window IS NULL OR last_window <> ?)`, window, r.ID, h.id, window)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("rollout: record the window of host %s: %w", h.id, err)
		}
		if n, err := res.RowsAffected(); err != nil || n != 1 {
			return errors.Join(errNotClaimed, err)
		}
		return s.cfg.Audit.Append(ctx, tx, core.JournalEntry{
			At: now, Actor: core.ActorUpdateSchedule, Action: ActionRollout, Version: 1,
			Phase: "apply", Input: input, Result: "ok",
		})
	})
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		s.cfg.Log.Info("rollout: scheduled update", "host", h.id, "version", target, "window", window)
		return true, nil
	case errors.Is(err, errNotClaimed), errcode.From(err).Code == errcode.RolloutInProgress:
		return false, nil
	}
	return false, fmt.Errorf("rollout: claim window %s of host %s: %w", window, h.id, err)
}
```
