```go
package audit

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/dispatch"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// The controller's host directory is what reports epochs in production.
var _ Epochs = (*dispatch.Hosts)(nil)

// epochHosts is a directory that reports each host's journal epoch, as
// dispatch.Hosts does from Hello; a host without a client is offline.
type epochHosts struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	clients map[string]core.AgentClient
	epochs  map[string]string
}

func newEpochHosts() *epochHosts {
	return &epochHosts{clients: map[string]core.AgentClient{}, epochs: map[string]string{}}
}

func (e *epochHosts) Agent(_ context.Context, hostID string) (core.AgentClient, error) {
	e.mu.Lock()
	defer e.mu.Unlock()
	if c, ok := e.clients[hostID]; ok {
		return c, nil
	}
	return nil, errcode.New(errcode.HostOffline, fmt.Sprintf("host %s is offline", hostID))
}

func (e *epochHosts) JournalEpoch(hostID string) string {
	e.mu.Lock()
	defer e.mu.Unlock()
	return e.epochs[hostID]
}

// connect gives hostID a session whose agent has journal m in epoch.
func (e *epochHosts) connect(hostID, epoch string, m *memJournal) {
	e.mu.Lock()
	defer e.mu.Unlock()
	e.clients[hostID], e.epochs[hostID] = m.agent(), epoch
}

// logRecords is a slog.Handler that keeps each record as
// "<level> <message> host=<host attribute>".
type logRecords struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	got []string
}

func (l *logRecords) Enabled(context.Context, slog.Level) bool { return true }

func (l *logRecords) Handle(_ context.Context, r slog.Record) error {
	host := ""
	r.Attrs(func(a slog.Attr) bool {
		if a.Key == "host" {
			host = a.Value.String()
		}
		return true
	})
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.got = append(l.got, fmt.Sprintf("%s %s host=%s", r.Level, r.Message, host))
	return nil
}

func (l *logRecords) WithAttrs([]slog.Attr) slog.Handler { return l }

func (l *logRecords) WithGroup(string) slog.Handler { return l }

func (l *logRecords) lines() []string {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(l.got)
}

func newEpochIngester(e *ingestEnv, hosts *epochHosts, log *slog.Logger) *Ingester {
	return NewIngester(IngesterConfig{Store: e.store, Agents: hosts, Clock: e.clock, Log: log})
}

func epochCursorOf(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, hostID string) (string, int64) {
	t.Helper()
	var epoch string
	var seq int64
	if err := db.Read().QueryRow("SELECT epoch, acked_seq FROM core_journal_cursor WHERE host_id = ?", hostID).
		Scan(&epoch, &seq); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("cursor of %s: %v", hostID, err)
	}
	return epoch, seq
}

func TestJournalEpochTables(t *testing.T) {
	db := openControllerDB(t)
	insert := `INSERT INTO core_audit (id, seq, epoch, at, actor, host_id, action, version, phase, result)
		VALUES (?, 1, ?, '2026-10-17T12:00:00.000000000Z', 'user:u1', 'h1', 'a.b.c', 1, 'apply', 'ok')`
	for _, row := range [][]any{{"01A", "E1"}, {"01B", "E2"}} {
		if _, err := db.Writer().Exec(insert, row...); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("insert %v: %v", row, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := db.Writer().Exec(insert, "01C", "E1"); err == nil {
		t.Error("a second row for host h1, epoch E1 and seq 1 was accepted")
	}
	if _, err := db.Writer().Exec(`INSERT INTO core_journal_cursor (host_id, acked_seq) VALUES ('h1', 4)`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if epoch, seq := epochCursorOf(t, db, "h1"); epoch != "" || seq != 4 {
		t.Errorf("cursor = (%q, %d), want the empty epoch by default", epoch, seq)
	}
}

func TestIngestDeduplicatesByHostEpochAndSeq(t *testing.T) {
	s, db, _ := newTestStore(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	for _, epoch := range []string{"E1", "E1", "E2"} {
		if err := s.IngestEpoch(ctx, "h1", epoch, []core.JournalEntry{journalEntry(1, t0), journalEntry(2, t0)}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	for epoch, want := range map[string]int{"E1": 2, "E2": 2} {
		if n := count(t, db, "SELECT count(*) FROM core_audit WHERE host_id = 'h1' AND epoch = ?", epoch); n != want {
			t.Errorf("rows of epoch %s = %d, want %d", epoch, n, want)
		}
	}
}

// TestIngestRestartsAtANewEpoch: an agent whose journal was recreated
// restarts its sequence at 1 under a new epoch; the ingester starts that
// host's cursor at (epoch, 0) and acknowledges the new journal although its
// sequence is below the old epoch's acknowledgement.
func TestIngestRestartsAtANewEpoch(t *testing.T) {
	e := newIngestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	addHost(t, e.db, "h1")
	hosts := newEpochHosts()
	in := newEpochIngester(e, hosts, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler))
	old := &memJournal{}
	old.add(5)
	hosts.connect("h1", "E1", old)
	if err := in.IngestHost(ctx, "h1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if epoch, seq := epochCursorOf(t, e.db, "h1"); epoch != "E1" || seq != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("cursor = (%s, %d), want (E1, 5)", epoch, seq)
	}

	recreated := &memJournal{}
	recreated.add(3)
	hosts.connect("h1", "E2", recreated)
	if err := in.IngestHost(ctx, "h1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	acks, reads := recreated.state()
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(reads, []int64{0}) || !reflect.DeepEqual(acks, []int64{3}) {
		t.Errorf("new journal: reads %v acks %v, want reads [0] and acks [3]", reads, acks)
	}
	if epoch, seq := epochCursorOf(t, e.db, "h1"); epoch != "E2" || seq != 3 {
		t.Errorf("cursor = (%s, %d), want (E2, 3)", epoch, seq)
	}
	for epoch, want := range map[string]int{"E1": 5, "E2": 3} {
		if n := count(t, e.db, "SELECT count(*) FROM core_audit WHERE host_id = 'h1' AND epoch = ?", epoch); n != want {
			t.Errorf("rows of epoch %s = %d, want %d", epoch, n, want)
		}
	}

	// A new epoch whose journal is still empty moves the cursor to (epoch, 0).
	hosts.connect("h1", "E3", &memJournal{})
	if err := in.IngestHost(ctx, "h1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if epoch, seq := epochCursorOf(t, e.db, "h1"); epoch != "E3" || seq != 0 {
		t.Errorf("cursor = (%s, %d), want (E3, 0)", epoch, seq)
	}
}

func TestIngestSkipsOfflineHosts(t *testing.T) {
	e := newIngestEnv(t)
	addHost(t, e.db, "h1")
	addHost(t, e.db, "h2")
	hosts := newEpochHosts()
	m := &memJournal{}
	m.add(2)
	hosts.connect("h2", "E1", m)
	logs := &logRecords{}
	if err := newEpochIngester(e, hosts, slog.New(logs)).IngestAll(context.Background()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("IngestAll = %v, want the offline host skipped", err)
	}
	if got, want := logs.lines(), []string{"DEBUG audit: offline host skipped host=h1"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("logs = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
	if n := count(t, e.db, "SELECT count(*) FROM core_audit WHERE host_id = 'h2'"); n != 2 {
		t.Errorf("h2 rows = %d, want 2", n)
	}
}

// TestIngestDeadline: a host whose agent stops answering is given up after
// IngestTimeout on the ingester's clock, and the next host is still read.
func TestIngestDeadline(t *testing.T) {
	e := newIngestEnv(t)
	addHost(t, e.db, "h1")
	addHost(t, e.db, "h2")
	hosts := newEpochHosts()
	entered := make(chan struct{})
	hosts.clients["h1"] = &coretest.FakeAgent{
		JournalFunc: func(ctx context.Context, _ int64, _ int) ([]core.JournalEntry, error) {
			close(entered)
			<-ctx.Done()
			return nil, context.Cause(ctx)
		},
	}
	m := &memJournal{}
	m.add(1)
	hosts.connect("h2", "E1", m)
	in := newEpochIngester(e, hosts, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler))
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- in.IngestAll(context.Background()) }()
	<-entered
	e.clock.Advance(IngestTimeout - time.Second)
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		t.Fatalf("IngestAll returned %v before the deadline", err)
	case <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
	}
	e.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "host h1") || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "30s") {
			t.Errorf("IngestAll = %v, want h1's deadline error", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("IngestAll did not return after the deadline")
	}
	if n := count(t, e.db, "SELECT count(*) FROM core_audit WHERE host_id = 'h2'"); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("h2 rows = %d, want 1", n)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/core/store/migrations/controller/0006_journal_epoch.sql`:

```sql
-- Journal epochs (spec section 5): an agent's journal sequence starts again
-- at 1 when agent.db or its journal table is recreated, under a new epoch the
-- agent reports in Hello. The ingestion cursor and the ingested rows are
-- therefore keyed by epoch. Rows stored before this migration, and
-- controller-side rows, carry the empty epoch.

ALTER TABLE core_journal_cursor ADD COLUMN epoch TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '';

ALTER TABLE core_audit ADD COLUMN epoch TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '';

DROP INDEX core_audit_host_seq;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX core_audit_host_epoch_seq ON core_audit (host_id, epoch, seq);
```

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/api"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// quietLog discards what the code under test logs.
var quietLog = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)

// registryEnv is a registry over a fresh authority whose database holds
// the enrolled host H1 with one certificate.
type registryEnv struct {
	r      *Registry
	a      *Authority
	db     *store.DB
	clock  *coretest.FakeClock
	serial string // H1's certificate
}

// newRegistry returns a registry checking peers with a fresh authority.
func newRegistry(t *testing.T, hb transport.HeartbeatConfig) *registryEnv {
	t.Helper()
	a, db, clock := newAuthority(t)
	addHost(t, db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	serial := transport.SerialText(issue(t, a, db, "H1").SerialNumber)
	r, err := NewRegistry(RegistryConfig{
		DB: db, Clock: clock, CheckPeer: a.CheckPeer, Version: "1.2.3", ControllerID: "C1", Heartbeat: hb, Log: quietLog,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(r.Close)
	return &registryEnv{r: r, a: a, db: db, clock: clock, serial: serial}
}

// register registers s as H1's session with H1's serial.
func (e *registryEnv) register(t *testing.T, s *transport.Session) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := e.r.Register(context.Background(), "H1", e.serial, s); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// livePair returns the two ends of one session over an in-memory
// connection, closed at the end of the test.
func livePair(t *testing.T) (ctrl, agent *transport.Session) {
	t.Helper()
	c1, c2 := net.Pipe()
	errs := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		var err error
		agent, err = transport.AgentSession(c2)
		errs <- err
	}()
	ctrl, err := transport.ControllerSession(c1)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := <-errs; err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_ = ctrl.Close()
		_ = agent.Close()
	})
	return ctrl, agent
}

// serveAgent serves the agent API over the streams the controller opens
// on s, and records the controller halves of the hellos it receives.
func serveAgent(t *testing.T, s *transport.Session, agent core.AgentClient) *helloLog {
	t.Helper()
	hellos := &helloLog{}
	srv := &http.Server{
		Handler:           api.NewHandler(api.Config{Agent: agent, Log: quietLog, OnHello: hellos.add}),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
	}
	go func() { _ = srv.Serve(s) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = srv.Close() })
	return hellos
}

type helloLog struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	list []api.ControllerHello
}

func (l *helloLog) add(c api.ControllerHello) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.list = append(l.list, c)
}

func (l *helloLog) all() []api.ControllerHello {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]api.ControllerHello(nil), l.list...)
}

// helloAgent answers Hello as hostID running version.
func helloAgent(hostID, version string) *coretest.FakeAgent {
	return &coretest.FakeAgent{HelloFunc: func(context.Context) (core.AgentHello, error) {
		return core.AgentHello{ProtocolVersion: 1, BinaryVersion: version, HostID: hostID}, nil
	}}
}

// hostHello calls Hello through the registry's client of hostID.
func hostHello(t *testing.T, r *Registry, hostID string) (core.AgentHello, uint64) {
	t.Helper()
	c, gen, err := r.Client(context.Background(), hostID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h, err := c.Hello(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h, gen
}

// wantOffline fails unless hostID's client answers host.offline.
func wantOffline(t *testing.T, r *Registry, hostID string) {
	t.Helper()
	_, _, err := r.Client(context.Background(), hostID)
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.HostOffline || coded.Detail != "host "+hostID+" is offline" {
		t.Fatalf("Client(%s) error = %v, want host.offline", hostID, err)
	}
}

// eventually polls cond every few milliseconds for up to five seconds.
func eventually(t *testing.T, what string, cond func() bool) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for !cond() {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatalf("timed out waiting for %s", what)
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
}

// ended reports whether s has ended.
func ended(s *transport.Session) bool {
	select {
	case <-s.Done():
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

// lastSeen returns core_hosts.last_seen_at of hostID, "" while it is null.
func lastSeen(t *testing.T, db *store.DB, hostID string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var v *string
	if err := db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT last_seen_at FROM core_hosts WHERE id = ?`, hostID).Scan(&v); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if v == nil {
		return ""
	}
	return *v
}

func TestRegistryOfflineHost(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
	e.r.Disconnect("H1")
	e.r.Disconnect("unknown")
	if got := lastSeen(t, e.db, "H1"); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("last_seen_at = %q before any session", got)
	}
}

func TestRegistryServesTheSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	hellos := serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "1.2.0"))
	e.register(t, ctrl)

	h, gen := hostHello(t, e.r, "H1")
	if h.HostID != "H1" || h.BinaryVersion != "1.2.0" || gen == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("hello = %+v, generation %d", h, gen)
	}
	want := api.ControllerHello{Protocol: 1, Version: "1.2.3", ControllerID: "C1"}
	if got := hellos.all(); len(got) != 1 || got[0] != want {
		t.Fatalf("the agent received the controller hellos %+v, want one %+v", got, want)
	}
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H2")
}

func TestRegistryReplacesTheSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctrl1, agent1 := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent1, helloAgent("H1", "first"))
	e.register(t, ctrl1)
	_, gen1 := hostHello(t, e.r, "H1")

	ctrl2, agent2 := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent2, helloAgent("H1", "second"))
	e.register(t, ctrl2)
	if !ended(ctrl1) {
		t.Fatal("the replaced session is still open")
	}
	eventually(t, "the agent to see its replaced session end", func() bool { return ended(agent1) })
	h, gen2 := hostHello(t, e.r, "H1")
	if h.BinaryVersion != "second" || gen2 <= gen1 {
		t.Fatalf("after the replacement: hello %+v, generation %d after %d", h, gen2, gen1)
	}

	// The replaced session's end must not take the new one with it.
	time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
	if h, gen := hostHello(t, e.r, "H1"); h.BinaryVersion != "second" || gen != gen2 {
		t.Fatalf("the replaced session's end changed the client: %+v, generation %d", h, gen)
	}

	_ = agent2.Close()
	eventually(t, "the ended session to leave the registry", func() bool {
		_, _, err := e.r.Client(context.Background(), "H1")
		return err != nil
	})
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
}

func TestRegistryGenerationsNeverRepeat(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	seen := map[uint64]bool{}
	for i := range 3 {
		ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
		serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
		e.register(t, ctrl)
		_, gen, err := e.r.Client(context.Background(), "H1")
		if err != nil || gen == 0 || seen[gen] {
			t.Fatalf("session %d: generation %d, %v (seen %v)", i, gen, err, seen)
		}
		seen[gen] = true
		e.r.Disconnect("H1")
	}
}

func TestRegistryDisconnect(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	e.register(t, ctrl)
	hostHello(t, e.r, "H1")

	e.r.Disconnect("H1")
	if !ended(ctrl) {
		t.Fatal("Disconnect left the session open")
	}
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
	eventually(t, "the agent to see its session end", func() bool { return ended(agent) })
}

// TestRegistryRefusesARevocationBeforeRegister interleaves a revocation
// between the handshake's peer check and Register: its Disconnect finds no
// session, so Register itself must refuse the session.
func TestRegistryRefusesARevocationBeforeRegister(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctx := context.Background()
	if err := e.a.CheckPeer(ctx, "H1", e.serial); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the handshake's check refused H1: %v", err)
	}
	if err := e.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return RevokeHost(ctx, tx, "H1", e.clock.Now()) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e.r.Disconnect("H1")

	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	err := e.r.Register(ctx, "H1", e.serial, ctrl)
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.Forbidden {
		t.Fatalf("Register of a revoked host = %v, want auth.forbidden", err)
	}
	if !ended(ctrl) {
		t.Fatal("the refused session is still open")
	}
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
	eventually(t, "the agent to see the refused session end", func() bool { return ended(agent) })
}

func TestRegistryLastSeen(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	e.register(t, ctrl)

	steps := []struct {
		advance time.Duration
		want    time.Time
	}{
		{0, testStart},
		{LastSeenInterval - time.Second, testStart},
		{time.Second, testStart.Add(LastSeenInterval)},
		{time.Hour, testStart.Add(LastSeenInterval + time.Hour)},
	}
	for i, s := range steps {
		e.clock.Advance(s.advance)
		hostHello(t, e.r, "H1")
		e.r.writes.Wait()
		if got, want := lastSeen(t, e.db, "H1"), formatTime(s.want); got != want {
			t.Fatalf("step %d: last_seen_at = %q, want %q", i, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestRegistryHeartbeatMarksTheHostSeen(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: 5 * time.Millisecond})
	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	e.register(t, ctrl)
	eventually(t, "a ping to mark the host seen", func() bool { return lastSeen(t, e.db, "H1") == formatTime(testStart) })
}

func TestRegistryClose(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour})
	ctrl, agent := livePair(t)
	serveAgent(t, agent, helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	e.register(t, ctrl)

	e.r.Close()
	if !ended(ctrl) {
		t.Fatal("Close left a session open")
	}
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
	late, _ := livePair(t)
	if err := e.r.Register(context.Background(), "H1", e.serial, late); err == nil || !ended(late) {
		t.Fatalf("a session registered after Close: error %v, ended %v", err, ended(late))
	}
	wantOffline(t, e.r, "H1")
	e.r.Close()
}

func TestNewRegistryRefusesBadConfig(t *testing.T) {
	a, db, clock := newAuthority(t)
	good := RegistryConfig{DB: db, Clock: clock, CheckPeer: a.CheckPeer, Version: "1.2.3", ControllerID: "C1"}
	cases := map[string]func(*RegistryConfig){
		"negative interval": func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.Heartbeat.Interval = -time.Second },
		"negative timeout":  func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.Heartbeat.Timeout = -time.Second },
		"negative failures": func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.Heartbeat.Failures = -1 },
		"no version":        func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.Version = "" },
		"no controller id":  func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.ControllerID = "" },
		"no database":       func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.DB = nil },
		"no clock":          func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.Clock = nil },
		"no peer check":     func(c *RegistryConfig) { c.CheckPeer = nil },
	}
	for name, change := range cases {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			cfg := good
			change(&cfg)
			if r, err := NewRegistry(cfg); err == nil {
				r.Close()
				t.Fatal("NewRegistry accepted the configuration")
			}
		})
	}
	r, err := NewRegistry(good)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewRegistry refused the zero heartbeat: %v", err)
	}
	r.Close()
}
```

Create `internal/controller/fleet/registry.go`:

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/api"
	agentruntime "github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/runtime"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// LastSeenInterval is the shortest time between two writes of one host's
// core_hosts.last_seen_at (spec section 4), so a busy session does not
// queue a database write per request.
const LastSeenInterval = 15 * time.Second

// RegistryConfig configures NewRegistry.
type RegistryConfig struct {
	DB    *store.DB // core_hosts.last_seen_at
	Clock core.Clock
	// CheckPeer is Authority.CheckPeer: Register checks the session's serial
	// again with it.
	CheckPeer transport.PeerCheck
	// Version and ControllerID are the controller's half of every hello.
	Version      string
	ControllerID string
	// Heartbeat tunes each session's heartbeat: a zero field is the spec's
	// value and a negative one is refused. OnAlive is ignored.
	Heartbeat transport.HeartbeatConfig
	// Log receives sessions starting and ending; nil discards.
	Log *slog.Logger
}

// Registry holds the live agent session of each host and is the
// controller's ClientSource. Online state is the registry's alone and is
// never stored, so every host is offline when the controller starts. Its
// methods are safe for concurrent use.
type Registry struct {
	cfg    RegistryConfig
	ctx    context.Context
	cancel context.CancelFunc

	mu         sync.Mutex
	live       map[string]*liveSession
	written    map[string]time.Time // the last last_seen_at written per host
	generation uint64
	closed     bool

	wg     sync.WaitGroup // heartbeats and session watchers
	writes sync.WaitGroup // last_seen_at writes
}

type liveSession struct {
	hostID     string
	session    *transport.Session
	client     *api.Client
	generation uint64
}

// NewRegistry returns a registry with no sessions.
func NewRegistry(cfg RegistryConfig) (*Registry, error) {
	hb := cfg.Heartbeat
	switch {
	case cfg.DB == nil || cfg.Clock == nil || cfg.CheckPeer == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("fleet: the registry needs a database, a clock and a peer check")
	case cfg.Version == "" || cfg.ControllerID == "":
		return nil, errors.New("fleet: the registry needs the controller's version and id for the hello")
	case hb.Interval < 0 || hb.Timeout < 0 || hb.Failures < 0:
		return nil, errors.New("fleet: heartbeat settings must be zero or positive")
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	return &Registry{
		cfg: cfg, ctx: ctx, cancel: cancel,
		live: map[string]*liveSession{}, written: map[string]time.Time{},
	}, nil
}

// Register makes s hostID's live session. The caller has authenticated s's
// peer as hostID with the certificate serial. Register checks serial again
// while it holds the lock Disconnect takes: a revocation that committed
// after the handshake's check and whose Disconnect found no session yet is
// seen here, so a revoked host never keeps a session. A refused or late
// session (after Close) is closed and its error returned. An earlier
// session of the host is closed, and s leaves the registry when it ends,
// whether its peer, its heartbeat, Disconnect or Close ended it.
func (r *Registry) Register(ctx context.Context, hostID, serial string, s *transport.Session) error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	if r.closed {
		r.mu.Unlock()
		_ = s.Close()
		return errors.New("fleet: the registry is closed")
	}
	if err := r.cfg.CheckPeer(ctx, hostID, serial); err != nil {
		r.mu.Unlock()
		_ = s.Close()
		return fmt.Errorf("fleet: register host %s: %w", hostID, err)
	}
	r.generation++
	ls := &liveSession{hostID: hostID, session: s, generation: r.generation}
	ls.client = api.NewClient(api.ClientConfig{
		Dial: func(ctx context.Context) (net.Conn, error) {
			c, err := s.Open(ctx)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			return &seenConn{Conn: c, seen: func() { r.seen(hostID) }}, nil
		},
		Controller: &api.ControllerHello{
			Protocol: agentruntime.ProtocolVersion, Version: r.cfg.Version, ControllerID: r.cfg.ControllerID,
		},
	})
	old := r.live[hostID]
	r.live[hostID] = ls
	r.wg.Add(2)
	r.mu.Unlock()

	if old != nil {
		_ = old.session.Close()
		r.cfg.Log.Info("fleet: a new session replaced the host's session", "host", hostID)
	} else {
		r.cfg.Log.Info("fleet: host online", "host", hostID)
	}
	go r.heartbeat(ls)
	go r.watch(ls)
	return nil
}

func (r *Registry) heartbeat(ls *liveSession) {
	defer r.wg.Done()
	cfg := r.cfg.Heartbeat
	cfg.OnAlive = func() { r.seen(ls.hostID) }
	if err := transport.Heartbeat(r.ctx, ls.session, cfg); err != nil {
		r.cfg.Log.Warn("fleet: the agent stopped answering; its session is closed", "host", ls.hostID, "err", err)
	}
}

// watch removes ls when its session ends, unless a newer session has
// replaced it already.
func (r *Registry) watch(ls *liveSession) {
	defer r.wg.Done()
	<-ls.session.Done()
	r.mu.Lock()
	current := r.live[ls.hostID] == ls
	if current {
		delete(r.live, ls.hostID)
	}
	r.mu.Unlock()
	if current {
		r.cfg.Log.Info("fleet: host offline", "host", ls.hostID)
	}
}

// Client returns hostID's agent client and its session's generation, which
// is positive and new for every session. A host without a live session
// answers host.offline.
func (r *Registry) Client(_ context.Context, hostID string) (core.AgentClient, uint64, error) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	ls, ok := r.live[hostID]
	if !ok {
		return nil, 0, errcode.New(errcode.HostOffline, fmt.Sprintf("host %s is offline", hostID))
	}
	return ls.client, ls.generation, nil
}

// Disconnect closes hostID's live session, if any. A revoked or deleted
// host calls it after its transaction commits, never inside it: Register's
// check must be able to see the committed revocation. The handshake
// refuses the host's redials.
func (r *Registry) Disconnect(hostID string) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	ls := r.live[hostID]
	delete(r.live, hostID)
	r.mu.Unlock()
	if ls != nil {
		_ = ls.session.Close()
		r.cfg.Log.Info("fleet: host disconnected", "host", hostID)
	}
}

// Close closes every session and waits for the registry's goroutines;
// later sessions are closed as they register.
func (r *Registry) Close() {
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.closed = true
	live := r.live
	r.live = map[string]*liveSession{}
	r.mu.Unlock()
	r.cancel()
	for _, ls := range live {
		_ = ls.session.Close()
	}
	r.wg.Wait()
	r.writes.Wait()
}

// seen records that hostID answered: it writes last_seen_at unless the
// last write is younger than LastSeenInterval. The write runs on its own
// goroutine, so neither a request nor the heartbeat waits for the
// database.
func (r *Registry) seen(hostID string) {
	now := r.cfg.Clock.Now()
	r.mu.Lock()
	if last, ok := r.written[hostID]; r.closed || ok && now.Sub(last) < LastSeenInterval {
		r.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	r.written[hostID] = now
	r.writes.Add(1)
	r.mu.Unlock()
	go func() {
		defer r.writes.Done()
		err := r.cfg.DB.Write(r.ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
			_, err := tx.ExecContext(r.ctx, `UPDATE core_hosts SET last_seen_at = ? WHERE id = ?`, formatTime(now), hostID)
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			r.cfg.Log.Warn("fleet: cannot record when the host was last seen", "host", hostID, "err", err)
		}
	}()
}

// seenConn reports the first bytes the agent sends on one stream: an
// answer to a request.
type seenConn struct {
	net.Conn
	once sync.Once
	seen func()
}

func (c *seenConn) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	n, err := c.Conn.Read(p)
	if n > 0 {
		c.once.Do(c.seen)
	}
	return n, err
}
```

```go
package fleet

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"math/big"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/wire"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// fleetServer is a Server on 127.0.0.1 over a fresh authority and registry.
type fleetServer struct {
	a     *Authority
	db    *store.DB
	clock *coretest.FakeClock
	reg   *Registry
	srv   *Server
	addr  string
}

// startServer serves a fresh authority's agent connections on
// 127.0.0.1:0 until the end of the test; change adjusts the configuration
// first.
func startServer(t *testing.T, change func(*ServerConfig)) *fleetServer {
	t.Helper()
	a, db, clock := newAuthority(t)
	reg, err := NewRegistry(RegistryConfig{
		DB: db, Clock: clock, CheckPeer: a.CheckPeer, Version: "1.2.3", ControllerID: a.ControllerID(),
		Heartbeat: transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour}, Log: quietLog,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg := ServerConfig{Authority: a, Registry: reg, Limiter: NewEnrollLimiter(clock), Log: quietLog}
	if change != nil {
		change(&cfg)
	}
	srv, err := NewServer(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var lc net.ListenConfig
	ln, err := lc.Listen(context.Background(), "tcp4", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	served := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { served <- srv.Serve(ln) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_ = ln.Close()
		if err := <-served; err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Serve = %v after the listener closed, want nil", err)
		}
		srv.Close()
		reg.Close()
	})
	return &fleetServer{a: a, db: db, clock: clock, reg: reg, srv: srv, addr: ln.Addr().String()}
}

// enrolledCert issues hostID a certificate for a fresh key, as enrollment
// does, and returns it with its key.
func enrolledCert(t *testing.T, a *Authority, db *store.DB, hostID string) tls.Certificate {
	t.Helper()
	csr, key := newCSR(t)
	var out []byte
	err := db.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		var err error
		out, err = a.IssueAgentCert(context.Background(), tx, hostID, csr)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	leaf := parseCert(t, out)
	return tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{leaf.Raw}, PrivateKey: key, Leaf: leaf}
}

// dialAgent dials the server as an agent with cert. Under TLS 1.3 the
// server checks the client certificate after the client's side of the
// handshake ends, so a refusal may surface only at the first read.
func dialAgent(t *testing.T, f *fleetServer, cert tls.Certificate) (*tls.Conn, error) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	return transport.Dial(ctx, f.addr, transport.AgentConfig(cert, f.a.Root(), f.a.ControllerSAN()))
}

// connectAgent connects as hostID's agent with cert, serves agent over the
// session and waits until the registry holds it.
func connectAgent(t *testing.T, f *fleetServer, cert tls.Certificate, hostID string, agent core.AgentClient) *transport.Session {
	t.Helper()
	conn, err := dialAgent(t, f, cert)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err := transport.AgentSession(conn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = s.Close() })
	serveAgent(t, s, agent)
	eventually(t, "the registry to hold "+hostID, func() bool {
		_, _, err := f.reg.Client(context.Background(), hostID)
		return err == nil
	})
	return s
}

// wantRefused fails unless the handshake refuses cert with a TLS alert,
// when the dial or the first read fails, and the host stays offline. A
// session closed after the handshake would fail the read without an alert.
func wantRefused(t *testing.T, f *fleetServer, cert tls.Certificate, hostID string) {
	t.Helper()
	conn, err := dialAgent(t, f, cert)
	if err == nil {
		defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
		_ = conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second))
		_, err = conn.Read(make([]byte, 1))
	}
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "remote error: tls: ") {
		t.Fatalf("the handshake did not refuse the agent with an alert: %v", err)
	}
	time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
	wantOffline(t, f.reg, hostID)
}

// sessionHTTP is an HTTP client whose requests open streams on the agent's
// side of s, as the agent's renewal does.
func sessionHTTP(s *transport.Session) *http.Client {
	return &http.Client{Transport: &http.Transport{
		DialContext:       func(ctx context.Context, _, _ string) (net.Conn, error) { return s.Open(ctx) },
		DisableKeepAlives: true,
	}}
}

// postJSON sends body to path with contentType and returns the status and
// the answer.
func postJSON(t *testing.T, c *http.Client, method, path, contentType string, body []byte) (int, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(context.Background(), method, "http://controller"+path, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if contentType != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", contentType)
	}
	resp, err := c.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	b, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return resp.StatusCode, b
}

// wantProblemBody fails unless b is problem details with status and code
// and, when detail is not empty, exactly that detail.
func wantProblemBody(t *testing.T, gotStatus int, b []byte, status int, code errcode.Code, detail string) {
	t.Helper()
	var p problem
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &p); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("answer %d %s is not problem details: %v", gotStatus, b, err)
	}
	if gotStatus != status || p.Status != status || p.Code != code || p.Title != code.Title() || p.Type != "about:blank" {
		t.Fatalf("answer %d %s, want status %d and code %s", gotStatus, b, status, code)
	}
	if detail != "" && p.Detail != detail {
		t.Fatalf("detail = %q, want %q", p.Detail, detail)
	}
}

func renewBody(t *testing.T, csr []byte) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(transport.RenewRequest{CSR: string(csr)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

func TestServerRegistersAnAgent(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	cert := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	connectAgent(t, f, cert, "H1", helloAgent("H1", "1.2.0"))

	h, gen := hostHello(t, f.reg, "H1")
	if h.HostID != "H1" || gen == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("hello through the registry = %+v, generation %d", h, gen)
	}
}

func TestServerHandshakeRevokesEarlierSerials(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	ctx := context.Background()
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	old := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	f.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	renewed := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	connectAgent(t, f, renewed, "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))

	if err := f.a.CheckPeer(ctx, "H1", transport.SerialText(old.Leaf.SerialNumber)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("the earlier serial is still valid after a handshake with the newer one")
	}
	if err := f.a.CheckPeer(ctx, "H1", transport.SerialText(renewed.Leaf.SerialNumber)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the serial that connected was refused: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestServerRenews(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	s := connectAgent(t, f, enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1"), "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	c := sessionHTTP(s)

	csr, _ := newCSR(t)
	status, b := postJSON(t, c, http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", renewBody(t, csr))
	if status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("renew answered %d %s", status, b)
	}
	var resp transport.RenewResponse
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &resp); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert := parseCert(t, []byte(resp.Certificate))
	if id, err := transport.HostIDOf(cert); err != nil || id != "H1" {
		t.Fatalf("the renewed certificate names %q, %v", id, err)
	}
	if _, err := cert.Verify(x509.VerifyOptions{Roots: f.a.Pool(), KeyUsages: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth},
		CurrentTime: f.clock.Now()}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the renewed certificate does not verify: %v", err)
	}

	big := append([]byte(`{"csr":"`), bytes.Repeat([]byte("a"), wire.MaxBody)...)
	big = append(big, `"}`...)
	cases := []struct {
		name, method, path, contentType string
		body                            []byte
		status                          int
		code                            errcode.Code
	}{
		{"wrong method", http.MethodGet, transport.RenewPath, "", nil, http.StatusMethodNotAllowed, errcode.MethodNotAllowed},
		{"wrong path", http.MethodPost, "/controller/v1/other", "application/json", renewBody(t, csr), http.StatusNotFound, errcode.NotFound},
		{"enrollment path", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", renewBody(t, csr), http.StatusNotFound, errcode.NotFound},
		{"not JSON", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "text/plain", renewBody(t, csr), http.StatusUnsupportedMediaType, errcode.UnsupportedMediaType},
		{"bad JSON", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", []byte(`{"csr":`), http.StatusBadRequest, errcode.InvalidJSON},
		{"two objects", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", []byte(`{} {}`), http.StatusBadRequest, errcode.InvalidJSON},
		{"bad CSR", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", renewBody(t, []byte("junk")), http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, errcode.InvalidInput},
		{"too large", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", big, http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, errcode.TooLarge},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			status, b := postJSON(t, c, tc.method, tc.path, tc.contentType, tc.body)
			wantProblemBody(t, status, b, tc.status, tc.code, "")
		})
	}
}

func TestServerRenewRefusesARevokedHost(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	s := connectAgent(t, f, enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1"), "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	// The session stays up because nothing calls Disconnect, as when a
	// revocation's transaction commits just before the renewal.
	if _, err := f.db.Writer().Exec(`UPDATE core_hosts SET state = 'revoked' WHERE id = 'H1'`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	csr, _ := newCSR(t)
	status, b := postJSON(t, sessionHTTP(s), http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", renewBody(t, csr))
	wantProblemBody(t, status, b, http.StatusForbidden, errcode.Forbidden, "host H1 is not enrolled")
}

func TestRevokedHostIsDisconnectedAndRefused(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	ctx := context.Background()
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	cert := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	s := connectAgent(t, f, cert, "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))

	if err := f.db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { return RevokeHost(ctx, tx, "H1", f.clock.Now()) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.reg.Disconnect("H1")
	wantOffline(t, f.reg, "H1")
	eventually(t, "the agent to see its session closed", func() bool { return ended(s) })

	wantRefused(t, f, cert, "H1")
}

func TestServerRefusesForeignAndRevokedCertificates(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)

	pub, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	san, err := url.Parse(transport.HostURI("H1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(7), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "self-signed"}, URIs: []*url.URL{san},
		NotBefore: testStart.Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: testStart.Add(time.Hour),
		ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}, BasicConstraintsValid: true, IsCA: true,
		KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature | x509.KeyUsageCertSign,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, pub, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	foreign := tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{der}, PrivateKey: key}
	wantRefused(t, f, foreign, "H1")

	revoked := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	if _, err := f.db.Writer().Exec(`UPDATE core_agent_certs SET revoked_at = ?`, formatTime(testStart)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantRefused(t, f, revoked, "H1")
}

func TestServerBoundsTheHandshake(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, func(c *ServerConfig) { c.HandshakeTimeout = 50 * time.Millisecond })
	var d net.Dialer
	conn, err := d.DialContext(context.Background(), "tcp4", f.addr)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
	_ = conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second))
	_, err = conn.Read(make([]byte, 1))
	var netErr net.Error
	if err == nil || errors.As(err, &netErr) && netErr.Timeout() {
		t.Fatalf("a silent client was not cut off: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestServerClose(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	cert := enrolledCert(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	s := connectAgent(t, f, cert, "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))

	f.srv.Close()
	eventually(t, "Close to end the agent's session", func() bool { return ended(s) })
	eventually(t, "the registry to drop the host", func() bool {
		_, _, err := f.reg.Client(context.Background(), "H1")
		return err != nil
	})
	if conn, err := dialAgent(t, f, cert); err == nil {
		_ = conn.Close()
		t.Fatal("a closed server completed a handshake")
	}
}

func TestNewServerRefusesBadConfig(t *testing.T) {
	e := newRegistry(t, transport.HeartbeatConfig{})
	a, r, l := e.a, e.r, NewEnrollLimiter(e.clock)
	for name, cfg := range map[string]ServerConfig{
		"no authority":        {Registry: r, Limiter: l},
		"no registry":         {Authority: a, Limiter: l},
		"no limiter":          {Authority: a, Registry: r},
		"negative handshake":  {Authority: a, Registry: r, Limiter: l, HandshakeTimeout: -time.Second},
		"negative enrollment": {Authority: a, Registry: r, Limiter: l, EnrollTimeout: -time.Second},
	} {
		if _, err := NewServer(cfg); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: NewServer accepted the configuration", name)
		}
	}
}

func TestProblemHidesInternalCauses(t *testing.T) {
	rec := &recordedAnswer{header: http.Header{}}
	writeProblem(rec, quietLog, errors.New("fleet: secret path /var/lib/darkpanel"))
	if strings.Contains(rec.body.String(), "secret") || rec.status != http.StatusInternalServerError {
		t.Fatalf("internal answer %d %s", rec.status, rec.body.String())
	}
	wantProblemBody(t, rec.status, rec.body.Bytes(), http.StatusInternalServerError, errcode.Internal, errcode.Internal.Title())

	rec = &recordedAnswer{header: http.Header{}}
	writeProblem(rec, quietLog, errcode.Wrap(errcode.Forbidden, errors.New("hidden cause"), "visible detail"))
	if strings.Contains(rec.body.String(), "hidden") {
		t.Fatalf("the cause leaked: %s", rec.body.String())
	}
	wantProblemBody(t, rec.status, rec.body.Bytes(), http.StatusForbidden, errcode.Forbidden, "visible detail")
	if got := rec.header.Get("Content-Type"); got != problemContentType {
		t.Fatalf("Content-Type = %q", got)
	}
}

// recordedAnswer is a minimal http.ResponseWriter for the writer tests.
type recordedAnswer struct {
	header http.Header
	status int
	body   bytes.Buffer
}

func (r *recordedAnswer) Header() http.Header { return r.header }

func (r *recordedAnswer) WriteHeader(status int) { r.status = status }

func (r *recordedAnswer) Write(p []byte) (int, error) { return r.body.Write(p) }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the problem writer**

Create `internal/controller/fleet/problem.go`:

```go
package fleet

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"mime"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/wire"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
)

const problemContentType = "application/problem+json"

// problem is an RFC 9457 problem details body, the shape every Darkpanel
// API answers a refusal with.
type problem struct {
	Type   string               `json:"type"`
	Title  string               `json:"title"`
	Status int                  `json:"status"`
	Detail string               `json:"detail"`
	Code   errcode.Code         `json:"code"`
	Errors []errcode.FieldError `json:"errors,omitempty"`
}

// writeProblem answers err as problem details; every refusal of the agent
// listener goes through it. A client sees only the code's fixed detail,
// never err.Error(): a code outside the registry becomes internal, and a
// 5xx answer carries only the code's title because neither its detail nor
// its cause is client-safe. The cause is logged instead.
func writeProblem(w http.ResponseWriter, log *slog.Logger, err error) {
	e := errcode.From(err)
	code := e.Code
	if !errcode.Known(code) {
		code = errcode.Internal
	}
	status := code.Status()
	p := problem{Type: "about:blank", Title: code.Title(), Status: status, Detail: e.Detail, Code: code, Errors: e.Fields}
	if status >= http.StatusInternalServerError {
		p.Detail, p.Errors = code.Title(), nil
		log.Error("fleet: request failed", "code", e.Code, "err", err)
	}
	if p.Detail == "" {
		p.Detail = code.Title()
	}
	b, merr := json.Marshal(p)
	if merr != nil {
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("fleet: marshal problem: %v", merr)) // strings, ints and field errors always marshal
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", problemContentType)
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_, _ = w.Write(b)
}

// writeJSON answers v as JSON with status.
func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, log *slog.Logger, status int, v any) {
	b, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		writeProblem(w, log, fmt.Errorf("fleet: marshal response: %w", err))
		return
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_, _ = w.Write(b)
}

// decodeBody reads one JSON object of at most wire.MaxBody bytes, sent as
// application/json, into dst. Unknown members are ignored: an agent one
// minor newer may send members this controller does not know.
func decodeBody(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, dst any) error {
	mediaType, _, err := mime.ParseMediaType(r.Header.Get("Content-Type"))
	if err != nil || mediaType != "application/json" {
		return errcode.New(errcode.UnsupportedMediaType, "send the request body with Content-Type: application/json")
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, wire.MaxBody))
	err = dec.Decode(dst)
	if err == nil {
		if _, extra := dec.Token(); !errors.Is(extra, io.EOF) {
			err = errors.New("fleet: data after the JSON object")
		}
	}
	var tooLarge *http.MaxBytesError
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &tooLarge):
		return errcode.New(errcode.TooLarge, fmt.Sprintf("the request body exceeds %d bytes", wire.MaxBody))
	case err != nil:
		return errcode.Wrap(errcode.InvalidJSON, err, "the request body must be one JSON object")
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the server**

Create `internal/controller/fleet/server.go`:

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// HandshakeTimeout bounds the TLS handshake of each accepted connection, so
// a client that connects and stays silent holds nothing for long.
const HandshakeTimeout = 10 * time.Second

// EnrollTimeout bounds an enrollment connection from the end of its
// handshake to the end of its answer.
const EnrollTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// Limits of the HTTP server on the streams an agent opens (renewal only).
const (
	streamHeaderTimeout = 10 * time.Second
	streamReadTimeout   = 30 * time.Second
	streamWriteTimeout  = 30 * time.Second
)

// ServerConfig configures NewServer.
type ServerConfig struct {
	Authority *Authority
	Registry  *Registry
	// Limiter admits the requests of enrollment connections.
	Limiter *EnrollLimiter
	// Log receives refused handshakes and failed requests; nil discards.
	Log *slog.Logger
	// HandshakeTimeout bounds each TLS handshake; zero means
	// HandshakeTimeout.
	HandshakeTimeout time.Duration
	// EnrollTimeout bounds each enrollment request; zero means
	// EnrollTimeout.
	EnrollTimeout time.Duration
}

// Server serves the agent listener's connections (spec section 4): each one
// is a TLS handshake whose ALPN picks the protocol. An agent connection
// becomes the host's session in the registry and serves the agent's
// certificate renewals. Its methods are safe for concurrent use.
type Server struct {
	cfg    ServerConfig
	tls    transport.ServerConfig
	ctx    context.Context
	cancel context.CancelFunc

	mu     sync.Mutex
	conns  map[net.Conn]struct{}
	closed bool
	wg     sync.WaitGroup
}

// NewServer returns a server over cfg's authority and registry.
func NewServer(cfg ServerConfig) (*Server, error) {
	switch {
	case cfg.Authority == nil || cfg.Registry == nil || cfg.Limiter == nil:
		return nil, errors.New("fleet: the server needs an authority, a registry and an enrollment limiter")
	case cfg.HandshakeTimeout < 0 || cfg.EnrollTimeout < 0:
		return nil, errors.New("fleet: the server's timeouts must be zero or positive")
	}
	if cfg.HandshakeTimeout == 0 {
		cfg.HandshakeTimeout = HandshakeTimeout
	}
	if cfg.EnrollTimeout == 0 {
		cfg.EnrollTimeout = EnrollTimeout
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	a := cfg.Authority
	return &Server{
		cfg: cfg,
		// Every handshake builds its own tls.Config from this, so no session
		// ticket key is ever shared between connections.
		tls:    transport.ServerConfig{Certificate: a.GetCertificate, AgentRoots: a.Pool(), CheckPeer: a.CheckPeer},
		ctx:    ctx,
		cancel: cancel,
		conns:  map[net.Conn]struct{}{},
	}, nil
}

// Serve accepts connections on ln until ln is closed, then returns nil. A
// failed Accept is retried after a pause that grows to one second. After
// Close it closes every connection it accepts.
func (s *Server) Serve(ln net.Listener) error {
	var delay time.Duration
	for {
		raw, err := ln.Accept()
		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, net.ErrClosed) {
				return nil
			}
			delay = min(max(2*delay, 5*time.Millisecond), time.Second)
			s.cfg.Log.Warn("fleet: accept failed; retrying", "addr", ln.Addr().String(), "err", err, "in", delay)
			time.Sleep(delay)
			continue
		}
		delay = 0
		if !s.track(raw) {
			_ = raw.Close()
			continue
		}
		go s.handle(raw)
	}
}

// Close closes every connection, which ends every agent session, and
// waits for their handlers. It does not close the listeners.
func (s *Server) Close() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.closed = true
	conns := s.conns
	s.conns = map[net.Conn]struct{}{}
	s.mu.Unlock()
	s.cancel()
	for c := range conns {
		_ = c.Close()
	}
	s.wg.Wait()
}

func (s *Server) track(c net.Conn) bool {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return false
	}
	s.conns[c] = struct{}{}
	s.wg.Add(1)
	return true
}

func (s *Server) untrack(c net.Conn) {
	_ = c.Close()
	s.mu.Lock()
	delete(s.conns, c)
	s.mu.Unlock()
	s.wg.Done()
}

func (s *Server) handle(raw net.Conn) {
	defer s.untrack(raw)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(s.ctx, s.cfg.HandshakeTimeout)
	peer, err := transport.Handshake(ctx, raw, s.tls)
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		s.cfg.Log.Debug("fleet: handshake refused", "remote", raw.RemoteAddr().String(), "err", err)
		return
	}
	defer func() { _ = peer.Conn.Close() }()
	switch peer.Protocol {
	case transport.ALPNAgent:
		s.serveAgent(peer)
	}
}

// serveAgent makes an agent connection its host's session and serves the
// streams the agent opens until the session ends.
func (s *Server) serveAgent(peer *transport.Peer) {
	log := s.cfg.Log.With("host", peer.HostID)
	if err := s.cfg.Authority.Handshake(s.ctx, peer.HostID, peer.Serial); err != nil {
		log.Error("fleet: cannot record the agent's handshake", "err", err)
		return
	}
	session, err := transport.ControllerSession(peer.Conn)
	if err != nil {
		log.Warn("fleet: cannot start the agent's session", "err", err)
		return
	}
	if err := s.cfg.Registry.Register(s.ctx, peer.HostID, peer.Serial, session); err != nil {
		log.Info("fleet: agent session refused", "err", err)
		return
	}
	srv := &http.Server{
		Handler:           s.renewHandler(peer.HostID, log),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: streamHeaderTimeout,
		ReadTimeout:       streamReadTimeout,
		WriteTimeout:      streamWriteTimeout,
		MaxHeaderBytes:    64 << 10,
		ErrorLog:          slog.NewLogLogger(log.Handler(), slog.LevelWarn),
		BaseContext:       func(net.Listener) context.Context { return s.ctx },
	}
	// Serve returns once the session ends, whoever ended it.
	_ = srv.Serve(session)
	_ = srv.Close()
}

// renewHandler serves POST transport.RenewPath for hostID, the host the
// session's certificate names; the request names no host of its own.
func (s *Server) renewHandler(hostID string, log *slog.Logger) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.URL.Path != transport.RenewPath {
			writeProblem(w, log, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "the controller serves only "+transport.RenewPath+" to agents"))
			return
		}
		if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
			w.Header().Set("Allow", http.MethodPost)
			writeProblem(w, log, errcode.New(errcode.MethodNotAllowed, transport.RenewPath+" takes POST"))
			return
		}
		var req transport.RenewRequest
		if err := decodeBody(w, r, &req); err != nil {
			writeProblem(w, log, err)
			return
		}
		cert, err := s.cfg.Authority.Renew(r.Context(), hostID, []byte(req.CSR))
		if err != nil {
			writeProblem(w, log, err)
			return
		}
		log.Info("fleet: renewed the agent's certificate")
		writeJSON(w, log, http.StatusOK, transport.RenewResponse{Certificate: string(cert)})
	})
}
```

```go
package fleet

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/tls"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/wire"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// enrollRequest sends one request over a fresh enrollment connection and
// returns the answer. It fails the test unless the server closes the
// connection after answering.
func enrollRequest(t *testing.T, f *fleetServer, method, path, contentType string, body []byte) (int, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	conn, err := transport.Dial(ctx, f.addr, transport.EnrollConfig(f.a.Fingerprint(), f.a.ControllerSAN()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
	_ = conn.SetDeadline(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second))
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, method, "https://controller"+path, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if contentType != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", contentType)
	}
	if err := req.Write(conn); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	br := bufio.NewReader(conn)
	resp, err := http.ReadResponse(br, req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	b, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	_ = resp.Body.Close()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !resp.Close {
		t.Error("the answer does not announce Connection: close")
	}
	if _, err := br.ReadByte(); !errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
		t.Errorf("after the answer the connection gave %v, want EOF", err)
	}
	return resp.StatusCode, b
}

func enrollBody(t *testing.T, token string, csr []byte) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(transport.EnrollRequest{Token: token, CSR: string(csr)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

func TestServerEnrolls(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostPending)
	token, _ := issueToken(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	csr, key := newCSR(t)

	status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, token, csr))
	if status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("enrollment answered %d %s", status, b)
	}
	var resp transport.EnrollResponse
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &resp); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if resp.CA != string(f.a.RootPEM()) {
		t.Fatal("the answer's ca is not the agent root")
	}
	leaf := parseCert(t, []byte(resp.Certificate))
	if id, err := transport.HostIDOf(leaf); err != nil || id != "H1" {
		t.Fatalf("the certificate names %q, %v", id, err)
	}
	if got := hostState(t, f.db, "H1"); got != core.HostEnrolled {
		t.Fatalf("host state = %s", got)
	}

	// The new credentials open an agent session.
	cert := tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{leaf.Raw}, PrivateKey: key, Leaf: leaf}
	connectAgent(t, f, cert, "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	if h, _ := hostHello(t, f.reg, "H1"); h.HostID != "H1" {
		t.Fatalf("hello = %+v", h)
	}
}

func TestServerEnrollRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	limits := coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart)
	f := startServer(t, func(c *ServerConfig) { c.Limiter = NewEnrollLimiter(limits) })
	addHost(t, f.db, "H1", core.HostPending)
	addHost(t, f.db, "H2", core.HostPending)
	used, _ := issueToken(t, f.a, f.db, "H1")
	csr, _ := newCSR(t)
	if status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, used, csr)); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("the first enrollment answered %d %s", status, b)
	}
	fresh, _ := issueToken(t, f.a, f.db, "H2")
	big := append([]byte(`{"token":"`), bytes.Repeat([]byte("a"), wire.MaxBody)...)
	big = append(big, `"}`...)

	const invalid = "the enrollment token is not valid"
	cases := []struct {
		name, method, path, contentType string
		body                            []byte
		status                          int
		code                            errcode.Code
		detail                          string
	}{
		{"reused token", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, used, csr),
			http.StatusForbidden, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid, invalid},
		{"malformed token", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, "dpe1.junk", csr),
			http.StatusForbidden, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid, invalid},
		{"bad CSR", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, fresh, []byte("junk")),
			http.StatusForbidden, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid, invalid},
		{"wrong path", http.MethodPost, transport.RenewPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, fresh, csr),
			http.StatusNotFound, errcode.NotFound, "the enrollment connection serves only " + transport.EnrollPath},
		{"wrong method", http.MethodGet, transport.EnrollPath, "", nil,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed, errcode.MethodNotAllowed, transport.EnrollPath + " takes POST"},
		{"not JSON", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "text/plain", enrollBody(t, fresh, csr),
			http.StatusUnsupportedMediaType, errcode.UnsupportedMediaType, "send the request body with Content-Type: application/json"},
		{"bad JSON", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", []byte(`{"token":`),
			http.StatusBadRequest, errcode.InvalidJSON, "the request body must be one JSON object"},
		{"too large", http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", big,
			http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, errcode.TooLarge, "the request body exceeds 4194304 bytes"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			limits.Advance(time.Minute)
			status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, tc.method, tc.path, tc.contentType, tc.body)
			wantProblemBody(t, status, b, tc.status, tc.code, tc.detail)
			if strings.Contains(string(b), "fleet:") || strings.Contains(string(b), "transport:") {
				t.Fatalf("the answer carries an error's text: %s", b)
			}
		})
	}

	// The refused CSR did not consume H2's token.
	limits.Advance(time.Minute)
	if status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, fresh, csr)); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("H2's token after the refusals answered %d %s", status, b)
	}

	// An expired token reads like every other refusal.
	addHost(t, f.db, "H3", core.HostPending)
	late, _ := issueToken(t, f.a, f.db, "H3")
	f.clock.Advance(TokenTTL)
	limits.Advance(time.Minute)
	status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, late, csr))
	wantProblemBody(t, status, b, http.StatusForbidden, errcode.EnrollTokenInvalid, invalid)
}

func TestServerEnrollRateLimited(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, nil)
	csr, _ := newCSR(t)
	body := enrollBody(t, "dpe1.junk", csr)
	for i := range EnrollPerSource {
		status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", body)
		if status != http.StatusForbidden {
			t.Fatalf("request %d answered %d %s", i, status, b)
		}
	}
	status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", body)
	wantProblemBody(t, status, b, http.StatusTooManyRequests, errcode.EnrollRateLimited,
		"too many enrollment requests; wait a minute and try again")

	f.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	if status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", body); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("a minute later the request answered %d %s", status, b)
	}
}

func TestServerBoundsTheEnrollment(t *testing.T) {
	f := startServer(t, func(c *ServerConfig) { c.EnrollTimeout = 50 * time.Millisecond })
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	conn, err := transport.Dial(ctx, f.addr, transport.EnrollConfig(f.a.Fingerprint(), f.a.ControllerSAN()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
	_ = conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second))
	_, err = conn.Read(make([]byte, 1))
	var netErr net.Error
	if err == nil || errors.As(err, &netErr) && netErr.Timeout() {
		t.Fatalf("a silent enrollment client was not cut off: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEnrollSource(t *testing.T) {
	for addr, want := range map[string]string{
		"127.0.0.1:41000":                "127.0.0.1",
		"[::ffff:198.51.100.7]:41000":    "198.51.100.7",
		"[2001:db8:1:2:3:4:5:6]:41000":   "2001:db8:1:2::/64",
		"[2001:db8:1:2:ffff::1]:1":       "2001:db8:1:2::/64",
		"[fe80::1%eth0]:41000":           "fe80::/64",
		"[2001:db8:1:3::1]:41000":        "2001:db8:1:3::/64",
		"@/run/darkpanel/odd-socket":     "@/run/darkpanel/odd-socket",
		"[2001:db8:1:2::1%tailscale0]:1": "2001:db8:1:2::/64",
	} {
		if got := enrollSource(fakeAddr(addr)); got != want {
			t.Errorf("enrollSource(%s) = %s, want %s", addr, got, want)
		}
	}
}

type fakeAddr string

func (a fakeAddr) Network() string { return "tcp" }

func (a fakeAddr) String() string { return string(a) }
```

Create `internal/controller/fleet/enroll.go`:

```go
package fleet

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"io"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/wire"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// maxEnrollRequest bounds everything an enrollment connection may send: the
// body limit plus room for the request line and headers.
const maxEnrollRequest = wire.MaxBody + 64<<10

// serveEnroll answers exactly one request on an enrollment connection; the
// caller closes the connection afterwards.
func (s *Server) serveEnroll(peer *transport.Peer) {
	conn := peer.Conn
	source := enrollSource(conn.RemoteAddr())
	log := s.cfg.Log.With("source", source)
	_ = conn.SetDeadline(time.Now().Add(s.cfg.EnrollTimeout))
	req, err := http.ReadRequest(bufio.NewReader(io.LimitReader(conn, maxEnrollRequest)))
	if err != nil {
		log.Debug("fleet: unreadable enrollment request", "err", err)
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(s.ctx, s.cfg.EnrollTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	answer := &enrollAnswer{header: http.Header{}}
	s.enroll(answer, req.WithContext(ctx), source)
	if err := answer.response(req).Write(conn); err != nil {
		log.Debug("fleet: cannot send the enrollment answer", "err", err)
	}
}

// enroll handles one enrollment request from source. Every request counts
// against the rate limits, whatever it asks for. A refusal carries only its
// code's fixed detail: Authority.Enroll answers every token or CSR failure
// as enroll.token_invalid with one detail, and its causes are logged here,
// never sent.
func (s *Server) enroll(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, source string) {
	log := s.cfg.Log.With("source", source)
	if err := s.cfg.Limiter.Allow(source); err != nil {
		writeProblem(w, log, err)
		return
	}
	if r.URL.Path != transport.EnrollPath {
		writeProblem(w, log, errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "the enrollment connection serves only "+transport.EnrollPath))
		return
	}
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		w.Header().Set("Allow", http.MethodPost)
		writeProblem(w, log, errcode.New(errcode.MethodNotAllowed, transport.EnrollPath+" takes POST"))
		return
	}
	var req transport.EnrollRequest
	if err := decodeBody(w, r, &req); err != nil {
		writeProblem(w, log, err)
		return
	}
	e, err := s.cfg.Authority.Enroll(r.Context(), req.Token, []byte(req.CSR))
	if err != nil {
		log.Info("fleet: enrollment refused", "err", err)
		writeProblem(w, log, err)
		return
	}
	log.Info("fleet: host enrolled", "host", e.HostID)
	writeJSON(w, log, http.StatusOK, transport.EnrollResponse{Certificate: string(e.Certificate), CA: string(e.CA)})
}

// enrollSource is the rate limiter's key for a client address, the rule of
// the login limiter: an IPv4-mapped IPv6 address becomes its IPv4 form and
// any other IPv6 address its /64 prefix, because one host commonly holds a
// whole /64 and could otherwise spend the global limit alone. An address
// that does not parse is used as it is.
func enrollSource(addr net.Addr) string {
	ap, err := netip.ParseAddrPort(addr.String())
	if err != nil {
		return addr.String()
	}
	a := ap.Addr().WithZone("").Unmap()
	if a.Is6() {
		p, _ := a.Prefix(64)
		return p.String()
	}
	return a.String()
}

// enrollAnswer collects the one answer of an enrollment connection, which
// has no http.Server to write it.
type enrollAnswer struct {
	header http.Header
	status int
	body   bytes.Buffer
}

func (a *enrollAnswer) Header() http.Header { return a.header }

func (a *enrollAnswer) WriteHeader(status int) {
	if a.status == 0 {
		a.status = status
	}
}

func (a *enrollAnswer) Write(p []byte) (int, error) {
	a.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	return a.body.Write(p)
}

// response is the answer to req, announcing that the connection closes.
func (a *enrollAnswer) response(req *http.Request) *http.Response {
	a.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	return &http.Response{
		StatusCode: a.status, ProtoMajor: 1, ProtoMinor: 1, Header: a.header,
		Body: io.NopCloser(&a.body), ContentLength: int64(a.body.Len()), Close: true, Request: req,
	}
}
```

```go
package listen

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"

	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"sync"
	"syscall"
	"testing"

)

func TestParseAgentListen(t *testing.T) {
	got, err := ParseAgentListen([]string{"10.77.0.10:7443", "[fd00::1]:9000", "10.77.0.10:7443", "[::ffff:10.0.0.5]:7443"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []netip.AddrPort{
		netip.MustParseAddrPort("10.77.0.10:7443"),
		netip.MustParseAddrPort("[fd00::1]:9000"),
		netip.MustParseAddrPort("10.0.0.5:7443"),
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("ParseAgentListen = %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	if got, err := ParseAgentListen(nil); err != nil || got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("no values = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	for _, v := range []string{
		"0.0.0.0:7443", "[::]:7443", "10.0.0.1", "10.0.0.1:0", "[fe80::1%eth0]:7443", "224.0.0.1:7443",
		"controller:7443", "https://10.0.0.1:7443", "",
	} {
		if _, err := ParseAgentListen([]string{v}); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("ParseAgentListen accepted %q", v)
		}
	}
}

func TestAgentEndpoints(t *testing.T) {
	got := AgentEndpoints(
		[]netip.AddrPort{netip.MustParseAddrPort("10.77.0.10:7443"), netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:7443")},
		addrs("fd7a:115c:a1e0::1", "100.64.0.1"))
	want := []Endpoint{
		{Addr: netip.MustParseAddrPort("10.77.0.10:7443"), TLS: true},
		{Addr: netip.MustParseAddrPort("100.64.0.1:7443"), TLS: true, FreeBind: true},
		{Addr: netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:7443"), TLS: true},
		{Addr: netip.MustParseAddrPort("[fd7a:115c:a1e0::1]:7443"), TLS: true, FreeBind: true},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("AgentEndpoints =\n%v\nwant\n%v", got, want)
	}
	if got := AgentEndpoints(nil, nil); len(got) != 1 || got[0].Addr != netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:7443") {
		t.Fatalf("without Tailscale or extras: %v, want loopback alone", got)
	}
	for _, e := range AgentEndpoints(nil, addrs("100.64.0.1", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::1")) {
		if e.Addr.Addr().IsUnspecified() {
			t.Fatalf("an endpoint binds every interface: %v", e)
		}
	}
}

// greeter is an AgentConfig.Serve that answers every connection with
// "agent" and records the listeners it served.
type greeter struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	served []net.Listener
	ended  int
}

func (g *greeter) serve(ln net.Listener) error {
	g.mu.Lock()
	g.served = append(g.served, ln)
	g.mu.Unlock()
	defer func() {
		g.mu.Lock()
		g.ended++
		g.mu.Unlock()
	}()
	for {
		c, err := ln.Accept()
		if errors.Is(err, net.ErrClosed) {
			return nil
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, _ = c.Write([]byte("agent"))
		_ = c.Close()
	}
}

func (g *greeter) counts() (served, ended int) {
	g.mu.Lock()
	defer g.mu.Unlock()
	return len(g.served), g.ended
}

// greet dials addr and returns what the server sent, or the dial error.
func greet(addr net.Addr) (string, error) {
	var d net.Dialer
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	c, err := d.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr.String())
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer func() { _ = c.Close() }()
	_ = c.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second))
	b, err := io.ReadAll(c)
	return string(b), err
}

type agentFixture struct {
	db     *store.DB
	clock  *coretest.FakeClock
	ifs    *fakeInterfaces
	binder *fakeBinder
	g      *greeter
}

func newAgentFixture(t *testing.T) *agentFixture {
	t.Helper()
	return &agentFixture{
		db: openControllerDB(t), clock: coretest.NewFakeClock(testStart),
		ifs: &fakeInterfaces{}, binder: &fakeBinder{}, g: &greeter{},
	}
}

func (f *agentFixture) start(t *testing.T, extra ...string) (*AgentService, error) {
	t.Helper()
	ex, err := ParseAgentListen(extra)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err := StartAgent(context.Background(), AgentConfig{
		Extra: ex, DB: f.db, Clock: f.clock, Serve: f.g.serve, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler),
		Interfaces: f.ifs.list, Bind: f.binder.bind,
	})
	if err == nil {
		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = s.Shutdown(context.Background()) })
	}
	return s, err
}

func agentEndpointsOf(bound []Bound) []string {
	out := make([]string, 0, len(bound))
	for _, b := range bound {
		out = append(out, b.Endpoint.Addr.String())
	}
	return out
}

func TestAgentServiceFollowsTailscale(t *testing.T) {
	f := newAgentFixture(t)
	f.ifs.set(true, "100.64.0.1")
	s, err := f.start(t, "10.77.0.10:7443")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	bound := s.Bound()
	if got, want := agentEndpointsOf(bound), []string{"10.77.0.10:7443", "100.64.0.1:7443", "127.0.0.1:7443"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("bound %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	for _, b := range bound {
		if got, err := greet(b.Addr); err != nil || got != "agent" {
			t.Fatalf("%s answered %q, %v", b.Endpoint.Addr, got, err)
		}
	}
	if savedAddrs(t, f.db) != `["100.64.0.1"]` {
		t.Fatalf("saved = %s", savedAddrs(t, f.db))
	}

	f.ifs.set(true, "100.64.0.2")
	f.clock.Advance(CheckInterval)
	next := s.Bound()
	if got, want := agentEndpointsOf(next), []string{"10.77.0.10:7443", "100.64.0.2:7443", "127.0.0.1:7443"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("after the change bound %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	if _, err := greet(bound[1].Addr); !errors.Is(err, syscall.ECONNREFUSED) {
		t.Fatalf("the old Tailscale socket still answers: %v", err)
	}
	if got, err := greet(bound[2].Addr); err != nil || got != "agent" {
		t.Fatalf("loopback stopped answering after the change: %q, %v", got, err)
	}

	f.ifs.set(false)
	f.clock.Advance(CheckInterval)
	if got := agentEndpointsOf(s.Bound()); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, agentEndpointsOf(next)) {
		t.Fatalf("an absent interface changed the binds to %v", got)
	}

	if err := s.Shutdown(context.Background()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if served, ended := f.g.counts(); served != 4 || ended != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("Serve ran on %d listeners and returned on %d, want 4 and 4", served, ended)
	}
	if got := s.Bound(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("after Shutdown bound %v", got)
	}
	for _, b := range next {
		if _, err := greet(b.Addr); err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s answers after Shutdown", b.Endpoint.Addr)
		}
	}
	pending := f.clock.Pending()
	f.clock.Advance(CheckInterval)
	if f.clock.Pending() > pending || len(f.binder.called()) != 4 {
		t.Fatal("a check ran after Shutdown")
	}
}

func TestAgentServiceStartsFromSavedAddresses(t *testing.T) {
	f := newAgentFixture(t)
	if err := SaveTailscaleAddrs(context.Background(), f.db, addrs("100.64.0.7")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err := f.start(t)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, want := agentEndpointsOf(s.Bound()), []string{"100.64.0.7:7443", "127.0.0.1:7443"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("bound %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestAgentServiceStartFailsOnABusyAddress(t *testing.T) {
	f := newAgentFixture(t)
	f.binder.fail = map[netip.AddrPort]error{netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:7443"): syscall.EADDRINUSE}
	if _, err := f.start(t, "10.77.0.10:7443"); !errors.Is(err, syscall.EADDRINUSE) {
		t.Fatalf("StartAgent = %v, want the bind error", err)
	}
	if served, ended := f.g.counts(); served != ended {
		t.Fatalf("a failed start left %d of %d listeners serving", served-ended, served)
	}
}

func TestAgentServiceRetriesAFailedBind(t *testing.T) {
	f := newAgentFixture(t)
	s, err := f.start(t)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.binder.mu.Lock()
	f.binder.fail = map[netip.AddrPort]error{netip.MustParseAddrPort("100.64.0.1:7443"): syscall.EADDRINUSE}
	f.binder.mu.Unlock()
	f.ifs.set(true, "100.64.0.1")
	f.clock.Advance(CheckInterval)
	if got := agentEndpointsOf(s.Bound()); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"127.0.0.1:7443"}) {
		t.Fatalf("bound %v while the Tailscale bind fails", got)
	}
	f.binder.mu.Lock()
	f.binder.fail = nil
	f.binder.mu.Unlock()
	f.clock.Advance(CheckInterval)
	if got := agentEndpointsOf(s.Bound()); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"100.64.0.1:7443", "127.0.0.1:7443"}) {
		t.Fatalf("bound %v; the failed bind was not retried", got)
	}
}

func TestStartAgentRefusesBadConfig(t *testing.T) {
	f := newAgentFixture(t)
	for name, cfg := range map[string]AgentConfig{
		"no serve":    {DB: f.db, Clock: f.clock},
		"no database": {Clock: f.clock, Serve: f.g.serve},
		"no clock":    {DB: f.db, Serve: f.g.serve},
	} {
		if s, err := StartAgent(context.Background(), cfg); err == nil {
			_ = s.Shutdown(context.Background())
			t.Errorf("%s: StartAgent accepted the configuration", name)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/listen"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// TestAgentListenerHandshake wires the agent listener as the controller
// does: listen.StartAgent serving Server.Serve. An agent completes the
// mutual-TLS handshake on the loopback endpoint, so the peer check is
// wired, and an enrollment answers on the same socket.
func TestAgentListenerHandshake(t *testing.T) {
	a, db, clock := newAuthority(t)
	reg, err := NewRegistry(RegistryConfig{
		DB: db, Clock: clock, CheckPeer: a.CheckPeer, Version: "1.2.3", ControllerID: a.ControllerID(),
		Heartbeat: transport.HeartbeatConfig{Interval: time.Hour}, Log: quietLog,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	srv, err := NewServer(ServerConfig{Authority: a, Registry: reg, Limiter: NewEnrollLimiter(clock), Log: quietLog})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Every endpoint binds a free loopback port: the test host may lack the
	// addresses, and 7443 may be taken.
	bind := func(ctx context.Context, _ listen.Endpoint) (net.Listener, error) {
		var lc net.ListenConfig
		return lc.Listen(ctx, "tcp4", "127.0.0.1:0")
	}
	ls, err := listen.StartAgent(context.Background(), listen.AgentConfig{
		DB: db, Clock: clock, Serve: srv.Serve, Log: quietLog, Bind: bind,
		Interfaces: func(string) ([]netip.Addr, bool, error) { return nil, false, nil },
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if err := ls.Shutdown(context.Background()); err != nil {
			t.Error(err)
		}
		srv.Close()
		reg.Close()
	})
	bound := ls.Bound()
	if len(bound) != 1 || bound[0].Endpoint.Addr != netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:7443") {
		t.Fatalf("bound %v, want 127.0.0.1:7443 alone", bound)
	}
	f := &fleetServer{a: a, db: db, clock: clock, reg: reg, srv: srv, addr: bound[0].Addr.String()}

	addHost(t, db, "H1", core.HostEnrolled)
	connectAgent(t, f, enrolledCert(t, a, db, "H1"), "H1", helloAgent("H1", "v"))
	if h, _ := hostHello(t, reg, "H1"); h.HostID != "H1" {
		t.Fatalf("hello = %+v", h)
	}

	addHost(t, db, "H2", core.HostPending)
	token, _ := issueToken(t, a, db, "H2")
	csr, _ := newCSR(t)
	if status, b := enrollRequest(t, f, http.MethodPost, transport.EnrollPath, "application/json", enrollBody(t, token, csr)); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("enrollment through the listener answered %d %s", status, b)
	}
}
```
