Create `internal/controller/listen/agent.go`:

```go
package listen

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/netip"
	"slices"
	"sync"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
)

// AgentPort is the agent listener's port on loopback and on each Tailscale
// address (spec section 4).
const AgentPort = 7443

var agentLoopback = netip.AddrPortFrom(netip.MustParseAddr("127.0.0.1"), AgentPort)

// ParseAgentListen parses the --agent-listen values, each <ip>:<port> of a
// specific address of this host. Repeated values count once. It refuses
// an unspecified address, so the agent listener never binds every
// interface.
func ParseAgentListen(values []string) ([]netip.AddrPort, error) {
	var out []netip.AddrPort
	for _, v := range values {
		ap, err := netip.ParseAddrPort(v)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("listen: --agent-listen %q is not <ip>:<port>", v)
		}
		addr := ap.Addr()
		switch {
		case addr.Zone() != "":
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("listen: --agent-listen %q: the address must not carry a zone", v)
		case ap.Port() == 0:
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("listen: --agent-listen %q needs a port between 1 and 65535", v)
		}
		addr = addr.Unmap()
		if addr.IsUnspecified() || addr.IsMulticast() {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("listen: --agent-listen %q: the address must be a specific address of this host", v)
		}
		if ap = netip.AddrPortFrom(addr, ap.Port()); !slices.Contains(out, ap) {
			out = append(out, ap)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// AgentEndpoints returns the agent listener's endpoints: 127.0.0.1:7443
// always, each Tailscale address on AgentPort (bound with FreeBind, as the
// web listener's are) and each extra address, sorted by address and without
// repeats. Every endpoint speaks TLS.
func AgentEndpoints(extra []netip.AddrPort, tailscale []netip.Addr) []Endpoint {
	byAddr := map[netip.AddrPort]Endpoint{agentLoopback: {Addr: agentLoopback, TLS: true}}
	for _, a := range tailscale {
		ap := netip.AddrPortFrom(a, AgentPort)
		byAddr[ap] = Endpoint{Addr: ap, TLS: true, FreeBind: true}
	}
	for _, ap := range extra {
		if _, ok := byAddr[ap]; !ok {
			byAddr[ap] = Endpoint{Addr: ap, TLS: true}
		}
	}
	out := make([]Endpoint, 0, len(byAddr))
	for _, e := range byAddr {
		out = append(out, e)
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b Endpoint) int { return a.Addr.Compare(b.Addr) })
	return out
}

// AgentConfig configures StartAgent.
type AgentConfig struct {
	Extra []netip.AddrPort // from ParseAgentListen
	DB    *store.DB        // the Tailscale addresses saved in core_meta
	Clock core.Clock
	// Serve serves the agent connections of one bound socket until the
	// socket is closed, then returns nil: fleet.Server.Serve.
	Serve func(net.Listener) error
	// Log receives binds, failures and Tailscale changes; nil discards.
	Log *slog.Logger
	// Interfaces lists interface addresses; nil means SystemInterfaceAddrs.
	Interfaces InterfaceAddrs
	// Bind opens the sockets; nil means Bind.
	Bind BindFunc
}

// AgentService runs the agent listener: it binds AgentEndpoints and, every
// CheckInterval, follows the Tailscale addresses as the web listener does.
// It owns the sockets only; Serve owns the connections it accepts. Its
// methods are safe for concurrent use.
type AgentService struct {
	cfg AgentConfig

	mu     sync.Mutex // serialises checks and Shutdown
	ts     *TailscaleWatcher
	bound  map[Endpoint]net.Listener
	timer  core.Timer
	closed bool
	wg     sync.WaitGroup // Serve calls
}

// StartAgent binds the agent listener and arms the periodic check. A failed
// bind fails it, with every socket it bound closed again.
func StartAgent(ctx context.Context, cfg AgentConfig) (*AgentService, error) {
	if cfg.Serve == nil || cfg.DB == nil || cfg.Clock == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("listen: the agent listener needs Serve, a database and a clock")
	}
	if cfg.Log == nil {
		cfg.Log = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	}
	if cfg.Bind == nil {
		cfg.Bind = Bind
	}
	ts, err := NewTailscaleWatcher(ctx, cfg.DB, cfg.Interfaces, cfg.Log)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	s := &AgentService{cfg: cfg, ts: ts, bound: map[Endpoint]net.Listener{}}
	s.mu.Lock()
	err = s.update(ctx)
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if err != nil {
		_ = s.Shutdown(ctx)
		return nil, err
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.timer = cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(CheckInterval, s.check)
	s.mu.Unlock()
	return s, nil
}

// update makes the bound sockets those of AgentEndpoints, closing the
// others; s.mu is held. It returns the bind failures joined; a later check
// retries them.
func (s *AgentService) update(ctx context.Context) error {
	want := AgentEndpoints(s.cfg.Extra, s.ts.Addrs())
	for e, ln := range s.bound {
		if !slices.Contains(want, e) {
			_ = ln.Close()
			delete(s.bound, e)
			s.cfg.Log.Info("listen: stopped serving agents", "addr", e.Addr.String())
		}
	}
	var errs []error
	for _, e := range want {
		if s.bound[e] != nil {
			continue
		}
		ln, err := s.cfg.Bind(ctx, e)
		if err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, err)
			continue
		}
		s.bound[e] = ln
		s.wg.Add(1)
		go s.serve(e, ln)
		s.cfg.Log.Info("listen: serving agents", "addr", e.Addr.String(), "bound", ln.Addr().String())
	}
	return errors.Join(errs...)
}

func (s *AgentService) serve(e Endpoint, ln net.Listener) {
	defer s.wg.Done()
	if err := s.cfg.Serve(ln); err != nil {
		s.cfg.Log.Error("listen: serving agents stopped", "addr", e.Addr.String(), "err", err)
	}
}

func (s *AgentService) check() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), CheckInterval)
	defer cancel()
	s.ts.Refresh(ctx)
	if err := s.update(ctx); err != nil {
		s.cfg.Log.Warn("listen: not every agent listener is bound; retrying at the next check", "err", err)
	}
	s.timer = s.cfg.Clock.AfterFunc(CheckInterval, s.check)
}

// Bound returns the bound sockets sorted by endpoint address, none after
// Shutdown.
func (s *AgentService) Bound() []Bound {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]Bound, 0, len(s.bound))
	for e, ln := range s.bound {
		out = append(out, Bound{Endpoint: e, Addr: ln.Addr()})
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b Bound) int { return a.Endpoint.Addr.Compare(b.Endpoint.Addr) })
	return out
}

// Shutdown stops the checks, closes every socket and waits until every
// Serve call has returned or ctx ends. Connections Serve accepted stay
// open; their owner closes them.
func (s *AgentService) Shutdown(ctx context.Context) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.closed = true
	if s.timer != nil {
		s.timer.Stop()
	}
	for e, ln := range s.bound {
		_ = ln.Close()
		delete(s.bound, e)
	}
	s.mu.Unlock()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		s.wg.Wait()
		close(done)
	}()
	select {
	case <-done:
		return nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return fmt.Errorf("listen: agent listener shutdown: %w", ctx.Err())
	}
}
```

```go
package daemon

import (

// probeModule has one agent read that reports who ran it where.
type probeModule struct{}

type probeIn struct {
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

type probeOut struct {
	HostID string `json:"hostId"`
	Actor  string `json:"actor"`
}

type probeRead struct{}

func (probeRead) Read(_ context.Context, env core.Env, _ probeIn) (probeOut, error) {
	return probeOut{HostID: env.HostID, Actor: env.Principal.Actor}, nil
}

var refProbe = core.ActionRef{Name: "probe.item.get", Version: 1}

func (probeModule) Manifest() core.Manifest {
	return core.Manifest{ID: "probe", Version: 1, DefaultEnabled: true, Actions: []core.ActionDef{
		core.DefineRead(core.ActionSpec{Name: refProbe.Name, Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}, probeRead{}),
	}}
}

// fakeRecoverer records the daemon's start recovery.
type fakeRecoverer struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	modes  []update.RecoverMode
	err    error
	during func() // runs inside Recover
}

func (f *fakeRecoverer) Recover(_ context.Context, mode update.RecoverMode, tx string) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	f.modes = append(f.modes, mode)
	f.mu.Unlock()
	if tx != "" {
		return errors.New("fakeRecoverer: unexpected transaction " + tx)
	}
	if f.during != nil {
		f.during()
	}
	return f.err
}

func (f *fakeRecoverer) calls() []update.RecoverMode {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(f.modes)
}

// stubSystemd knows no unit and accepts every job.
type stubSystemd struct{}

func (stubSystemd) StartTransientService(context.Context, string, platform.TransientService) error {
	return nil
}
func (stubSystemd) StartTransientTimer(context.Context, string, platform.TransientTimer) error {
	return nil
}
func (stubSystemd) Start(context.Context, string) error                { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Stop(context.Context, string) error                 { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Restart(context.Context, string) error              { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Kill(context.Context, string, syscall.Signal) error { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) ResetFailed(context.Context, string) error          { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Enable(context.Context, ...string) error            { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Disable(context.Context, ...string) error           { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) Reload(context.Context) error                       { return nil }
func (stubSystemd) State(context.Context, string) (platform.UnitState, error) {
	return platform.UnitState{LoadState: "not-found", ActiveState: "inactive", SubState: "dead"}, nil
}

// noUsers finds no user.
type noUsers struct{}

func (noUsers) Lookup(string) (int, int, bool, error) { return 0, 0, false, nil }

// syncBuffer is a log destination the daemon's goroutines share.
type syncBuffer struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	buf bytes.Buffer
}

func (b *syncBuffer) Write(p []byte) (int, error) {
	b.mu.Lock()
	defer b.mu.Unlock()
	return b.buf.Write(p)
}

func (b *syncBuffer) String() string {
	b.mu.Lock()
	defer b.mu.Unlock()
	return b.buf.String()
}

// agentHost is one host's installed layout, agent.db and clock.
type agentHost struct {
	t      *testing.T
	layout install.Layout
	db     *store.DB
	clock  *coretest.FakeClock
	logs   *syncBuffer
	rec    *fakeRecoverer
}

func newAgentHost(t *testing.T) *agentHost {
	t.Helper()
	l := install.Rooted(t.TempDir())
	sockDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "dp")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(sockDir) })
	l.Paths.AgentSocket = filepath.Join(sockDir, "run", "agent.sock")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(l.Paths.AgentDir, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	db, err := store.Open(context.Background(), l.Paths.AgentDB())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = db.Close() })
	return &agentHost{
		t: t, layout: l, db: db, clock: coretest.NewFakeClock(time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second)),
		logs: &syncBuffer{}, rec: &fakeRecoverer{},
	}
}

func (h *agentHost) config() Config {
	h.t.Helper()
	reg, err := core.NewRegistry(probeModule{})
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return Config{
		Paths: h.layout.Paths,
		Runtime: runtime.Config{
			DB: h.db, Sealer: coretest.Sealer{}, Clock: h.clock, Log: slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(h.logs, nil)),
			Registry: reg, BinaryVersion: "0.2.0", BackupDir: h.layout.Paths.AgentBackups(),
			Exec: platform.NewRunner(nil), FilesTestMode: true,
		},
		Updater:   h.rec,
		AllowPeer: func(uint32) bool { return true },
	}
}

// start runs the daemon with cfg until the test ends, and waits until its
// socket answers. The returned function stops it and returns Run's result.
func (h *agentHost) start(cfg Config) func() error {
	h.t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- Run(ctx, cfg) }()
	var once sync.Once
	var result error
	stop := func() error {
		once.Do(func() {
			cancel()
			result = <-done
		})
		return result
	}
	h.t.Cleanup(func() { _ = stop() })
	waitFor(h.t, "the agent socket", func() bool {
		select {
		case err := <-done:
			done <- err
			h.t.Fatalf("Run returned %v before serving", err)
		default:
		}
		_, err := h.socketClient().Hello(context.Background())
		return err == nil
	})
	return stop
}

func (h *agentHost) socketClient() *api.Client {
	return api.NewClient(api.ClientConfig{Dial: func(ctx context.Context) (net.Conn, error) {
		var d net.Dialer
		return d.DialContext(ctx, "unix", h.layout.Paths.AgentSocket)
	}})
}

// hello asks the agent socket for Hello.
func (h *agentHost) hello() core.AgentHello {
	h.t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	hello, err := h.socketClient().Hello(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return hello
}

// waitClock polls cond like waitFor, advancing the clock by the longest
// backoff between polls so the dial loop never sleeps for long.
func (h *agentHost) waitClock(what string, cond func() bool) {
	h.t.Helper()
	waitFor(h.t, what, func() bool {
		if cond() {
			return true
		}
		h.clock.Advance(transport.BackoffMax)
		return false
	})
}

// sessionClient is the controller's client over an accepted agent session.
func sessionClient(ctrl *testController, s *agentSession) *api.Client {
	return api.NewClient(api.ClientConfig{Dial: s.sess.Open, Controller: &api.ControllerHello{
		Protocol: 1, Version: "0.2.0", ControllerID: ctrl.auth.ControllerID(),
	}})
}

func connected(h core.AgentHello) string {
	if h.ControllerConnected == nil {
		return "null"
	}
	if *h.ControllerConnected {
		return "true"
	}
	return "false"
}

// Register row 67: uninstall refuses while the daemon runs, and a second
// daemon does not start.
func TestRunHoldsTheAgentLock(t *testing.T) {
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	stop := h.start(h.config())
	ctx := context.Background()
	u, err := install.PlanUninstall(ctx, install.UninstallConfig{
		Layout: h.layout, Systemd: stubSystemd{}, Exec: platform.NewRunner(nil), Users: noUsers{},
		Clock: h.clock, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), FilesTestMode: true,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	err = u.Run(ctx, io.Discard)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "an agent still runs") {
		t.Fatalf("uninstall Run = %v, want the running agent refused", err)
	}
	if err := Run(ctx, h.config()); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "another agent runs") {
		t.Errorf("a second Run = %v, want it refused", err)
	}
	if err := stop(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Run = %v after its context ended, want nil", err)
	}
	fd, err := unix.Open(filepath.Join(h.layout.Paths.AgentDir, install.AgentLockName), unix.O_RDWR|unix.O_CLOEXEC, 0)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = unix.Close(fd) }()
	if err := unix.Flock(fd, unix.LOCK_EX|unix.LOCK_NB); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("the stopped daemon still holds the lock: %v", err)
	}
}

// Spec S-C20: the start recovery runs before the socket serves, and
// update.in_progress is logged without stopping the start.
func TestRunRecoversBeforeServing(t *testing.T) {
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	h.rec.err = errcode.New(errcode.UpdateInProgress, "a runner holds the update lock")
	var socketDuring bool
	h.rec.during = func() {
		_, err := os.Lstat(h.layout.Paths.AgentSocket)
		socketDuring = err == nil
	}
	h.start(h.config())
	if got := h.rec.calls(); !slices.Equal(got, []update.RecoverMode{update.RecoverStart}) {
		t.Errorf("Recover modes = %v, want one RecoverStart", got)
	}
	if socketDuring {
		t.Error("the socket existed while the start recovery ran")
	}
	if logs := h.logs.String(); !strings.Contains(logs, "update.in_progress") {
		t.Errorf("the recovery error was not logged:\n%s", logs)
	}
}

// The real update library at start: a reservation whose operation has no
// record is swept.
func TestRunSweepsStaleReservations(t *testing.T) {
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	cfg := h.config()
	for _, dir := range []string{h.layout.Paths.UpdateDir, h.layout.Paths.BinaryDir} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	u, err := update.New(update.Config{
		Paths: h.layout.Paths, DB: h.db, Systemd: stubSystemd{}, Clock: h.clock, Version: "0.2.0",
		Log: cfg.Runtime.Log,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg.Updater = u
	// The runtime creates core_locks; a first start and stop migrates.
	stop := h.start(cfg)
	if err := stop(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := h.db.Writer().Exec(`INSERT INTO core_locks (key, owner) VALUES ('*', 'op:01JAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA')`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.start(cfg)
	var n int
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT count(*) FROM core_locks`).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("core_locks holds %d rows (%v) after the start, want none", n, err)
	}
}

func TestRunServesTheSocketBeforeEnrollment(t *testing.T) {
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	h.start(h.config())
	hello := h.hello()
	if hello.HostID != UnenrolledHostID || hello.ControllerConnected != nil || hello.JournalEpoch == "" {
		t.Errorf("Hello = %+v, want the unenrolled host with controllerConnected null", hello)
	}
	out, err := h.socketClient().Read(context.Background(), core.Principal{Actor: "user:mallory"}, refProbe, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"x"}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var got probeOut
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &got); err != nil || got != (probeOut{HostID: UnenrolledHostID, Actor: core.ActorUnenrolled}) {
		t.Errorf("probe over the socket = %s, %v", out, err)
	}
}

func TestRunConnectsAndRedials(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	if err := (Agent{TLSDir: h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir}).Enroll(context.Background(), ctrl.token("H1")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.start(h.config())
	waitFor(t, "a session", func() bool { return len(ctrl.agentSessions()) == 1 })
	s := ctrl.agentSessions()[0]
	ctx := context.Background()
	hello, err := sessionClient(ctrl, s).Hello(ctx)
	if err != nil || hello.HostID != "H1" || connected(hello) != "true" {
		t.Fatalf("Hello over the session = %+v, %v", hello, err)
	}
	alice := core.Principal{Actor: "user:alice", Scope: core.ScopeOperate}
	out, err := sessionClient(ctrl, s).Read(ctx, alice, refProbe, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"x"}`))
	if err != nil || string(out) != `{"actor":"user:alice","hostId":"H1"}` {
		t.Errorf("probe over the session = %s, %v", out, err)
	}
	if hello := h.hello(); hello.HostID != "H1" || connected(hello) != "true" {
		t.Errorf("Hello over the socket = %+v", hello)
	}

	// The controller drops the session: the agent reports it and redials.
	_ = s.sess.Close()
	waitFor(t, "the session to end", func() bool { return connected(h.hello()) == "false" })
	h.waitClock("a second session", func() bool { return len(ctrl.agentSessions()) == 2 })
	waitFor(t, "the agent to report it", func() bool { return connected(h.hello()) == "true" })

	// A revoked agent keeps redialing and keeps serving its socket.
	ctrl.revoke("H1")
	refused := ctrl.refusals()
	_ = ctrl.agentSessions()[1].sess.Close()
	h.waitClock("refused redials", func() bool { return ctrl.refusals() >= refused+2 })
	if hello := h.hello(); connected(hello) != "false" {
		t.Errorf("a refused agent reports controllerConnected %s", connected(hello))
	}
}

func TestRunTakesUpAnEnrollment(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	h.start(h.config())
	before := h.hello()
	if before.HostID != UnenrolledHostID {
		t.Fatalf("Hello before the enrollment = %+v", before)
	}
	if err := (Agent{TLSDir: h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir}).Enroll(context.Background(), ctrl.token("H1")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.waitClock("the agent to dial as H1", func() bool { return len(ctrl.agentSessions()) == 1 })
	waitFor(t, "the socket to serve H1", func() bool {
		hello := h.hello()
		return hello.HostID == "H1" && connected(hello) == "true"
	})
	if after := h.hello(); after.JournalEpoch != before.JournalEpoch {
		t.Errorf("the enrollment changed the journal epoch from %s to %s", before.JournalEpoch, after.JournalEpoch)
	}
	out, err := h.socketClient().Read(context.Background(), core.Principal{Actor: "user:mallory"}, refProbe, json.RawMessage(`{"name":"x"}`))
	if err != nil || !strings.Contains(string(out), `"actor":"local:root@H1"`) {
		t.Errorf("probe over the socket = %s, %v", out, err)
	}
}

// Spec section 2: the previous generation is kept until the first
// successful handshake with the new one, then deleted.
func TestRunDeletesTheOldGenerationAfterAHandshake(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl := newTestController(t, time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	dir := h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir
	old, err := Enroll(context.Background(), dir, ctrl.token("H1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	csr, err := csrPEM(key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// The controller revokes serials created before the one a handshake uses.
	ctrl.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	certPEM, err := ctrl.auth.Renew(context.Background(), "H1", []byte(csr))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := parseCertificate(certPEM)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	next := &Credentials{Key: key, Cert: cert, CA: old.CA, Controller: old.Controller}
	if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, next); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if gens := generations(t, dir); !slices.Equal(gens, []string{"gen-1", "gen-2"}) {
		t.Fatalf("generations before the handshake = %v", gens)
	}
	h.start(h.config())
	waitFor(t, "a session", func() bool { return len(ctrl.agentSessions()) == 1 })
	if s := ctrl.agentSessions()[0]; s.serial != transport.SerialText(cert.SerialNumber) {
		t.Errorf("the agent connected with serial %s, want the new %s", s.serial, transport.SerialText(cert.SerialNumber))
	}
	waitFor(t, "the old generation to go", func() bool { return slices.Equal(generations(t, dir), []string{"gen-2"}) })
	var revoked bool
	if err := ctrl.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT revoked_at IS NOT NULL FROM core_agent_certs WHERE serial = ?`,
		transport.SerialText(old.Cert.SerialNumber)).Scan(&revoked); err != nil || !revoked {
		t.Errorf("the old serial is not revoked after the handshake: %v", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/daemon/daemon.go`:

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/sys/unix"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/api"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/runtime"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/agent/socket"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/install"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/paths"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
)

// UnenrolledHostID is the runtime's host id until the agent enrolls. It
// cannot be a real host id, which is an upper-case ULID.
const UnenrolledHostID = "unenrolled"

// CredentialPoll is how often an agent without credentials looks for the
// generation an enrollment writes.
const CredentialPoll = 5 * time.Second

// Recoverer is the update library's recovery; *update.Updater implements it.
type Recoverer interface {
	Recover(ctx context.Context, mode update.RecoverMode, tx string) error
}

// Config is what darkpanel agent run needs.
type Config struct {
	Paths paths.Paths
	// Runtime is the agent runtime's configuration. The daemon sets HostID
	// and ControllerConnected; Clock and Log are also the daemon's.
	Runtime runtime.Config
	// Updater runs Recover(RecoverStart) at start.
	Updater Recoverer
	// AllowPeer is the agent socket's peer check; nil admits root alone.
	AllowPeer func(uid uint32) bool
	// Heartbeat paces the agent's pings of the controller; the zero value is
	// transport's defaults.
	Heartbeat transport.HeartbeatConfig
}

func (c Config) check() error {
	switch {
	case c.Paths.AgentDir == "" || c.Paths.AgentSocket == "" || c.Paths.AgentTLSDir == "":
		return errors.New("daemon: Paths.AgentDir, AgentSocket and AgentTLSDir are required")
	case c.Updater == nil:
		return errors.New("daemon: Config.Updater is required")
	case c.Runtime.Clock == nil || c.Runtime.Log == nil:
		return errors.New("daemon: Config.Runtime.Clock and Log are required")
	}
	return nil
}

// errIdentityChanged ends the dial loop when the current generation names
// another host than the runtime serves, or the credentials appeared or went.
var errIdentityChanged = errors.New("daemon: the agent's identity changed")

// identity is who the runtime serves as.
type identity struct {
	hostID   string
	enrolled bool
}

func identityOf(c *Credentials) identity {
	if c == nil {
		return identity{hostID: UnenrolledHostID}
	}
	return identity{hostID: c.HostID(), enrolled: true}
}

type daemon struct {
	cfg       Config
	log       *slog.Logger
	clock     core.Clock
	connected atomic.Bool // a controller session is live

	// mu is held for reading by every request on the socket or a session,
	// and for writing while the runtime is replaced.
	mu    sync.RWMutex
	ident identity
	rt    *runtime.Runtime
	sock  http.Handler
}

// Run is darkpanel agent run: it takes the agent lock, opens the runtime as
// the host the credentials name, runs the update library's start recovery,
// serves the agent socket, and keeps one session to the controller until ctx
// ends, when it returns nil. When an enrollment writes credentials for
// another host, it reopens the runtime as that host. Only the dial loop waits
// on the controller (R28).
func Run(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) error {
	if err := cfg.check(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	unlock, err := lockAgent(cfg.Paths.AgentDir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer unlock()
	d := &daemon{cfg: cfg, log: cfg.Runtime.Log, clock: cfg.Runtime.Clock}
	c, err := LoadCredentials(cfg.Paths.AgentTLSDir)
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
		d.log.Error("daemon: the saved credentials cannot be read; serving as unenrolled", "error", err)
	}
	if err := d.open(ctx, identityOf(c)); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { d.rt.Close() }()
	// A runner may hold the update lock while it stages a binary, so a
	// failed recovery is logged and the agent keeps starting: exiting would
	// start a recover pass through OnFailure= in the middle of the update.
	if err := cfg.Updater.Recover(ctx, update.RecoverStart, ""); err != nil {
		d.log.Warn("daemon: update recovery at start", "error", err)
	}
	srv, err := socket.Listen(socket.Config{Path: cfg.Paths.AgentSocket, Handler: d, Log: d.log, Allow: cfg.AllowPeer})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %w", err)
	}
	served := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { served <- srv.Serve() }()
	defer func() {
		_ = srv.Close()
		if err := <-served; err != nil {
			d.log.Error("daemon: agent socket", "error", err)
		}
	}()
	for {
		err := d.dialLoop(ctx)
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if !errors.Is(err, errIdentityChanged) {
			return err
		}
		c, err := LoadCredentials(cfg.Paths.AgentTLSDir)
		if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) {
			continue
		}
		if err := d.reopen(ctx, identityOf(c)); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
}

// lockAgent takes the exclusive lock uninstall checks (register row 67) and
// returns its release.
func lockAgent(dir string) (func(), error) {
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: create %s: %w", dir, err)
	}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, install.AgentLockName)
	fd, err := unix.Open(path, unix.O_RDWR|unix.O_CREAT|unix.O_NOFOLLOW|unix.O_CLOEXEC, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: open %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	if err := unix.Flock(fd, unix.LOCK_EX|unix.LOCK_NB); err != nil {
		_ = unix.Close(fd)
		if errors.Is(err, unix.EWOULDBLOCK) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: another agent runs and holds %s", path)
		}
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: lock %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	return func() { _ = unix.Close(fd) }, nil
}

// open opens the runtime as id and builds the socket handler over it.
func (d *daemon) open(ctx context.Context, id identity) error {
	rc := d.cfg.Runtime
	rc.HostID = id.hostID
	rc.ControllerConnected = func() *bool {
		if !id.enrolled {
			return nil
		}
		v := d.connected.Load()
		return &v
	}
	rt, err := runtime.Open(ctx, rc)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("daemon: %w", err)
	}
	d.ident, d.rt = id, rt
	d.sock = api.NewSocketHandler(api.Config{Agent: rt, Log: d.log}, api.SocketConfig{
		Identity: func() (string, bool) { return id.hostID, id.enrolled },
	})
	d.log.Info("daemon: serving", "host", id.hostID, "enrolled", id.enrolled)
	return nil
}

// reopen replaces the runtime once no request runs on it.
func (d *daemon) reopen(ctx context.Context, id identity) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.rt.Close()
	return d.open(ctx, id)
}

// ServeHTTP serves the agent socket.
func (d *daemon) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	d.mu.RLock()
	defer d.mu.RUnlock()
	d.sock.ServeHTTP(w, r)
}

// dialLoop keeps one session to the controller the credentials name,
// redialing with transport.Backoff, until ctx ends or the identity changes.
func (d *daemon) dialLoop(ctx context.Context) error {
	b := transport.NewBackoff()
	for {
		c, err := LoadCredentials(d.cfg.Paths.AgentTLSDir)
		var delay time.Duration
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled) && !d.ident.enrolled:
			delay = CredentialPoll
		case err == nil && c.HostID() == d.ident.hostID && d.ident.enrolled:
			if err := d.session(ctx, c, b); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil {
				d.log.Warn("daemon: controller session", "error", err)
			}
			delay = b.Next()
		case err == nil, errors.Is(err, ErrNotEnrolled):
			return errIdentityChanged
		default:
			d.log.Error("daemon: read the credentials", "error", err)
			delay = b.Next()
		}
		if err := sleep(ctx, d.clock, delay); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
}

// session connects with c and serves the agent API on the session until it
// ends. A session the controller accepted resets the backoff and deletes
// every other credential generation.
func (d *daemon) session(ctx context.Context, c *Credentials, b *transport.Backoff) error {
	dialCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, DialTimeout)
	sess, err := connect(dialCtx, c)
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	b.Reset()
	if err := PruneGenerations(d.cfg.Paths.AgentTLSDir, c.Generation); err != nil {
		d.log.Warn("daemon: delete old credentials", "error", err)
	}
	d.connected.Store(true)
	defer d.connected.Store(false)
	d.log.Info("daemon: connected to the controller", "address", c.Controller.Address, "generation", c.Generation)

	sctx, stop := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer stop()
	srv := &http.Server{
		Handler:           d.guard(api.NewHandler(api.Config{Agent: d.rt, Log: d.log, OnHello: d.onHello})),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
		ErrorLog:          slog.NewLogLogger(d.log.Handler(), slog.LevelWarn),
	}
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(2)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		_ = srv.Serve(sess)
	}()
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		// The controller's heartbeat takes the host offline; the agent's
		// own notices a controller that vanished without closing the
		// connection, so it redials instead of waiting on a dead session.
		if err := transport.Heartbeat(sctx, sess, d.cfg.Heartbeat); err != nil {
			d.log.Warn("daemon: the controller stopped answering", "error", err)
		}
	}()
	select {
	case <-sess.Done():
	case <-ctx.Done():
	}
	stop()
	_ = srv.Close()
	_ = sess.Close()
	wg.Wait()
	d.log.Info("daemon: disconnected from the controller")
	return nil
}

// guard holds mu for reading around each request, so the runtime is never
// replaced under one.
func (d *daemon) guard(h http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		d.mu.RLock()
		defer d.mu.RUnlock()
		h.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}

func (d *daemon) onHello(h api.ControllerHello) {
	d.log.Info("daemon: controller hello", "protocol", h.Protocol, "version", h.Version, "controller", h.ControllerID)
}

// sleep waits d on clock, or until ctx ends.
func sleep(ctx context.Context, clock core.Clock, d time.Duration) error {
	done := make(chan struct{})
	t := clock.AfterFunc(d, func() { close(done) })
	select {
	case <-done:
		return nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		t.Stop()
		return ctx.Err()
	}
}
```

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/x509"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/controller/fleet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

func TestRenewAt(t *testing.T) {
	start := time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	cert := &x509.Certificate{NotBefore: start, NotAfter: start.Add(fleet.CertValidity)}
	if got, want := renewAt(cert), start.Add(fleet.CertRenewAfter); !got.Equal(want) {
		t.Errorf("renewAt = %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
}

// renewalHost enrolls H1 with a certificate issued 300 days ago, past two
// thirds of its life, then moves the controller's clock to now, so the
// certificates it issues next are fresh.
func renewalHost(t *testing.T) (*testController, *agentHost, *Credentials) {
	t.Helper()
	now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Second)
	ctrl := newTestController(t, now.Add(-300*24*time.Hour))
	ctrl.addHost("H1", "pending")
	h := newAgentHost(t)
	old, err := Enroll(context.Background(), h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir, ctrl.token("H1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ctrl.clock.Advance(300 * 24 * time.Hour)
	return ctrl, h, old
}

// Spec section 2, Renewal: after two thirds of its life the agent renews
// over its live session, saves a new generation, reconnects with it, and the
// first handshake with it deletes the old generation.
func TestRunRenewsTheCertificate(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl, h, old := renewalHost(t)
	dir := h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir
	h.start(h.config())
	h.waitClock("a session with the renewed certificate", func() bool { return len(ctrl.agentSessions()) == 2 })
	sessions := ctrl.agentSessions()
	if sessions[0].serial != transport.SerialText(old.Cert.SerialNumber) {
		t.Errorf("the first session used serial %s, want the enrolled %s", sessions[0].serial, transport.SerialText(old.Cert.SerialNumber))
	}
	current, err := LoadCredentials(dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if current.Generation != 2 || current.Key.Equal(old.Key) || sessions[1].serial != transport.SerialText(current.Cert.SerialNumber) {
		t.Errorf("after the renewal: generation %d, new key %v, second session serial %s",
			current.Generation, !current.Key.Equal(old.Key), sessions[1].serial)
	}
	if current.HostID() != "H1" || !current.CA.Equal(old.CA) || current.Controller != old.Controller {
		t.Errorf("the renewed generation changed the host, root or controller: %+v", current)
	}
	waitFor(t, "the old generation to go", func() bool { return slices.Equal(generations(t, dir), []string{"gen-2"}) })
	var revoked bool
	if err := ctrl.db.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT revoked_at IS NOT NULL FROM core_agent_certs WHERE serial = ?`,
		transport.SerialText(old.Cert.SerialNumber)).Scan(&revoked); err != nil || !revoked {
		t.Errorf("the enrolled serial is not revoked after the handshake with the renewed one: %v", err)
	}
	// The renewed certificate is fresh: no further renewal or reconnection.
	for range 20 {
		h.clock.Advance(transport.BackoffMax)
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	if n := len(ctrl.agentSessions()); n != 2 {
		t.Errorf("%d sessions after the renewal, want 2", n)
	}
}

func TestRunRetriesAFailedRenewal(t *testing.T) {
	ctrl, h, _ := renewalHost(t)
	ctrl.mu.Lock()
	ctrl.renewErr = errcode.New(errcode.Forbidden, "no renewal today")
	ctrl.mu.Unlock()
	renewals := func() int {
		ctrl.mu.Lock()
		defer ctrl.mu.Unlock()
		return ctrl.renewals
	}
	h.start(h.config())
	waitFor(t, "a renewal attempt", func() bool { return renewals() == 1 })
	waitFor(t, "the failure to be logged", func() bool {
		return strings.Contains(h.logs.String(), "daemon: renew the agent certificate")
	})
	// The session stays up and the next attempt waits RenewRetry.
	h.clock.Advance(RenewRetry - time.Minute)
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
	if renewals() != 1 || len(ctrl.agentSessions()) != 1 || connected(h.hello()) != "true" {
		t.Errorf("before RenewRetry: %d renewals, %d sessions, connected %s", renewals(), len(ctrl.agentSessions()), connected(h.hello()))
	}
	h.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	waitFor(t, "a second attempt", func() bool { return renewals() == 2 })
	if gens := generations(t, h.layout.Paths.AgentTLSDir); !slices.Equal(gens, []string{"gen-1"}) {
		t.Errorf("generations after failed renewals = %v, want gen-1 alone", gens)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `internal/agent/daemon/renew.go`:

```go
package daemon

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/transport"
)

// RenewRetry is how long the agent waits after a failed renewal before it
// asks again on the same session.
const RenewRetry = time.Hour

// RenewTimeout bounds one renewal request.
const RenewTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// renewAt is when a certificate is renewed: after two thirds of its life
// (spec section 2).
func renewAt(cert *x509.Certificate) time.Time {
	return cert.NotBefore.Add(cert.NotAfter.Sub(cert.NotBefore) * 2 / 3)
}

// renewLoop renews c's certificate over sess once it is due, retrying every
// RenewRetry, and closes renewed after the new generation is saved. It ends
// with ctx, which ends with the session.
func (d *daemon) renewLoop(ctx context.Context, sess *transport.Session, c *Credentials, renewed chan<- struct{}) {
	at := renewAt(c.Cert)
	for {
		// A fake clock fires a timer only when it advances, so a renewal
		// already due runs at once rather than through a timer.
		if delay := at.Sub(d.clock.Now()); delay > 0 {
			if err := sleep(ctx, d.clock, delay); err != nil {
				return
			}
		}
		rctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, RenewTimeout)
		next, err := renew(rctx, d.cfg.Paths.AgentTLSDir, sess, c)
		cancel()
		if err == nil {
			d.log.Info("daemon: renewed the agent certificate", "generation", next.Generation, "notAfter", next.Cert.NotAfter)
			close(renewed)
			return
		}
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return
		}
		d.log.Warn("daemon: renew the agent certificate", "error", err)
		at = d.clock.Now().Add(RenewRetry)
	}
}

// renew sends a certificate request for a new key over an agent-opened
// stream of sess (POST transport.RenewPath), requires the answer to name the
// same host and chain to the same root, and saves it as a new generation.
func renew(ctx context.Context, dir string, sess *transport.Session, c *Credentials) (*Credentials, error) {
	_, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("daemon: generate the agent key: %w", err)
	}
	csr, err := csrPEM(key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out transport.RenewResponse
	if err := post(ctx, sess.Open, transport.RenewPath, transport.RenewRequest{CSR: csr}, &out); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	cert, err := checkIssued(out.Certificate, c.CA, c.HostID())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	next := &Credentials{Key: key, Cert: cert, CA: c.CA, Controller: c.Controller}
	if _, err := SaveGeneration(dir, next); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return next, nil
}
```

```go
		// one is still on disk, and lets the controller revoke the old
		// serial at the first handshake with the new one.
		d.log.Info("daemon: reconnecting with the renewed certificate")
	}
```
